# Test stories and evals

Generated by `python scripts/stories_csv.py` from every test file's header and every
golden eval set. Do not edit by hand: change the test or the eval, then run the script.

**398 test files** (part 1) and **15 eval sets, 54 cases** (part 2).

## Part 1 · Tests as user stories

### As a speaker using the app, I want the server's front door (sign-in, job progress, uploads, error answers) to behave the same every time, so that the app never breaks on it or shows me someone else's job.

`tests/integration/test_api_seams.py` · Story

- **O1** The paths the website and Railway call exist, and the health check answers "ok".
- **O2** Without a valid sign-in, a protected page answers a clear "not signed in" the app can read.
- **O3** A guest or the owner can follow their own processing job; someone else's job looks exactly like no job at all.
- **O4** Errors come back in a form the app can read, never as a web page.
- **O5** The browser's pre-upload check accepts every header the app sends, so big deck uploads go through.
- **O6** Without a sign-in, the provider diagnostics answer "not signed in" and never ask OpenAI.

*Why:* Incident 2026-07-15: big deck uploads failed at the browser's pre-upload (CORS) check. 2026-10-25: the provider diagnostics answered anyone, with the key's prefix and the deployed commit.

### As a business that buys a model's answers, and as the teachers who earn from them, I want the API's tables to keep only a hash of my key, never let a test key bill, never let a sale's split stop adding up, and keep what the teachers earned when a buyer leaves (N528, N531, N566, N577, N583).

`tests/test_a_business_can_buy_a_model_s_answers_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The five new tables have RLS on and grant nothing to a browser role; ai_models has its price, which is never zero or below.
- **O2** The tables refuse what the rules never allow: a key stored as anything but a SHA-256 hash, the same key twice, free answers on a live key, a billed test answer, a split that does not add up, a second version 1, a payout month that is not a month's first day.
- **O3** Erasing a buyer deletes their keys and keeps their paid answers with no buyer; erasing a model's founder keeps the version with no founder; erasing a teacher takes their share and payouts.

*Why:* Migration a_business_can_buy_a_model_s_answers (0521), applied twice in both rehearsal lanes (the reapply is its idempotency check).

### As a coach, I want to be asked to listen again only for clips I judged blind, once at a time, with a fresh line each time, so that my second answer is still my own and not prompted by the machine.

`tests/test_a_coach_is_asked_to_listen_again_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The flag opens one ask per coach with a blind judgment of record and none for a self-report, a peer, an abstention, an anonymous row or a coach whose rating was not blind; no second ask while one is open.
- **O2** The coach's new answer closes their ask; the next flag opens a new one with the next signed line (A then B) and the closed ask stays as history.
- **O3** Never-2: the ask carries no reason, answer or machine read, by the table's shape (only take, moment, coach, line key and two times).

*Why:* 0444 (QG12a A, P26c): the coach's second answer must stay blind.

### As a coach, I want to change my answer to a speaker's request and have every earlier answer kept, so that I can correct myself without losing the record.

`tests/test_a_coach_may_change_their_answer_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Giving the same answer again changes nothing.
- **O2** A different answer replaces what the speaker sees; the old one, with its words, video and share, is kept as version 1, 2, 3.
- **O3** A new answer that is not shared withdraws the earlier share.
- **O4** Changing from words to an exercise keeps the answer well-formed.

*Why:* Coach panel lock flows 7 and 12; Q-B12 A (migration 0446).

### As the founder, I want a coach's chosen exercise frozen once, with the reason it was chosen, and its showing receipted, so that what the speaker practises is traceable and never silently swapped.

`tests/test_a_coach_pick_counts_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A coach pick is frozen once (a replay naming another exercise returns the first row) under the coach policy, with no seed and no draw.
- **O2** Its match trace is stored once, with outcome coach_chosen.
- **O3** Showing the card is receipted once against that frozen pick; a card for another exercise is refused by name.

*Why:* Migration 0398 (founder 2026-09-29, decision 3). Runs only on a disposable database whose name starts with willab_confident_moment_.

### As the founder, I want a confidence event whose Take has no clip a rater could judge to be given up for good, so that the outbox stops claiming and failing it every 30 seconds (two events, 30,903 attempts since 2026-10-08) and keeps the record of why.

`tests/test_a_confidence_event_without_a_clip_is_given_up_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** An event the worker gives up is never claimed again, keeps its error code, and cannot be locked by anything afterwards.
- **O2** Only the worker holding the lease can give an event up, and only for a reason on the allow-list.
- **O3** The migration gives up the events already stuck on NoEligibleConfidenceClip and leaves every other event as it was.

*Why:* 0487, founder 2026-10-20; runs in one rolled-back transaction on a database whose name starts with ``willab_confident_moment_`` (rehearsal tier only).

### As the founder, I want a coach-imported recording processed only under my written corpus basis (N58) and never under a speaker's own permit, so that every provider call on the critical path (record -> process) has a lawful basis and a speaker's Take is never processed without their own acceptance.

`tests/test_a_corpus_import_is_processed_under_the_founders_basis_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The basis is one N58 row with the founder's note, never pooled; it can only be retired, never edited.
- **O2** A registered import gets a permit naming N58 and its session; its events land in the corpus ledger, nothing in the Phase-1 one.
- **O3** A user's Take, a guest's Take and an owned Take with a forged "training_import" flag are refused by the corpus writers.
- **O4** The Phase-1 permit writer refuses a corpus import, even for the importing coach who has accepted the terms.
- **O5** With no basis in force, no import is registered and no permit issued.
- **O6** The service key may only call the entry points; the browser roles (anon, authenticated) can neither call them nor write the tables.

*Why:* Migration a_corpus_import_is_processed_under_the_founders_basis.sql (0435; founder 2026-10-07, decisions log N58). Runs the real SQL on the rehearsal tier, lane "confident-moment released".

### As the founder, I want each corpus speaker to stay in the train or test split they were first given, so that evaluation results are honest.

`tests/test_a_corpus_speaker_keeps_its_split_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The app's writer (services.corpus_split.assign_speaker_split) stores a speaker's split once, and a stored split wins over a later computation.
- **O2** The stored row cannot be changed or removed: an UPDATE and a DELETE are refused with CORPUS_SPLIT_IMMUTABLE, so no path can re-shuffle.

*Why:* Migration 0433 (N56.4). Runs only on a disposable database whose name starts with willab_confident_moment_.

### As the founder, I want each speaker's train/test split to survive even a whole-table wipe (TRUNCATE), so that the separation is never reset by accident.

`tests/test_a_corpus_split_survives_a_truncate_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Every form of TRUNCATE is refused with CORPUS_SPLIT_IMMUTABLE.
- **O2** Every stored split stays exactly as it was.

*Why:* N56.4: a split is "never re-shuffled" (migration 0434).

### As a speaker who asks to delete my account, I want my processing to stop at once, seven days to change my mind, and everything erased after that, so that deletion is real and only briefly reversible.

`tests/test_a_deletion_completes_after_seven_days_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The request blocks me at once, cancels running jobs and permits, takes my coached pairs out of training, deletes nothing yet, and ends seven days later; asking twice is one request.
- **O2** I can cancel inside the seven days and the block lifts; asking again later starts a new request.
- **O3** After the seven days the run erases my data, marks the request done with proof, and never does it twice.
- **O4** A dry run only reports what is due and writes nothing.
- **O5** Once it is done, my sign-in is gone: no email, no phone, no password, no linked sign-in, no live session, and I cannot sign in; what the policy says survives (the proof of my agreement, my training choice) is still there, and nobody else's sign-in is touched. If my sign-in cannot be erased, the deletion is not marked done and the next run finishes it. An account deleted before this was fixed loses its sign-in on the next run.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-05, N48.4 Q14 A, Q17 A, Q19 A (migration 0422); Privacy 3.8 sections 7 and 9, N217 item 4 and N95 (migration 0497).

### As a speaker who withdrew my training "yes", I want a half-finished export that held my pair voided and cleaned up, so that my data never leaves in a broken release.

`tests/test_a_failed_export_voids_its_release_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** When an export's last step is refused because a pair is no longer allowed, the release is voided as "export_failed" and queued for file cleanup.
- **O2** Pairs of speakers who still say yes wait and leave in a later week's release.
- **O3** The weekly consent refresh keeps the "export_failed" reason.

*Why:* Door 2, ML-9 (migration 0455).

### As a speaker, I want encouragement lines to rotate, and "Your voice moved more here than on Take 1." said only when it is measurably true and never twice in a row, so that feedback feels earned and fresh.

`tests/test_a_line_is_never_said_twice_in_a_row_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** On Take 1 the ordinary lines rotate, one after another.
- **O2** On Take 2 the "more than on Take 1" line appears only when that cue was weaker on Take 1.
- **O3** That line is never said twice in a row; the rotation carries on after it.

*Why:* D-FW-3; signed line bank, "Rotation" (migration 0438).

### As someone starting a model, I want my setup kept with the model from step to step, and a moment of a Take gathered into my model only once, so ✕ loses nothing and a rerun of the gathering adds nothing twice (N566 item 2, MOD-02, MOD-25).

`tests/test_a_model_keeps_its_setup_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** ai_models has a setup that is always an object, empty for a model made before it, and the column is locked to the backend as the table is.
- **O2** The same moment of a Take goes into one model once; a second model may take it too, and an upload's rows are never held to it.
- **O3** When the owner's account is erased, the model keeps its name and its card for its teachers, and loses what the owner wrote: the description, their name, the setup's words; a draft is archived.

*Why:* Migration a_model_keeps_its_setup (0520), applied twice in both rehearsal lanes after 0516 (the reapply is its idempotency check).

### As a person whose example taught a model, I want every version that learned from it found and marked to retrain when it is deleted or withdrawn, however it is deleted (PIPE-06, N615 item 3).

`tests/test_a_model_learns_from_its_settled_examples_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The kept vectors are locked to the backend (RLS on, nothing for a browser role) and go with their example.
- **O2** Deleting an example marks every version whose example_ids holds it, and no other version.

*Why:* Migration a_model_learns_from_its_settled_examples (0522), applied twice in both rehearsal lanes (the reapply is its idempotency check).

### As a speaker, I want the model trained only on my coached pairs that I said yes to and that were released, and only once, so that my "no" is respected.

`tests/test_a_model_learns_only_from_the_yes_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A released pair that is still allowed is marked trained, once.
- **O2** A pair that is not released, no longer allowed, or already trained is refused.
- **O3** An unknown training run is refused.

*Why:* Doors 3 and 4 (migration 0406).

### As a host whose people's answers teach a model, I want it to answer from what they settled, not from a general model reading its name; to say "sure" only when it has been measured right; to be checked by me on examples it never saw before it answers in my chat; and to be shared or sold only after an exam the app computes (N615, N616 items 8, 9, 10 and 23, N620).

`tests/test_a_model_that_learns.py` · Story

- LEARN-01  Training reads each settled example once and fits one head on the settled examples only: never an exam case, another model's example or a withdrawn speaker's. LEARN-02  The answer comes from the head: one of its three answers, one reader call and no chat call; "sure" from the head's measured confidence. Without a head, the recipe card answers as before. LEARN-03  The owner's check is ten examples the version was not fitted on, none of the first 10, the guess first; Keep or Change on each. LEARN-04  After the owner's check the model answers in its owner's chat; it is not shared for tokens or sold before a passed exam. LEARN-05  A version's exam is computed by the app, with the sign test against the version before; nobody enters "passed" by hand. LEARN-06  No model that reads a photo starts, learns or is offered. PIPE-06   A withdrawn person's examples mark every version that learned from them to retrain, and the next fit leaves them out.

*Why:* services/model_head.py (the rules) and services/model_head_store.py (migration a_model_learns_from_its_settled_examples). The reader is mocked: no provider call in CI.

### As a speaker, I want a clip I shared for listening to vanish from the shared list as soon as I withdraw it, so that I control who hears me.

`tests/test_a_moment_may_be_lent_an_ear_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A share with revoked_at set leaves shared_clips_live at once.

*Why:* Migration 0410 (F3). The Voice Album share switch is RETIRED (services/lend_your_ear.py docstring): the app no longer writes voice_album_shares, so the revocation is written here by hand, and the view's one reader (delayed_measure.clips_for_listener) has no caller. The live sharing path is pinned in test_a_take_may_be_shared_with_a_community_postgres.py. Candidate for deletion together with its lane entry in scripts/rehearsal_tier.sh.

### As a coach, I want to know whether a speaker's request came from the open moment or from a judgement, recorded once, so that I answer in the right context.

`tests/test_a_moment_opens_before_it_is_judged_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** request_exercise_from_coach_v3 records raised_on; a repeat on the same moment keeps the first request.
- **O2** A raised_on outside judgement/open is refused by name (EXERCISE_COACH_REQUEST_INPUT_INVALID).
- **O3** Opening a moment is logged once: the app's writer (DatabaseService.record_moment_event) reports True the first time and False on a repeat, and one row is stored.

*Why:* Migration 0408 (F1). Runs only on a disposable database whose name starts with willab_confident_moment_.

### As a speaker who never edited my Ideal Text, I want my document to get paragraph identity when it is published, so that feedback and slides attach to my paragraphs and the screen is not empty.

`tests/test_a_never_edited_document_has_paragraphs.py` · Story

- **O1** Publishing a never-edited document writes one part per paragraph, each with its own id, and the words are unchanged.
- **O2** A second publish of the same document writes nothing; the ids stay.
- **O3** A document that already has its own parts is never rewritten.
- **O4** A document whose paragraphs cannot be proven gets no parts; the words are still published unchanged.

*Why:* Production 2026-09-19: `parts=0 pieces=3` -- nothing ever minted identity, so V3 feedback and the slide join were dark.

### As a speaker, I want my training "yes" to count only for the wording I actually agreed to, so that a new consent sentence needs my new yes.

`tests/test_a_new_training_wording_retires_the_old_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** One call retires the old wording and starts the new one at the same instant; exactly one wording is in force, with the signed sentence.
- **O2** A yes given under the old wording now reads as off: no new yes to the old wording, and no training copy made on it.
- **O3** The old yes stays on record as it was and can still be withdrawn.

*Why:* Founder D3, N68 (migration 0458).

### As a speaker, I want my coached pairs released for training only while my yes is active, and pulled back if I withdraw, so that withdrawing really stops their use.

`tests/test_a_pair_remembers_the_yes_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A pair is allowed into training only while my training yes is active; withdrawing turns it off.
- **O2** A pair leaves once: a released pair cannot be released again.
- **O3** Withdrawing voids the release that carried my pair and returns the pair; the release keeps its record.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-30, L1, L2, L5 (migration 0405).

### As a person who starts or teaches an AI model, I want the models' tables to keep my model, my teaching and my answers safely, so that a public model outlives its owner's account, my own rows leave with mine, and no browser reaches any of it (N528, N563, N566).

`tests/test_a_person_can_teach_and_start_ai_models_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The five tables exist with RLS on, and no browser role is granted anything on them; the ring row ai_models sits at ring 6.
- **O2** The tables refuse what the rules never allow: an added example with no adder, an example from Takes with no speaker, a known clip in the sealed exam, an unknown stage, the same answer twice.
- **O3** Erasing the owner's account keeps their public model, with no owner; erasing a teacher's account takes their teaching and answers.

*Why:* Migration a_person_can_teach_and_start_ai_models (0516), applied twice in both rehearsal lanes (the reapply is its idempotency check).

### As a speaker practising, I want each try read once, quickly, against my own practice and Take, so that feedback on the try arrives fast, belongs to me, and its number never reaches me.

`tests/test_a_practise_try_is_read_fast_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** One stored read per try; the database takes the owner, practice and Take from the try itself and computes W and S*W; a repeat is a no-op.
- **O2** A read that fails (services.v4_practice_read.record) still keeps its row, with no values, and counts as "not reached yet".
- **O3** The phone's times are stored once, only for the try's owner, and only for a wait within 0..600 000 ms; the timing route answers 204 with no body, so the speaker is told nothing about the read (Never rule 1: no numbers shown to speakers).
- **O4** The read goes when the try goes (deletion: FK cascade).

*Why:* Migration 0450 (V4 B1.4, D-ML-9; founder O5, V7 A). The app's writers run over a thin RPC-to-SQL client on the rehearsal tier.

### As a speaker, I want my Take processed without a long wait when there is nothing to praise structurally, so that I get to my feedback sooner.

`tests/test_a_praise_search_stops_looking.py` · Story

- **O1** With no device anywhere, the praise pass asks the model about at most three waves of three pieces (nine calls), in document order, then stops; nothing is stored.
- **O2** A Take whose first pieces carry devices stores exactly those pieces, without asking about the rest.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-14 (the wait). Driven through the public generate_praise_for_session; only the provider call (chat_complete), the Take's readout and the cross-Take baseline are faked.

### As a speaker sharing a Take with a community, I want the exact sharing words I agreed to recorded with my share, and kept after I revoke, so that there is proof of what I consented to (consent on the critical path).

`tests/test_a_share_records_the_words_the_speaker_saw_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A share stamps the consent (Privacy/Terms) version and the words version on one row per Take and community.
- **O2** A revocation (fewer communities, or "None") stamps revoked_at and keeps both versions.
- **O3** Sharing again under newer words re-stamps the same row and makes it live again.
- **O4** A words version the server does not list (Config.SHARE_WORDS_VERSIONS) is refused and no row is written.

*Why:* Migration 0443 (CM2 B, N53.2; Q-B6 A, N62). The real services.communities.share_take and services.db writers run over a thin PostgREST-to-SQL client on the rehearsal tier. The queue dropping a revoked Take is pinned in test_a_take_may_be_shared_with_a_community_postgres.py.

### As a speaker, I want the recording screen to use the server's minimum-speech threshold, so that a short but valid Take is not rejected.

`tests/test_a_short_take_meets_the_served_floor.py` · Story

- **O1** /config/recording serves no minimum duration and 3 s of voiced speech: the same thresholds the upload gate enforces.
- **O2** The upload gate passes a short, fully voiced Take and refuses the same length of silence.

*Why:* Founder 2026-07-15: the duration minimum was retired; the FE used to hardcode 60 s. (Formerly tests/test_wave2_be.py.)

### As a speaker who deletes my account after skipping helper words, I want the erasure to finish, with my skip kept only as an empty receipt, so that deletion never gets stuck and nothing I wrote survives.

`tests/test_a_skip_keeps_an_empty_receipt_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The account erasure reaches "done": my skip stays with an empty payload and my paragraph text reads "[erased]".
- **O2** Someone else's skip is untouched.
- **O3** During an erasure, any change to the skip other than emptying it, and any delete, is refused.

*Why:* Before 0448 the erasure failed on the skip (founder N12, "empty receipt").

### As a speaker, I want to change my answer to a piece of feedback with my first answer kept exactly as it was, so that both my choice and its history are kept.

`tests/test_a_speaker_may_change_a_judgement_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A change is stored as a new revision; the same change again changes nothing; changing back is revision 2.
- **O2** The first answer's row is unchanged, and the revision list holds both changes.

*Why:* D-FW-9, founder QA1 A (migration 0440).

### As the founder, I want each Take's random evaluation moments drawn by the database exactly as the code draws them, so that the evaluation sample is reproducible and unbiased.

`tests/test_a_take_draws_its_random_moments_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The database picks the same moments, in the same order and number, as the code does for the Take's seed.
- **O2** Each stored moment records its rank, seed, frame, slide and draw version.

*Why:* V4 B1.2, D-ML-7; founder V2 A, V3 A (migration 0447).

### As the founder, I want an imported corpus clip transcribed only under my corpus basis (N58), and a speaker's or guest's Take never under it, so that no Take is processed without its own lawful basis (processing authorization on the record -> process step).

`tests/test_a_take_is_transcribed_only_under_its_own_basis.py` · Story

- **O1** An ordinary or guest Take goes the Phase-1 way (its owner's receipt); refused there, the transcriber is never called and the corpus basis is never asked.
- **O2** An import goes the corpus way: one corpus permit for the session, the transcriber is called, its events are recorded, and no Phase-1 permit is ever asked for.
- **O3** When the database refuses the corpus permit, or names any basis but N58, the import is refused with that code and the transcriber is never called.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-07, CO2, decisions log N58. The database client is a stand-in that only answers each RPC; the SQL rules themselves (who is an import, which basis is in force) run for real in tests/test_a_corpus_import_is_processed_under_the_founders_basis_postgres.py. Was tests/test_corpus_queue_end_to_end.py.

### As a speaker, I want a Take I shared to leave the community the moment I revoke it or the community closes, so that I stay in control of who hears me.

`tests/test_a_take_may_be_shared_with_a_community_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A shared Take shows in the community's live list.
- **O2** Revoking the share removes it.
- **O3** Closing the community removes it.

*Why:* N52.4 (migration 0432).

### As a speaker, I want each Take 2 rewrite of my Ideal Text stored as a Paragraph revision tied to that Take, with my locked helper words still locked afterwards, so that every old version stays in my history.

`tests/test_a_take_rewrite_is_a_paragraph_revision_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Never-5: a take_rewrite revision is kept beside the older ones and names its Take; the Ideal Text read points at it as the current one.
- **O2** The Ideal Text read takes a Paragraph's lock from the Paragraph row, so a locked Paragraph stays locked after a Take rewrite and an unlocked one is not locked by an old 'lock' revision.

*Why:* 0416 (contract 14, 16): the core read used the newest revision as the lock, so Take 2 unlocked a locked Paragraph on the cold open. The Take-2 writer itself is proven in test_take_two_rewrites_an_edited_slide_postgres.py.

### As a speaker who asks to delete my account, I want the erasure to actually start and run to the end, so that a request never sits at "requested" with nothing erased.

`tests/test_account_deletion_starts_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A list of my data that is not really mine (forged or stale) is refused.
- **O2** With the retention rules in place, the erasure runs to "done" and my practice rows are gone.
- **O3** Without the deletion-evidence retention rule, the run stops for review and names only that missing rule.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-25, decision 6: every deletion sat at "requested" (migration 0362).

### As a speaker, I want to change my email and my password on Data & consent, with a link for a forgotten password, so that I keep my account when I lose an address or a password; and as the founder, I want every request, change and wrong password kept in a security log, so that a taken-over account can be traced.

`tests/test_account_security.py` · Story

- **O1** Change email: a wrong current password changes nothing and is logged; the right one sends a link to the new address and a notice to the old one, and the email stays until the link is opened. The log keeps the token's SHA-256, never the token, and never a password.
- **O2** An address another account has is refused before any link goes, and the refusal is logged.
- **O3** The link changes the email once and logs it; opened twice it changes nothing more. An older link after a newer request, an expired link and an unknown token change nothing.
- **O4** Change password: a wrong current password or one under 6 characters changes nothing; the right one sets the new password, signs out the other devices, sends the notice and is logged with the IP address and the device.
- **O5** An account that signs in with Google has no password to ask for: its email change needs none, Set a password sends the set email, and the saved password is logged as its first; a reset link's password is a change and gets the notice. One that signs in with Microsoft is called "Microsoft" (N401 item 6), never Supabase's "Azure".
- **O6** When the email doesn't go, the card is told so: nothing is reported as sent.

*Why:* WIL-95 (founder 2026-10-26, N190: "approved, implement it"; rules in the frontend's docs/flows/consent-screens.md §1).

### As the founder, I want "Delete" on the Users list and on a profile to delete any account, an old one that never agreed to processing included, and to delete again after an Undo, so that the people I delete are really deleted.

`tests/test_admin_delete_user.py` · Story

- **O1** An account with no owner principal is given one and gets the same pending seven-day deletion as everyone else; its sign-in record is never deleted outright. The person then leaves the Users list.
- **O2** Deleting again after a cancel records a new pending deletion, not the cancelled one.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-28 (N222): the list's Delete failed with a 500 for an account from May; Supabase refuses to delete a sign-in record whose Takes are kept as history. "Make Delete really delete".

### As a speaker, I want any AI call an admin makes about me to run under my own processing permit, and be refused when I have none, so that my words never reach a provider without my authority (processing authorization on the critical path).

`tests/test_admin_speaker_permit.py` · Story

- **O1** Directive suggestions: with my authority the model call runs inside the protected scope bound to MY principal; a refused permit answers 403 before any provider client exists.

### As a speaker, after a Take I want one thing to do at a time: the Take's card and its summary, then each ask on its own, then a goodbye with nothing pushing me to record again, and the settings asks only the next time I open the app, so that nothing competes with the walk and I practise because I want to (founder 2026-10-20, N89; 2026-10-31, N294, N295; N319 item 7).

- **O2** From Take 2 the same: Will's question, then "Did this help?"; the first that waits comes and the others wait for a later Take; an open ask from the CEO panel is not among them (it is in the bar at the top, N391); Will's next question may follow his first, only when his is the ask (N294 item 4); nothing comes when nothing applies.
- **O3** The asks come one at a time, each under whatever closed the one before: a typed turn, under Will's reply; "Yes", tapped or typed, under "We're glad it helped you."; a reply that opens a form keeps the ask open. An ask that no longer applies is skipped, and so is a settings ask a message held from before still names. After the last comes "Good job. See you next time.", no push to the next Take.
- **O4** A message still waiting when a newer Take is written shows where it stands.
- **O5** After a distressed message the chat's safety reply comes and the asks wait: nothing lands under it until I write again (founder 2026-10-21, N94).
- **O6** The plan's lines after a Take wait for the ask: the ask stays the newest bubble, so its answers stay; after my answer come the lines, then "Good job. See you next time." (no practice day is named, N564), then the plan's bar (N319 item 7, N564 item 3); a question the plan asks comes last, under them, right above its answers (N537).
- **O7** The WhatsApp ask waits for the next visit and comes in the bar at the top, never in the chat (N294 item 8, N295, N391): never before my second Take is done, one per Take, at most twice, opened with "It seems you enjoy using WillpowerLab."; never once my number is saved. The home-screen ask is never made (N391).
- **O8** The first Take's goodbye says it is the first Take's, so "Record Take 2" comes at once under it on any device (N449 item 2); a later Take's does not.
- **O9** The visit on every app open makes the same WhatsApp ask from what it reads of the thread: when each message was written and the few keys it looks at, never the messages' words.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-20: "one action at a time"; 2026-10-31 (N294): "No call to action means that there is no push", "the next time you come and open the app ask the settings questions".

A clearer version's example keeps the default.

My voice starts being made as my spoken Take is stored, beside its processing (N84), so the clip can be ready by the walk.

- **O7** A praised moment's practice gets its clip too, started early like a corrected moment's (N122).

- **O2** "next" with no joke before it is left to Will.
- **O3** Telling the story of my startup after Will asks about my talk is an answer, not deflected; "tell me a story" on its own is still a request.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-15: "next" after a dad joke was read as a new message.

### As a person in WillpowerLab, I want my own link that I paste into any email, so that a colleague who opens it joins me in a chat, and nobody else can reach me (N308, N328, N335).

`tests/test_chat_links.py` · Story

- **O1** My link is made the first time I ask and stays the same after; replacing it makes a new one, and the old one opens nothing.
- **O2** Before signing in, the join page learns only the first name of whose link it is, in its three lines (N601). A wrong link and a replaced one answer 404, the same answer.
- **O3** Joining makes one chat between the two of us, each named for the other; opening a link again, from either side, finds the same chat.
- **O4** My own link is refused (400 OWN_LINK), and nothing is made.
- **O5** As a user with no ring at all, I get my link and someone joins me through it (N456; was ring 6 only).
- **O6** In the chat, the one who joined sees whose link they joined through; the link's owner does not. In the list, the joiner's row says it too until the chat's first message (N488).
- **O7** A chat left without its two people (a crash between the two writes) gets them back on the next join, so the pair is never locked out.
- **O8** A message I send reaches the other person's iPhone as a push: my name over the chat list's line for it, opening our chat (N385).

*Why:* The join link, the chats' second slice (founder 2026-11-02: "go, build the join link slice"); the intimacy rule (N328); joining is onboarding (N335 item 1).

### As a speaker, I want every chat turn saved with its buttons, so that my chat bubbles never disappear when I reload.

`tests/test_chat_persist.py` · Story

- **O1** POST /v2/chat/query with persist writes my row and the bot's row, with the bot's choices in its metadata; posting the same turn again writes the same two ids, so the table keeps it once.
- **O2** A reload (GET /v2/user/lounge/messages) brings the bot's choices back.
- **O3** If saving fails, the turn still answers.
- **O4** The bot's reply is saved under my turn even when my browser's clock runs ahead of the server's, so a reload keeps the order I saw.

*Why:* Founder #2: bubbles must never disappear; founder 2026-10-20: the chat keeps the order it shows. The harness is tests/chat_route_fakes.py; what the chat answers is pinned in test_chat_answers.py.

### As a person in a chat with someone else, I want to block them and to report what they sent, so that I stop hearing from someone abusive and the founder can act on it; and as the founder, I want every report in front of me at once.

`tests/test_chat_safety.py` · Story

- **O1** When I block the other person, what they send from then on never reaches me: not in my chat, not as my list's last line, not as a push, and not after I unblock either. They are not told: their own chat reads as before, their message sent. My chat and my list say I blocked them; after Unblock, what they send reaches me again (N466 item 2).
- **O2** When I report them, the chat's last five messages as I see them are kept with who reported whom and whether I also blocked; the founder gets an email and sees it in the CEO panel's list, and can mark it read. Nobody else can read that list, and someone not in the chat can neither block nor report it, and writes nothing (N466 item 3).

*Why:* Founder 2026-11-04 (N466): Block and Report in the chats between people, for Apple's rule 1.2.

### As a speaker, I want Will to read my whole talk slide by slide, so that his answers refer to what I actually said on each slide.

`tests/test_chat_talk.py` · Story

- **O1** Each slide lists its bullet points and what I said on it.
- **O2** A slide I said nothing on reads "(nothing yet)".
- **O3** Words not tied to a slide are listed on their own.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-15; Privacy 3.7 section 4.

### As a person in WillpowerLab, I want my own chat on my website, so that anyone there can write to me, I answer as in any chat, and they sign up after asking to get my answer (N591).

`tests/test_chat_widget.py` · Story

- **O1** Before the visitor writes, with no card in the way, the website chat learns only whose chat it is, my welcome and how a visitor may write, as my chat's setup says: no fixed questions; a wrong or replaced link, or the switch still off (the default), opens nothing, the same answer as the join page's.
- **O2** The visitor's first message makes their own chat with me: in my list it is "Widget / Visitor 1" with "From {site}" under it, like any other chat, and it does not count toward the day's joins through my link; my welcome and my own questions come first, as for anyone who joins; a first message the chat does not take makes nothing.
- **O3** The visitor taps my questions, then writes as my setup lets them: typed words when I allow typing, refused when I don't; no recording without an account.
- **O4** I answer as in any chat, recorded or typed; the visitor gets my answer only after signing up, and nobody without the visitor's secret reads or plays anything.
- **O5** Signing up after asking (phone, email, Google): the signed-in person takes the visitor's seat, my list names the chat after them, the chat is theirs in the app, their questions join their bundles, and the widget, in its own tab too, learns where the first notice goes.
- **O6** The first answer after the sign-up goes once to the email they signed up with, in the signed words.

*Why:* The founder, 9 November 2026 (N591): "There's really no difference between the widget and the normal chat. It's just that the chat is placed on the public side."

### As the founder, I want my own chats with a colleague and a customer, apart from my chat with Will, where I answer with one of three emoji or a voice note, and start something new with a photo or a file, so that I can see the chats between people working before anyone else is in them.

`tests/test_chats.py` · Story

- **O1** As a user, I have my chats with no ring at all: the list answers without the rings ever being read (N456; was ring 6 only). As a server, I answer nobody who is not signed in, and write nothing.
- **O2** The demo makes Sarah's customer chat and Ariel's colleague chat once, however often it is asked; the list names each chat for the other person, with their line and the last thing said.
- **O3** In my chat I read what was said, oldest first, mine marked; I send 👍, 👀 or 🙏 with its meaning kept as a label (N311), and anything else is refused.
- **O4** Someone not in a chat can neither read it, nor send to it (an emoji, a voice note, a photo or a file), nor play its voice notes, nor open its photos and files, not even through a chat of their own.
- **O5** I send a voice note (N321 item 2) and the other person in the chat sees it, with its length, and plays my recording as I sent it.
- **O6** A recording that is missing, empty, not audio or over two minutes is refused (400), one over 12 MB too (413), and nothing is written.
- **O7** A voice note is a recording: it passes the processing authorization and the recording consent a Take asks for, after the chat's own 404; when they refuse, their refusal is the answer and nothing is written.
- **O8** My voice note is turned into words (N324) under my own permit, before the transcriber hears it, and the words are kept with its recording, up to 20000 characters; the chat's members get them with the voice note, to open under its player (N331), and the list never shows them.
- **O9** A voice note that cannot be turned into words (none heard, the transcriber failing, the permit refused) is sent all the same: the same answer, its message and recording kept, its words empty.
- **O10** I send a photo and a file from the "+" (N351); the other person in the chat sees them, the list's last line has the file's own name, and the photo opens in place and the file downloads under its name, as I sent them, never sniffed as anything else.
- **O11** My photo or file opens a new question for the other person (N341 item 2): it is marked as one, and so is the voice note I send straight after it to say what I need (N337); a voice note that follows nothing of mine is not.
- **O12** A photo that is not a raster image (text, SVG), an empty or missing file, or anything over 10 MB is refused (400, 413), and nothing is written.
- **O13** An answer I recorded with "Record an answer" is sent into the chat as a voice message that plays my Take's own recording (N363): I see it with its Take; the other person gets just the answer, no Take (N336). An answer has no five-minute cap: a long one is sent (N486 item 4). A Take that is not mine, or a withdrawn recording consent, sends nothing.
- **O14** Once my answer's Take has its words, both of us get them with the answer, slide by slide, and when each slide starts, for the strip that follows its voice (N355, N331); before that, no words and no slides.
- **O15** Will reads a question that waits for an answer once its words are known (N361 item 1): asking about a product or a treatment, it carries his mark (record_answer), the one "Record an answer" waits for; his no is kept as no; anything else leaves no mark and the note is sent all the same. The demo customer's question carries it, an older seeded one too.
- **O16** My answer waits on the server for what is left of its 5 seconds (N441 item 3): until then the other person does not see it, the list does not show it and no push goes; Undo takes it back then, and Will says so in my chat with him, once, naming the other person (N441 item 5). Once its time is over Undo is refused and it reaches them; with no time left it goes at once. Nobody else can take my answer back.
- **O17** A question I recorded with "Record your question" (N483 item 4) is sent and held as an answer is, and the other person gets it marked for them to answer ("Record your answer"), without its Take; Undo takes it back the same way.
- **O18** "Not now" under a Take or a question someone asked me (N483 item 3, N493 item 3) tells nobody: no message is written. The system keeps it once, with whose it was, what and the flow (N504 item 2), and the founder reads how often and the newest; "Not now" on my own message, or on one that asks for nothing, is refused.
- **O19** The list reads every chat's newest messages with the chats in one read, never one read per chat, and answers what the reads one per chat answer.

*Why:* The chats between people, first slice (founder 2026-10-31, N298 to N316; the three emoji N311; the mic N321 item 2; its words N324, shown under the player N331; the photos and files of the "+" N351; an answer recorded with "Record an answer" N336, N361, N363; a question recorded with "Record your question" N483 item 4, N498). Every speaker's recording and every provider call goes through the processing-authorization path (CLAUDE.md).

### As a speaker, I want the model's clearer rewrites to reach my feedback with their evidence, so that I get a rewrite where my wording was weak.

### As a speaker, I want to share a Take only with communities I belong to, revocably, with listeners hearing audio only, so that sharing is my choice and listeners judge blind.

`tests/test_communities.py` · Story

- **O1** Clips the listener answered or labelled, clips the quorum settled, and clips that can no longer settle (unresolved, quarantined for unclear audio) are not queued again.

- **O7** An imported corpus clip plays through a short-lived signed link, audio only, never a name, transcript or level.
- **O8** My answer on an imported clip is kept once (a second answer is refused) and never becomes a label.
- **O9** The judging reopened from the Lounge lists imported clips too.
- **O10** An empty queue is logged with its reason, and so is a short one.

- **O12** It is asked again when the sharing words change, when I join a community after my choice, or when nothing of my choice is left.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-06 (N52.4, migration 0432; CM2). The consent and words versions on a share are pinned on the database in test_a_share_records_the_words_the_speaker_saw_postgres.py. O6 to O10: founder 2026-10-23 (N119, migration a_speaker_may_judge_an_imported_clip.sql): the imported corpus the coaches judge is played to speakers, three levels, three speakers; this amends CM1 A and replaces N97's one moment per try. ### As the founder, I want each speaker's clips kept in one split, so that the confidence model is never tested on voices it trained on.

`tests/test_confidence_labels.py` · Story

- **O2** A projection whose owner decision names a different family is refused.

- **O1** The receipt ties together the bundle, the item and what was shown; asking again returns the same receipt.
- **O2** A receipt naming another bundle is refused and nothing is written.

### The database transport is a pool of HTTP/1 connections, kept warm wide enough for the app's opening requests, and opened at the start of a Take's job, so the app opens fast and processing does not wait for connections.

`tests/test_db_transport_pool.py` · Guard

- A supabase/httpx upgrade, a config edit or a session rebuilt in services/db.py that brings back the one shared HTTP/2 socket, shrinks the pool, or pings slower than the pool drops idle connections.

*Why:* If removed: Boot requests queue behind the slowest one again (founder 2026-10-16: fast calls took 4.00 s on the socket, 0.07 s on the pool) and nobody notices until speakers do.

`tests/test_feedback_data_contract.py` · Story

- **O1** A V3 bundle records its candidates as service evidence only: training_eligible is false on every row; a V3 selection without its Confident Voice item records nothing at all.
- **O2** Never-2 (coach judgements stay blind): the blind packet a coach's judgement is sealed against holds only evidence (span, audio, window, codec); the owner's, machine's and peers' answers are not in it.
- **O3** Paragraph and helper-word decisions repeat identically; a reversed span is refused.
- **O4** A candidate's id belongs to its set: the same inventory gives the same ids (replay is idempotent), a changed inventory gives ids of its own.

*Why:* 2026-09-21: 23505 on feedback_candidates_pkey when a second candidate set for a Take reused the first set's ids.

### As a speaker, I want my published readout to show evidence-backed items and the coach's note only, so that I see what was said with its proof.

`tests/test_feedback_repository.py` · Story

- **O1** A great_formulation item carries its exact text span, without audio.
- **O2** A published readout whose coach drafts are not surfaced shows no items, and its coach review is the written note only: no coach video, no insights payload.

*Why:* The coach's Take video was retired (founder 2026-09-25).

### As a speaker, I want the machine's feedback straight away and the coach's revision only once the coach publishes, so that I never see an unreleased verdict.

`tests/test_feedback_supersession.py` · Story

- **O1** A coach verdict on an unpublished Take is hidden; on a published Take it is released, each judged against its own Take.
- **O2** A released verdict becomes a coach_revision change against the words the speaker accepted, with the exact span, text and note.

### As a speaker, I want my page to show only the moments that matter, so that it stays focused.

`tests/test_feedback_window.py` · Story

- **O1** Confident moments, the exercise and items I already answered are shown; other open moments are not; a paragraph I saved in this Take shows nothing new, except a moment picked for me there, which stays until I walk it; and no score fields reach the page.
- **O2** A Take with nothing picked still shows two moments; one whose picked moments I answered shows no others in their place.
- **O3** A paragraph I saved in an earlier Take gets its moments again in later Takes.

*Why:* Founder lock 2026-10-12; project "S" 2026-10-13; Never-rule 1; 2026-10-28 (N246 item 5, "Second stays").

### As a speaker, I want a Take whose feedback can't be proven against the frozen bundle to show no V3 feedback rather than unrecorded feedback, so that what I see is always what was recorded.

- **O2** A visible row whose candidate is not in the bundle declines by name, and hands back no learning bundle.
- **O3** A provable Take serves its rows, and only then hands the recorded bundle back through the `learning` out-parameter.

`tests/test_first_visit.py` · Story

- **O2** The founder reads the whole path in order, the sign-up screen and the accounts made included, in all and per day; nobody else can read it.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-29, N271.

### As the founder, I want to clear what waits on a blind list of the coach queue, so that it stays empty until next week's items come.

`tests/test_founder_clears_a_blind_line.py` · Story

- **O1** A cleared item is never shown again.
- **O2** A line cleared this week does not refill before next week, on each of the four lines.

- The confidence pool offers a moment labelled blind by a coach or another speaker inside practice, and skips a speaker's own answer, an answer from any other lane, and an answer given after seeing the clip's other side.

*Why:* walk I1 (labels come only from blind answers); LEARNING-AND-QUORUM §3 B2: the pool used to read every row.

### As a signed-out speaker, I want to reopen my own readout with my guest pass but never someone else's, and to have a video upload refused clearly, so that my recording stays private and the record step stays audio-only.

`tests/test_guest_readout_video.py` · Story

- **O1** My guest pass (or my account, once claimed) opens my readout, with its state.
- **O2** A bare Take id, or someone else's Take, answers 404 SESSION_NOT_FOUND without saying whose it is; a malformed id answers 400.
- **O3** A video upload answers 415 AUDIO_ONLY; the mic's own audio/webm passes the gate.
- **O4** The readout that opens the walk reads my Take's pieces, its edits and its taps side by side, and reads my Take's row once.

- **O2** Words out of order, or words I never said, are refused.

### As a speaker, I want my helper words to survive the next Take's rewrite until I pick new ones, so that my cues are not lost.

`tests/test_helper_words_own_text.py` · Story

- **O1** Never-5 (helper words stay until the speaker picks new ones): after Take 2 rewrites the Paragraph in different words, the next read still returns the stored helper words on that Paragraph, with Take 2's text.
- **O2** Their position is kept while it proves the words, re-found when they occur once in the new text, and otherwise left empty, never guessed.

### As the founder, I want one "Homework" email a day listing who sent a Take, not one email per Take, so that my inbox stays readable while the coach queue still shows every Take at once.

`tests/test_homework_digest.py` · Story

- **O1** The Takes noted during the day go out as one email from 8:00 in my time zone, a row per student with their Takes and moments; nothing before 8:00 and nothing twice in a day.
- **O2** A day with no Takes sends nothing.
- **O3** A failed send keeps its Takes for the next day's email.

### As a speaker, I want my feedback marks built on the Take I was just told is ready, so that I never see the previous Take's marks.

`tests/test_ideal_text_bake_fresh_head.py` · Story

- **O1** If the stored Ideal Text still points at the previous Take, it is republished first and the new Take is the one baked.
- **O2** If it is already current, it is baked as it stands, with no republish.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-15: the bake re-ran the old Take and threw every mark away.

### As a speaker, I want my feedback marks to appear quickly and the same every time, without the server working them out twice, so that the page never goes blank or slow.

`tests/test_ideal_text_bake_in_flight.py` · Story

- **O1** If the marks are already being prepared (by the background job or by another request), I am told at once to ask again, nothing is worked out a second time, and my next ask gets the finished marks.
- **O2** With nothing in progress, the marks are worked out right away, as before, and stored.
- **O3** Repeated asks that find no marks are answered from memory, not recomputed, until I answer an item or it is a new version; an empty answer before the Take is ready is not remembered.

### As a speaker, I want my Ideal Text document never rebuilt by a later Take, so that my accepted words stay put.

*Why:* CLAUDE.md rule 5 and N48.3 Q13 A: Take 1 creates the canonical Ideal Text; later Takes only produce feedback.

### As a speaker, I want my Ideal Text's feedback block to be the V3 feedback, each answered item keeping its decision, every Confident Voice item playable, served fast, and empty rather than wrong when something fails, so that I can trust what is marked (feedback on the critical path).

`tests/test_ideal_text_changes.py` · Story

- **O1** A failing stage serves its named fallback (no rows, a named stage in `degraded`), never wrong rows.
- **O2** When V3 fails on the way, no V2 rows are served and the failure is reported.
- **O3** A served V3 Confident Voice row carries its recording (snippet_audio_ref), so the speaker can hear the moment.
- **O4** An answered row stays, with its decision as its status.
- **O5** Every served V3 row carries its learning handles and nothing else leaves the server with it.
- **O6** The block equals the frozen block, each row read once, and the live read is served before the dark frame is written.
- **O7** On a plan, the walk I see knows this week's level, and which helper words I had when I recorded (N246).
- **O8** My walk reads this Take's frame even when the Ideal Text was computed live and its frame is not written yet: "Last time" plays the fix that rose, and the tempo step knows the One thing walk, with the frame written once.

*Why:* services.ideal_text_changes (audit Q-C1). O3: founder 2026-09-20 "there is no playback on the overlay". O4 and O5 drive stages of _ChangesRun directly; the rest go through build_changes_block. O8: founder 2026-11-06 "Go, fix both", through the walk's own readers.

### As a speaker, I want opening my Ideal Text never to show it as missing when the read failed, and never to serve an out-of-date snapshot, so that the loop never breaks quietly.

`tests/test_ideal_text_cold_open.py` · Story

- **O1** A failed cold-open read answers 503 IDEAL_TEXT_READ_FAILED, retryable, never the "pending" 404 that would show an existing Ideal Text as missing.
- **O2** A snapshot that turns stale while the enrichment readers run answers 409 SNAPSHOT_STALE with the current snapshot id, and the server-side acknowledgement token never leaves.
- **O3** An open answers with whether the version on screen was saved and with the deck flags, read side by side once the document is read: each is one read of its own, and one after the other they added a round trip to every open.

NOTE Stale-at-first-check refusals live in tests/test_ideal_text_route_contracts.py::test_enrichment_refusals; this file pins the stale-DURING-readers case only.

### As a speaker, I want my first Ideal Text built from my first Take, retried after a dropped connection, and never undone by a failed notification, so that Take 1 always produces my text.

`tests/test_ideal_text_confirmation.py` · Story

- **O1** A retry builds version 1 from the Take that created the document, not from a later Take.
- **O2** One dropped database connection is retried instead of failing Take 1.
- **O3** If the "ready" notification fails, the document still counts as confirmed.
- **O4** A later Take may queue the retry too, and the retry job carries no audio, upload or transcription fields.

### As a speaker, I want to save helper words on a paragraph I have not locked, without it locking, so that the first pass works without an error.

`tests/test_ideal_text_part_root_unlocked.py` · Story

- **O1** PUT root on an unlocked paragraph answers 200 saved with my words, and writes exactly those words and their span.
- **O2** Never-5 (the speaker's words are theirs to commit): saving helper words never locks the paragraph -- no lock is written.

### As a speaker, I want my paragraphs to keep their identity, my locked words never rewritten by the machine and my saves never quietly refused, so that the words I committed stay mine.

`tests/test_ideal_text_parts.py` · Story

- **O1** An invalid part list is refused whole; an empty list is an empty document, and no stored rows is ABSENT (not empty).
- **O2** The parts join exactly as the FE's joinSegments joins them, so a save whose parts match its text is never refused for drift.
- **O3** Never-5 (my latest words are the Ideal Text): every compose path serves each locked part exactly once, with its id and words intact.
- **O4** On a locked part the machine only re-offers (tagged), Confident Voice arrives as a pending prompt, a straddling change is dropped, and Save never silently declines an offer hidden under a lock.
- **O5** A bad PUT writes nothing; an explicit unlock reopens the paragraph.
- **O6** Locking a never-edited document seeds its parts and locks.
- **O7** An edit alone marks a paragraph reviewed.

*Why:* Founder field report #5: "my locked chunk came back unlocked".

### As a developer on call, I want an Ideal Text whose publication keeps failing to stop being retried every minute, so that the logs and the job queue stay usable.

`tests/test_ideal_text_publication_rests_after_repeated_failures.py` · Story

- **O1** After three failed publications the sweep leaves that version alone for the failure window instead of queueing it again.
- **O2** Resting versions never use up the sweep: a newer interrupted publication behind them is still queued.

*Why:* Production 2026-10-03: two documents failed LINEAGE_REQUIRED every minute. Production 2026-10-26: 103 resting versions filled the 100-row sweep.

### As a speaker, I want the Ideal Text routes (read, extras, earlier-Take decision, paragraph lock, helper words and my own edits) to answer exactly as they did before the code was moved into services, so that the loop never breaks.

`tests/test_ideal_text_route_contracts.py` · Story

- **O1** Reading my Ideal Text returns the same full answer and records, in order, what I was shown; someone else's project is "not found".
- **O2** The extras (notes, history, entitlement) are served for the current version only, with no caching; a missing, unknown, outdated or wrong request gets its exact refusal.
- **O3** Accepting an earlier Take's text, locking a paragraph and picking or skipping helper words each write their records in the same order; every refusal returns its exact code and writes nothing.
- **O4** My own edit is saved through the one safe writer and published; a bad, outdated or failed edit returns its exact code (a database refusal is a 409) and publishes nothing.
- **O5** The One thing walk's own helper words are saved whole; a pick by hand past eight words is refused (N323 item 2).

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-12: move logic out of the routes; Never-rule 3; 2026-11-02 (N323 item 2).

### As a speaker, I want each paragraph of my Ideal Text tied to its slide and my deck link kept, and my locked words shown even when composing fails, so that I can read my talk slide by slide.

`tests/test_ideal_text_slide_linkage.py` · Story

- **O1** Pieces that line up 1:1 with the served paragraphs carry their slide.
- **O2** Pieces that do not line up all degrade to slide_index null; the paragraph text still serves.
- **O3** presentation_ref is the first deck across Takes; a later deckless Take never clobbers it.
- **O4** The GET never runs the composer (only the stored compose cache is read).

`tests/test_ideal_text_slides_survive_a_missing_head.py` · Story

- **O2** Opening a project with no head builds one and serves it, its pieces still on their slides.
- **O3** After three failed builds the open answers "pending" at once, until a new Take finishes.

### As a speaker who worries before a talk, I want Will to sort the worry once into what is mine and what is not, and end on one thing I can do, so that I spend the evening on what I control.

`tests/test_in_control.py` · Story

- **O1** A note that worries about something out of my hands gets Will's two or three lines under its "Noted.", ending on one thing I control, from Will's signed guide (N241 O1, N258).
- **O2** The same worry is not sorted twice: Will may ask once whether the one thing happened, then says nothing more about it.

### As a speaker, I want at most the budgeted number of marks on my document, one per span, from known lanes only, and nothing at all if arbitration fails, so that the page stays focused and honest.

`tests/test_intervention_candidates.py` · Story

- **O1** The budget holds: more than BUDGET_CEILING changes in, at most BUDGET_CEILING served; overlapping changes serve one mark; a zero-width insert never takes a slot.
- **O2** Only declared lanes are served; a change with no span is dropped.
- **O3** If arbitration fails, nothing is served.
- **O4** Never-1 (no numbers to speakers): a served change carries no internal number (priority, ppv, deviation) and no funnel.
- **O5** Accents on a locked part ride the style lane: outside the budget, capped per Take, not suppressed by single-point focus.
- **O6** Confident Voice needs playable audio, keeps its reserved slot, is not capped by rewrites and is not bound by the accent window; an accent past the window is never offered.

### As a speaker, I want the processing poll to show only progress, the same for an unknown job and someone else's job, and never a score, so that waiting is private and judgement-free.

`tests/test_jobs_status_route.py` · Story

- **O1** The poll body carries status/stage/percent and a state from the readout GETs' vocabulary: processing, ready or failed.
- **O2** An unknown, malformed or someone else's job gets the identical 404.
- **O3** Never-1 (no scores, numbers or verdicts to speakers): no score or verdict key or value anywhere in the body, even when the job row itself carries one.
- **O4** The recovery sweep runs only with the internal secret.

### As a person who shares what I know, I want everyone who joins through my link to answer a few practical questions by tapping, so that we start our knowledge base knowing the deadline and the language, and I read their answers on one card, not my own questions waiting (N487, words signed in N491, N496 and N502).

`tests/test_join_questions.py` · Story

- **O1** My questions are kept and read back; a question without two to six answers is refused; an empty set means none.
- **O2** Someone who joins me finds my first lines in our chat, as my messages, and is told a few questions come first; in my own view of the chat I see neither the questions nor their taps, only that they joined through my link.
- **O3** They answer by tapping: "No" to "Are you in a hurry?" puts the deadline one step of my plan away and moves on; a question that takes more than one answer takes several; then my closing line; I see one card with the deadline and the languages.
- **O4** A tap that is not one of the answers, two answers to a one-answer question, a second answer, or my tapping my own question is refused.
- **O5** Joining someone with no questions posts nothing, and the line at the top says nothing about questions.
- **O6** A question I type is kept only when it is practical and has answers to tap: Will gives a yes-or-no question its answers, and leaves out a question about the work itself and one he finds no answers for.
- **O7** The first time I open "Add someone to chat with", Will asks me in his chat, one question at a time: how far ahead I plan, the languages (I speak two), anything else; my own question gets answers to tap; the summary shows what someone new will see; "That's right" keeps it, and the next time my link opens at once (N503).
- **O8** Setting no questions is an answer too; later "Your questions for people who join ›" opens the summary again, "Change it" makes the change from my words or says it could not, and "That's right" saves.
- **O9** "Change it" understands my words however I put them (the model, under my processing authorization), and never adds a question about the work.

*Why:* The founder, 4 November 2026, on the prototype "Asked When They Join": "in the user set up, we need to ask you the questions on what questions should be asked to the people that will ask you for the knowledge"; "they can only click through the answers they can not type".

### As the founder, I want the messages after a speaker's first three Takes to say exactly the words I signed off, with their two actions, so that no unapproved words reach a speaker.

`tests/test_journey_messages.py` · Story

- **O1** The Take 1 message's text and its two actions (prepare Take 2, keep practising) equal the locked copy.
- **O2** The Take 2 message's text and its two actions (prepare Take 3, keep practising) equal the locked copy.
- **O3** The Take 3 message's text and its two actions (export, keep practising) equal the locked copy.

*Why:* Never-rule 4: no new words on screen without the founder's OK. All three simplified by the founder in chat, 2026-10-20.

### As a speaker, I want to judge myself after the feedback and not before, with my answer shown to the coach only after their blind rating, so that the machine leads and the coach stays blind.

`tests/test_judgement_after_feedback.py` · Story

- **O1** Opening a moment raises one coach request, under the machine's kind, once; it never asks me for a judgement first.
- **O2** My later answer sets the request's answer_kind from the matrix.

`tests/test_lab_analysis_dispatch.py` · Story

- **O1** If the queue fails, the Take is processed right away instead.
- **O2** In background mode the upload answers "pending" at once, and the Take is marked ready when the work finishes.
- **O3** A Take 1 whose Ideal Text could not be confirmed is reported as failed, never as complete.
