`tests/test_lab_recording_intake.py` · Story

- **O1** The flat form fields sent with a recording become the recording's context, with numbers, lists and slide data in their proper types.

### As a developer on call, I want every upload answer and every early worker exit to leave one log line with its code, so that a Take that "never started" can be traced.

`tests/test_lab_recording_outcome_log.py` · Story

- **O1** A refused upload logs one WARNING line with its status and code=.
- **O2** An accepted upload logs one line with code= and the session and job ids.
- **O3** The worker's early exits (unknown job, lost claim) each log a line.

*Why:* 2026-09-28 "processing doesn't even start": the web log was empty.

### As the founder, I want ground-truth labels settled only by independent blind humans, so that our confidence data is never circular.

`tests/test_label_quorum.py` · Story

- **O1** A machine prediction never counts as a vote: a machine-lane row, or a machine value beside a human answer, adds nothing.
- **O2** Never-2 (coach judgements stay blind): a rating made after the rater saw the clip's non-blind side is not a vote.
- **O3** A self-report (the speaker's own answer) is never one of the votes.
- **O4** One rating never settles; only two matching blind humans do, and a blind coach and a blind peer share one quorum.
- **O5** Not sure and Audio unclear never settle a label.
- **O6** Once settled, no further vote is accepted.
- **O7** Two historical v1 "neutral" answers keep J6's meaning: the clip is closed (perceptually ambiguous), never routed again, never a label. One neutral is still an IDK, and a perceptual quorum still wins.

*Why:* Founder 2026-08-11: a model grading its own homework, or one person agreeing with themselves, poisons the gold set beyond repair.

### As the founder, I want each record of what a speaker was shown to keep the machine's, the speaker's and the coach's answers apart and to show no numbers, so that the three judgements stay independent.

`tests/test_learning_exposures.py` · Story

- **O1** What the speaker sees carries no score, rank or prediction, and no copy of any other person's answer; the machine's own score stays only in the machine's column.
- **O2** A shown item that carries a score or someone's answer is refused before anything is saved.

*Why:* Never-rules 1 and 2 (AC-9, L3).

### As a speaker, I want my data used for cross-person learning only while I hold the training yes, so that withdrawing consent really stops it. (Consent, beside the loop: coach sheets and exercise order learn from speakers; the record -> feedback loop never waits on them.)

`tests/test_learning_lanes_honour_3_5.py` · Story

- **O1** The shared read (services.pair_consent) says yes only for a speaker with an active yes; never-said-yes and withdrawn both read as no.
- **O2** Fails closed: an unreadable ledger, an unreadable Take or an unreadable blind-check objection reads as no.
- **O3** E4 V4 coach sheets: only yes-speakers' Takes are written; after a withdrawal the sheet leaves the queue and cannot be answered.
- **O4** E4 blind block pick: the same, for the coach's block pick.
- **O5** E5 learned exercise order: only yes-speakers' tries are learned from; a withdrawal drops them at the next read.

*Why:* Privacy 3.5 section 4a, items E4 and E5 (founder signed 2026-10-08). The ledger here is a Python double: this file covers the Python lanes, not the SQL status function.

### Every file, line and commit cited in the counsel pack (legal/phase1-2026.1/ and every docs/legal/*.md) still resolves.

`tests/test_legal_citations.py` · Guard

- A cited path that no longer exists; a cited line past the end of its file or on a blank/bare comment rule; a frontend line citation with no commit next to it; a cited commit that is not an ancestor of HEAD (a squash-merge destroys branch hashes).

*Why:* If removed: Counsel follows a citation in a signed, immutable document and lands on code that does not say what the document says (2026-09-18: the DPIA cited services/voice_confidence.py:177, a comment rule). It cannot check that a line still MEANS what the document claims.

### As the founder, I want Will to ask for my life goals once after my first note and walk me through one goal at a time, drafted only from my words, so that my notes, my talk and my week answer to the same goals, and I can change any step without losing what it said before.

`tests/test_life_goals.py` · Story

- **O1** After the first # note's "Noted." the two signed bubbles come, the second carrying the Not now | Start ask; not again while it waits; after a Not now once more after the next note, never a third time; nothing once a goal is set, and nothing for anyone else.
- **O2** Will's draft keeps only phrases found word for word in my answers; with every question skipped there is no goal.
- **O3** A set goal leaves the draft, posts its ladder, "Noted. Your career goal is set." and the line that sends me out to live it (N183) to the Lounge; a goal it replaces is kept as replaced.
- **O4** A changed step keeps the earlier words as a dated version, shown newest first in its history with the day the goal was set; the talk goal changes the setup answer itself; the same words change nothing.
- **O5** "#goals" opens my ladders; "can I add another goal? I want to get healthier" is answered "Of course, ..." and opens the lens with my words; a talk-goal change and anyone else go on as before.
- **O6** Two goals whose words pull against each other are asked "When they clash, which wins?" once per pair, before the new goal is set; the answer is a step on both ladders, and a changed answer keeps the earlier one with its date (N185).
- **O7** On the quarter's last day the first opening of the Lounge finds "The quarter ends today." and "Did it move?" with Not now | Start, once; opened later, "The quarter ended on …"; the review keeps how it went in words, quotes only notes that match, and sets the next quarter's step with the earlier one kept (N186).
- **O8** A note that says the quarter should be different is asked about once, under its Noted., quoting it: Change writes the step with the earlier words kept, Not now changes nothing; words not in the note ask nothing (N191).
- **O9** While a plan runs, the career ladder's THIS WEEK is this week's level from the plan, read-only; no plan, or the plan not on: none (the plan's G5).

*Why:* Will learning plan, step 3 (founder 2026-10-26, N172: "build the life goals now"; rules in the frontend flow page §1 M).

### As the founder, I want the lines in code to be exactly the lines I signed, with no number but the Take number, so that no unapproved words or scores reach a speaker.

`tests/test_line_bank.py` · Story

- **O1** Every bank in services/line_bank.py, the AI clip's openers, tips per problem and closers included (N120), equals the signed doc, bullet for bullet and in order; no line is invented, dropped or reworded (Never-4).
- **O2** A line a speaker can be shown carries no number except the Take number a later line fills in (Never-1: no scores or numbers to speakers).

*Why:* docs/SIGNED-line-bank-2026-10-06.md is the source of truth (N54); the AI clip's bubble signed 2026-10-23 (N120).

### As a speaker, I want my Ideal Text to be my full latest Take with only fillers, repeats and punctuation smoothed, and feedback anchored on exact words, so that the text is what I said.

`tests/test_living_transcript.py` · Story

- **O1** The document is the full transcript of the LATEST spoken Take, in speech order, each piece's span slicing exactly its words; the flag no longer swaps in a shorter best-of text (Never-5).
- **O2** Only hesitations, immediate repeats and punctuation/casing change silently; false starts, expressive repeats and real words stay (Never-5: no word silently changed). A full stop goes in between two pieces only where the speaker paused clearly and the next begins a sentence with a capital (N126).
- **O3** Every change is anchored on an exact substring of the served text or dropped; a bold that cannot narrow is dropped, not widened.
- **O4** A piece is never lost when its words change after a bake or a lock: it keeps its order and falls back to its paragraph.
- **O5** What reaches the speaker carries no internal vocabulary, no score and no cue key the screen has no signed words for (Never-1, Never-4).

*Why:* Founder decisions 2026-07-20 #1-#3 (the Living Transcript re-shape).

### One OpenAI client constructor (services/llm_client.py) and no new direct `.chat.completions.create(` call sites. services/llm.py (`chat_complete`: authorization, timeouts, usage ledger) is the sanctioned entry. Both rules read the AST, so prose cannot trip or dodge them.

`tests/test_llm_client_fence.py` · Guard

- A second provider client, or a new direct chat call that skips the processing-authorization path (CLAUDE.md: every provider call goes through it). And a client shared across a fork: the rq worker forks a process per job, and two processes on one pooled TLS connection corrupt each other.

*Why:* If removed: A provider call can be added anywhere without authorization or usage accounting, and nothing goes red; a job could write on its parent's provider connection.

### As someone signing up or in with WhatsApp, I want my code to arrive on WhatsApp, and only when Supabase asked for it, so that my number is proven and nobody else can send codes in our name.

`tests/test_login_code.py` · Story

- **O1** A request signed with the hook's secret sends the code to the number with the login template, the code in its body and its Copy code button.
- **O2** A request without Supabase's signature, signed with another secret or too old sends nothing (401); with no secret set the door is shut (503).
- **O3** When WhatsApp sending is off the hook answers an error, so Supabase tells the person the code could not be sent.
- **O4** A number being added in Settings gets its code at that number, which is not yet the account's.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-27, N214.

### As a speaker, I want the clips, recordings and slide decks in my Lounge to get fresh links from my own recordings only, a coach's video to play only while it is their word's current one, and each page to load quickly, so that my media plays and I never see another user's.

`tests/test_lounge_clips.py` · Story

- **O1** My own clip gets a fresh link; a clip pointing at another user's recording is left untouched.
- **O2** One page gets fresh clip, recording and deck links and the "new coach feedback" flags from one batched read per kind, not one per Take.
- **O3** An older page carries no flags, and a failing step (such as decks) costs only that step; my clips still refresh.
- **O4** A coach's video plays only while it is their word's current one; a video they replaced or removed never plays, an older "Later · from your coach" bubble is left out, and each bubble says whether it is new.
- **O5** My practice tries from several practices on one page all get fresh links, and the practices are read side by side, not one after another.

### As the founder, I want the feedback arbitration's experiment honest (protected Confident Voice slot, per-slide exploration, withheld notes recorded as having won), so that we can later learn what feedback helps.

`tests/test_manager_engine.py` · Story

- **O1** A protected note never gives up its slot to exploration or withholding.
- **O2** Exploration trades only inside the swapped note's own slide.
- **O3** Every withheld note is logged as would_have_surfaced, not surfaced.
- **O4** Take 1 is never withheld from (science arms, 2026-09-18 ruling).
- **O5** A note shown again and again unacted is never escalated.

*Why:* Appendix H (H.4, H.7, H.13.4); PM-8 the experiment's record.

### As a speaker, I want the coloured highlight marks in my Ideal Text never to break across a line, so that I never see a stray `{{orange:` in the middle of my text.

`tests/test_marker_hygiene.py` · Story

- **O1** Sanitised Ideal Text has no marker that opens on one line and closes on another; an accent across a paragraph break is re-wrapped per line and every word stays.
- **O2** A dangling `{{orange:` or a stray `}}` loses only the token, never the words, and sanitising twice changes nothing.
- **O3** The ledger bake of an approved highlight whose phrase spans a newline writes one closed mark per line; baking again or with stale offsets leaves the text as it was.

*Why:* BE-1, founder screenshot 2026-07-27: a bare `{{orange:` on its own line.

### As a speaker, I want the Lounge chat bot never to tell me about scores, ratios, KPIs or retired collections, whatever the AI model wrote, so that what I read is in words and points me to the right place.

`tests/test_master_doc_output_guards.py` · Story

- **O1** An answer that mentions a score, ratio, KPI or classifier is replaced by a safe answer in words.
- **O2** An answer that recreates the retired "Strong Sides" collection (in any language) is replaced by a pointer to Voice Album.
- **O3** Ordinary answers pass through untouched.
- **O4** Will's knowledge holds no price and tells him to name none: prices are on the pricing page only.

*Why:* Never-rule 1 (RULE A / AC-9) and RULE K; no AI call is made in the test. O4: founder 2026-10-27, N210 ("once they see the price on the pricing page, they don't see any prices later on").

### As the founder, I want WillpowerLab to be by invitation: a person's link is the only way in, and without one a person joins the waitlist and I answer by email (N471).

`tests/test_membership.py` · Story

- **O1** While the switch is off, everyone is a member, as before.
- **O2** An account from before the switch is a member; a new account is on the waitlist, and its first ask sends one email to the founder's inbox, which a reply answers; asking again sends no second email.
- **O3** A new account that joins through a member's link is let in at once, and so is one with a Take from before the switch.
- **O4** A waiting account's link opens nothing: the same answer as a wrong link.
- **O5** After the day's number of people join through one person's link, the next one goes to the waitlist and no chat is made.
- **O6** A waiting account cannot start a project or record; a guest cannot start one either, and no new guest identity is made.
- **O7** A member the server already knows opens the app without another lookup of their account (the app waits on this answer).

*Why:* The founder's decision of 4 November 2026 (N471): members only, links as the one way in, the waitlist answered by hand.

### As a developer, I want every new table to switch on RLS and every new function to revoke EXECUTE from public, anon and authenticated, so that the browser's anon key can never read tables or call money-moving functions again.

`tests/test_migration_security_rules.py` · Story

- **O1** CI goes red on a new migration that creates a public table without ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY in the same file.
- **O2** CI goes red on a new function that does not revoke EXECUTE from PUBLIC.
- **O3** CI goes red on a new function that is not itself revoked from anon AND authenticated (a REVOKE on that function naming both roles, or a DO block that revokes that function looping over both). Supabase grants both roles directly, so a PUBLIC revoke alone leaves them in.
- **O4** A SECURITY DEFINER migration pins its search_path.
- **O5** CI goes red on a new migration that creates a public table without granting service_role on it in the same file.

*Why:* 2026-07-25: 57 tables shipped readable with the anon key (docs/RLS-AUDIT.md); RLS says nothing about functions, so the function rule arrived with the first .rpc() on 2026-08-03. The two frozen lists below describe FILES that predate a rule (applied migrations cannot be edited); they may only shrink. From 2026-10-30 Supabase no longer grants a new public table to the Data API roles, so a table the backend reaches through PostgREST answers 42501 until a migration grants service_role on it.

### As the founder, I want the list of database migrations to be complete, append-only, in dependency order, safe to run and free of surprise drops, so that merging a migration (which runs it in production) cannot corrupt or desynchronise the database.

`tests/test_migrations.py` · Story

- **O1** The list matches the files on disk one to one, with no gaps, duplicates or reordering, and the first 239 (already recorded in production) can never change; "verify" passes on the real tree and fails on a file missing from the list.
- **O2** Only the known, deliberate migrations drop or delete anything.
- **O3** Every migration runs in order after what it depends on, has balanced brackets and quotes, and can run inside one transaction.
- **O4** A migration and its ledger row are saved together; a failed one leaves no ledger row, and the ledger and baseline scripts are safe to re-run.
- **O5** A connection on the wrong (transaction) pooler port is warned about, without ever printing the database address or password.

*Why:* CLAUDE.md: merging a migration runs it in production (stdlib only: CI installs nothing).

### As a coach, I want the blind packet I rate to carry no transcript, score, rank, model guess or judgement, so that my rating is my own.

`tests/test_mlc2_confidence.py` · Story

- **O1** A packet carrying any such hint is refused.

*Why:* Never-rule 2: no coach sees the machine's guess before answering.

### As the founder, I want a Take promoted with a training yes to flow to one finalized confidence frame with exact spans, one selected candidate, a replay that changes nothing and a blind packet with no hints, so that the training corpus is consented, exact and blind.

`tests/test_mlc2_confidence_end_to_end_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** One Take, one producer receipt and one outbox event per promotion.
- **O2** The worker's frame is accepted: one candidate per snippet, each span the snippet's exact offsets on the Take's own audio object, one prediction per eligible clip, exactly one selected candidate.
- **O3** A replay of the same event changes nothing and the health check reports no processed event without its frame.
- **O4** The coach's blind packet carries the clip and the five answers and no transcript, prediction, score, rank or selection hint (Never-2: no coach sees the machine's guess before answering).
- **O5** An owner without a training yes is refused and the promotion rolls back.

*Why:* G-6 part 2; runs in one rolled-back transaction on a database whose name starts with ``willab_confident_moment_`` (rehearsal tier only).

### As the founder, I want the confidence frame built from a Take's snippets to be exact, deterministic, free of transcript text and off unless the mode is open, so that blind labels are reproducible and nothing runs before I switch it on.

`tests/test_mlc2_confidence_frame_factory.py` · Story

- **O1** The frame passes the contract's validator: every clip is on the Take's own audio object with its exact span, and exactly one clip is selected.
- **O2** A replay (rows in any order) is byte-identical.
- **O3** Nothing in the frame carries transcript text.
- **O4** While the mode is dark, the worker boot line starts nothing.
- **O5** Under founder_canary the sweep owner claims, finalizes and re-arms.
- **O6** A Take with no clip a rater could judge is promoted plainly and opens no outbox event, because its frame could never be finalized.
- **O7** A failed event is not offered again in 30 seconds: one whose Take has no clip is given up (0487), any other failure waits longer after each attempt (founder's pg_stat_statements, 2026-10-20: 976 failures, 540 claims).

*Why:* G-6, audit 2026-09-22.

### The new learning code (MLC-2) never names a legacy learning table: no mlc2_*.py module, and none of the door modules that build, release, evaluate, train on or promote learning data.

`tests/test_mlc2_legacy_isolation.py` · Guard

- A read or write of a retired, unconsented label store (for example confidence_labels, star_verdicts) from canonical training code. Method names count (get_confidence_labels_by_snippet_ids), and so does importing services.professional_confidence. Reads source: the mlc2_* scan is plain text; the door scan reads code only (identifiers, imports and non-docstring strings), because the doors explain in prose what they must not read.

*Why:* If removed: Retired, unconsented labels can leak into training unnoticed (DA-PROHIBIT; the training-corpus copy read confidence_labels until 0430).

### As the founder, I want the general-user service tables locked to browser roles and the candidate-set write repaired on the body production runs, so that rollout cannot leak data or crash.

`tests/test_mlc3_general_user_service_d4_postgres.py` · Story

### As a model's founder and its teachers, I want every answer sold to be split the same way for every model, running costs first, then WillpowerLab's 20% of the margin, then the founder's 10% of the rest, then the teachers by their points, with nothing lost to rounding, so the price screen and the payouts always agree (N531, N577, N583).

`tests/test_model_api.py` · Story

- **O1** At any price the parts add up; at the lowest price, what an answer costs to run, "Infrastructure" takes it all.
- **O2** A teacher's points are their counted answers, weighed by reliability, a hard clip worth 1.5; a month's teachers' part is divided by them to the unit, and with no founder left the founder's part goes to them.
- **O3** The model is "sure" only when it is confident and answers with a choice its recipe offers.

*Why:* services/model_api.py holds the money's rules once, for the price screen, every API call and every payout.

### As a business, I want to read a model's card, try it, take a free test key that I see only once, and call its API, paying in tokens only for the answers I use and never more than my monthly limit; as a model's founder, I want to set its price above what an answer costs, with the split shown live; and as a teacher, I want my share of each version and my earnings paid in tokens (N528, N531, N566, N577, N583).

`tests/test_model_api_routes.py` · Story

- **O1** Without the ring row (ai_models) the card, the terms and keys are 404.
- **O2** A ready model's card says "Good at", "Not so good at", "Not for", "When it isn’t sure" and "Version", with "Use it in your business" and "Try it"; a model not for sale says "trying it · not for sale yet", and when it is not sure it says "I’m not sure about this one, so I won’t guess." with "Not sure · a coach will answer".
- **O3** "Create a key" shows the key once ("This is a test key: free for 100 answers. You see it only once."); only its hash is stored; the code comes in "JavaScript", "Python" and "curl"; the key answers 100 free answers and then stops.
- **O4** A live key pays the model's price in tokens, split as N577 sets it; it gets no answer when the tokens are not charged, and it stops before an answer that would pass its monthly limit.
- **O5** A business's unsure questions reach the teachers, names removed, only once the business allows it.
- **O6** "Set your price": − and + preview a price and its split without saving; "Use {price}" saves it, never below what an answer costs to run; nobody else can.
- **O7** A version fixes its teachers' shares; "Your share of version 1" is their part of it, and a finished month's earnings are credited in tokens, once, however small: there is no minimum.
- **O8** "Download the data" (the one download route) holds the teachers' own ratings and answers, each teacher and example a number, never what a speaker said or the sealed exam, and a private example's answers only for its owner and the teachers invited to it; someone who never taught it is told "Only its owner and its teachers can download its data."
- **O9** An API answer runs under its buyer's processing authority: refused there, the model is never asked.
- **O10** Never an unpaid answer and never a charge with no answer: the price is taken first, and refunded when the model gives no answer.
- **O11** A monthly limit and a month's payouts count every answer, past the database's 1,000 rows an answer; only a finished month is paid.

*Why:* The models' API and its money (migration a_business_can_buy_a_model_s_answers), served by services/model_api_store.py over services/model_api.py.

### As a person in WillpowerLab, I want to find AI models, see how far each is, teach the ones in my field from one queue, and, as a model's founder, release a version only when it is clearly better (N528, N563, N566; the locked prototype "The Model Launchpad").

`tests/test_model_launchpad.py` · Story

- **O1** The list puts my draft on top, then the most mature models, with search, the three filters, and an empty state that offers to start one.
- **O2** A public model's page is in totals only; a private one is unseen by outsiders; nobody's page reads on a person.

- **O4** A newcomer's answers count only once the quiet trust check admits them, and only then are they told, once.
- **O5** One queue across my models, what helps most first, none starved, with Pause; an empty queue says so.
- **O6** An answer counts for two models only on the same question and scale; a private example only for its own model; added examples count once a second teacher agrees and stay under 1 in 5.
- **O7** Release appears only when the new version is clearly not better by luck.
- **O8** A speaker's Takes reach teachers only while their training yes is on.

*Why:* The model launchpad, locked on its prototype (N566); its rules live once in services/model_launchpad.py, before its tables.

### As someone who teaches or owns an AI model, I want to put off an invite and find it again, accept it, add a case I know well, give a spoken answer as its words, and, as the owner, archive my model, make it public only after its disclaimer, so the launchpad's actions all work from the app (N528 items 3, 5 and 12, N563 item 4).

### As a person in WillpowerLab, I want the AI models' list, a model's page, the door, the one queue and teaching to answer from the models' tables through /v2/models, so the launchpad's rules reach me as they are written (N528, N563, N566).

### As someone on the models page, I want "For you" to show the models that need what I know and the ones I teach, so that my answers go where they count (founder, 5 November 2026, N518 items 7 and 8, N520 item 7; App Model MOD-53).

`tests/test_model_match.py` · Story

- **O1** "For you" shows only the models whose words match the person's profile at the matching's threshold (2.0) or more, and the ones they teach, whatever their score; nothing else, and never a score.
- **O2** An answer feeds the one model that fits the profile best at 2.0 or more; below it, no model.

### As the founder, I want the model pipes tight before the launchpad opens to anyone else, so that no teacher can teach a model what it is tested on, no newcomer is let in on another newcomer's word, a withdrawn training yes reaches every model, counts are whole, and a private example stays private (N615 item 2).

`tests/test_model_pipes.py` · Story

- **O1** (PIPE-01) An answer on an exam example, or on an example the teacher added themself, is refused and nothing is stored.
- **O2** (PIPE-02) Two new teachers who answer the same examples alike are not admitted on each other's answers: a newcomer is measured against trusted teachers and known answers only.
- **O3** (PIPE-03) A speaker whose Takes are examples in more than twenty models turns the training yes off: at the next run their examples leave every model.
- **O4** (PIPE-04) A model with more than a thousand counted answers shows every one of them in its counts.
- **O5** (PIPE-09) A teacher the owner invites after the model went public is never sent, and cannot answer, the examples that were private; a teacher invited while it was private still is.

*Why:* The audit of the launchpad's code (N615); the build's stories PIPE-01 to PIPE-09 (docs/design/the-blank-setup-2026-11-10).

### As someone starting a model, I want the setup to walk me through three chapters one step at a time, propose what my model does and which AI does each part, check my data the way a data scientist would, keep the final exam fair, and show me afterwards what needs me, in plain words, so a model I start can learn from day one (N528 item 4, N566 items 2 to 6, N570, N585).

`tests/test_model_setup.py` · Story

- **O1** The setup has three chapters with a thin bar; the caption says where I am and optional steps say so (MOD-24).
- **O2** From my three answers the app proposes what the model does, and each part gets the AI that fits it: a voice score is WillpowerLab's voice scorer, picking and answering GPT-4o mini; others say "Soon" (MOD-27, MOD-29).
- **O3** The scale step says what one example is, with a word for every point, and a model can start right there; what is left becomes a checklist (MOD-28, MOD-36).
- **O4** Uploaded data is checked: repeats and near repeats removed, rows with no score skipped, mislabelled rows flagged, names removed, a column naming a person left out; 60 rows are re-rated first (MOD-31).
- **O5** The exam takes whole speakers, never an upload, never one of my first 10; it is set aside by two experts (MOD-32).
- **O6** Shorter and Longer switch the teachers' steps; Public shows its disclaimer first (MOD-33, MOD-34).
- **O7** My page puts "Needs you now" on top where teachers disagree, gives an honest range and the slowest jar, and agreement within one point and exactly; a starting rule is kept or dropped on the first 50 ratings (MOD-30, MOD-37).

*Why:* services/model_setup.py holds the setup's rules and signed words (the locked prototype, version 8).

### As someone starting a model, I want "Start your own" to keep my draft at every step, fill itself from my first Take, tell me when a model like mine exists, let me start right after the scale, take my existing data only with my tick, have me answer the first 10, and let me invite people who each choose; and as a speaker, I want my Takes to teach someone's model only while my training yes is on (N528 item 4, N563 item 4, N566, N570, N571, N575, N582).

`tests/test_model_setup_routes.py` · Story

- **O1** Without the ring row every setup route is 404, and a draft is 404 to anyone but its owner.
- **O2** Three answers, one per screen; ✕ keeps the draft, which the list shows with "You stopped at the scale. Nothing is lost." and Continue (MOD-24, MOD-25).
- **O3** "A model like yours already exists" slides up over the third answer, once; "Set up mine anyway" goes on (MOD-26).
- **O4** After a first Take, Describe opens filled from its answers, with "Filled from your first Take. Change any word." (MOD-54).
- **O5** "Start with this, finish the rest later" starts the model learning after its scale; the rest is a checklist on its page (MOD-28, MOD-36).
- **O6** Existing data counts only after the tick: its rows become the model's private examples, their labels never sent to a teacher, and only the 60 re-rates reach the queue (MOD-31).
- **O7** The owner answers the first 10 on the teachers' screen, never an exam case, and their answers are kept like any teacher's (MOD-38, MOD-55).
- **O8** Public shows its disclaimer before it goes public; the second expert and the people invited each get an invite, and "Copy an invite link" is the owner's own chat link (MOD-32, MOD-34, MOD-35, N598).
- **O9** When the processing authorization is not current, no provider call is made and the rules' draft stands.
- **O11** An erased person's id is nowhere in a model's setup: it never keeps one, and the expert and the invited people come from rows the purge registry deletes.
- **O12** The owner's first 10 become the model's known answers; an answer counts only once another trusted teacher gives the same one, and two matching trusted answers settle an example (N570 item 4).
- **O13** A teacher hears a voice example by a signed link, and never receives the speaker, the Take or the snippet.
- **O14** A deleted Take leaves every model it taught.
- **O10** A host's model gathers the moments of Takes sent to them only from speakers whose training yes is on, recorded after it; turning it off takes them out again (MOD-02).

*Why:* routes/v2/model_setup.py over services/model_setup_store.py, and the job services/model_examples_from_takes.py.

### As a speaker, I want each Take to show my strongest moment, praised, and my weakest, fixed with the tool its weakness needs (its words first, else its voice with the AI clip), so that I know what to keep and get one fix on the moment that needs it.

`tests/test_moment_pick.py` · Story

- **O1** The strongest is the Take's highest willfidence clip, a block's best clip standing for it and a clip with enough words before a short one; it is praised whatever its voice and called my most confident.
- **O2** The weakest is, from another block, the clip whose own voice shows the strongest problem the AI clip can teach (pace, pauses, a flat loudness range, fillers, against my own usual), with a whole thought and a sound read; it is practised with the voice. A clip that shows no such problem is never the weakest, however low its read (N425).
- **O3** One fix, on one moment, its tool by the kind of its weakness (N548): the weakest gets its Clearer version first when its words need help too (and the AI clip stays behind it), else the AI clip; my words on another moment are fixed only when no moment's voice shows a problem, and never with a Clearer version that cannot be given.
- **O4** A slot nothing fills goes to the next best block, praised: three moments when the Take has three blocks, one per block, never fewer than two with two.
- **O5** A clip with no sound read can be the strongest, never the weakest.
- **O6** A sentence that already has my helper words is not the strongest again: the next best is, and it comes back only when nothing else can be shown.
- **O7** On a plan, my level takes the slot it is given, never the strongest's block, never as a fourth moment.
- **O8** A slide I already gave helper words to counts a little less in every slot, so my best and my fix come from slides I have not touched, unless a moment on the worked slide clearly stands out: clearly my best, or clearly my most serious problem (N546).
- **O9** My best and my fix land in different parts of my talk: with my ending praised, my weak beginning is corrected rather than the ending's own error, unless that error is clearly worse; and the part my last walk praised or fixed counts a little less this time (N546).

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-30 (N289: "the praise clips just get the praise", "a negative [word] has been spotted, or it was too fast, that's the second moment", "the third moment, the weakest one, is where we display the AI audio"); 2026-10-28 (N246, "go for the next confident moment"); 2026-10-30 (N284, three moments); 2026-11-03 (N425, "go for A, B and C": the AI clip only where the voice shows the problem); 2026-11-06 (N546, "I also don't want to skip the great and very poor moments", "if I had already the ending praised I should have the beginning corrected"; was N543, "Untouched first"); 2026-11-09 (N548, "it shouldn't be decided based on the weakness of the moment but type of the weakness").

### As a speaker, I want my Ideal Text read to carry no star or suggestion outside the gated changes lane and no scores, so that I see only words and the latest text.

`tests/test_moment_suggestions.py` · Story

- **O1** Whatever lane a stored moment suggestion came from (replace, emphasize, structure), the GET serves the moment with its anchor and playback identity but no star and no suggestion.
- **O2** The GET serves the text with no score, rank or internal read (Never-1: no scores, numbers or verdicts shown to speakers).
- **O3** Serving never writes the canonical or auto Ideal Text (L1, Never-5).

*Why:* Founder 2026-08-10: the old star lane is ripped; moment suggestions reach the speaker only through the gated ``changes`` lane.

### As a coach, I want to see the emotion the speaker named before recording, as their own word, so that I coach from what they felt and not from a machine bucket.

`tests/test_named_emotion.py` · Story

- **O1** The upload stores the named emotion on intake_context as a key from the closed vocabulary (case and padding tolerated).
- **O2** An unknown word is dropped and never blocks the recording.
- **O3** Once the coach's blind labels are in, the coach view shows the stored key unchanged, and nothing else is attached to it.

*Why:* F2 handoff section 2, 2026-08-03: the answer used to evaporate.

### As a speaker, I want the Lounge to show a dot when my coach has said something new and clear it once the Feedback walk shows it, so that I never miss coach feedback.

`tests/test_new_coach_feedback_waits_on_the_lounge_bubble_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Publish lights it, the walk showing it clears it, a later or edited coach word lights it again; a project with nothing is false.
- **O2** A coach word that is not shared never lights it.
- **O3** Another speaker's Take is refused (404) and their flag is untouched.
- **O4** The browser roles cannot call the two functions.

*Why:* 0439, D-FW-5; the Feedback walk lock, flow 1. Runs services.coach_feedback_signal against the real functions.

### As a speaker, I want every stored recording of my voice served only through a fresh signed link, so that my voice is never reachable on a permanent public URL.

`tests/test_object_key_prefixes_are_classified.py` · Story

- **O1** Every object-key prefix the services and routes write is classified, as user content or deliberately not.
- **O2** A user-content key gets no public URL; a non-user key still does.

*Why:* P1 / DPIA RISK-11: casual_voice/ sat outside the list and got permanent public URLs. O1 reads SOURCE TEXT: it finds key prefixes as written in f-strings (f"prefix/{...}") and prefix constants.

### As a speaker, I want my training yes or no kept separate from my processing consent, so that saying yes to one never ticks, blocks or undoes the other.

`tests/test_old_consent_ignores_training_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** With a training policy active beside a bundled one, the bundled status reader still answers, on the bundled policy.
- **O2** The bundled grant writer refuses the training policy.
- **O3** The processing receipt refuses training as a tick; other ticks record.

*Why:* 0377 (P5 packet section 4, items 1-2). Runs on the released rehearsal lane; every case is one rolled-back transaction.

### As a speaker, I want the moment I open to show the card the follow-up rules choose (praise, exercise, coach request or rewrite), so that I never see a rewrite where a coach request was due.

`tests/test_open_card.py` · Story

- **O1** Each cell of the open matrix gives its card: confident -> praise; weak and a problem fired -> exercise (matched) or coach request; weak and nothing fired -> rewrite.
- **O2** Only that closed set of values is served, nothing about the read.
- **O3** A moment is left without a card when its clip cannot be read, and the coach request is left out when no coach is on the panel.
- **O4** Served Confident Voice moments carry the card; notes do not.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-05, N48.1 Wave 1 step 1: the page guessed its card without knowing what fired (AC-9) and showed the rewrite.

### As a speaker, I want to be able to decline coach review and still record, and to have my "yes" recorded when I give it, so that my consent is freely given.

`tests/test_optional_consent_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** When I tick an optional purpose, my receipt holds the required purposes plus that one, and I am authorized.
- **O2** When I decline, my receipt holds only the required purposes, and I am still authorized to record.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-23: bundling consent with the core service is invalid.

### As a speaker, I want a Take stuck at "Working on your take" to be closed off rather than spin forever, a broken config to fail fast, and my Take not to wait behind someone else's, so that I can always record again. (Critical path: record -> process.)

`tests/test_orphan_sweep_and_concurrency.py` · Story

- **O1** A session left in 'processing' with no job is marked 'failed' (which stops the polling and offers a re-record).
- **O2** One failed write does not stop the sweep: the other orphans are still marked 'failed'.
- **O3** A config error fails the job and the session at once, without retries.
- **O4** WORKER_COUNT=N starts N worker slots, so N Takes process in parallel; unset it is 2, and never fewer than 1.

*Why:* "Processing forever" found live 2026-08-04 (no job row to sweep), and jobs dead at fetch_audio after 3 retries on missing R2 creds, 2026-08-06.

### As a speaker, I want my latest answer to each feedback item shown back to me, and nobody else able to read it, so that my judgements stay mine and current.

`tests/test_owner_feedback_answers_route.py` · Story

- **O1** I get one answer per item, at its latest change.
- **O2** Someone else's Take, or a Take that does not exist, is "not found".

*Why:* Q19 A: the answered bookmark's read.

### As the founder, I want feedback pairs released for training only with a yes, my sentence, a signed manifest and a speaker-disjoint split, at most once a week, with failed exports voided and swept, so that no unconsented or partial data leaves.

`tests/test_pair_release.py` · Story

- **O1** Nothing leaves without the founder's sentence for the surface, a bucket and a signing key; the reason is given in words.
- **O2** With everything in place only pairs with a yes in force leave, under one release whose manifest signature verifies, split by the owner's speaker, and carrying no user id, coach id or take.
- **O3** One file per surface per week: a second run that week writes nothing.
- **O4** A voided release's objects are deleted by the sweep and it is marked.
- **O5** A failure after the release row voids it and the same weekly run sweeps what was put.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-30 (L1, L2, L5); build plan ML-8, ML-9; 0405, 0455.

### As a speaker who withdraws, I want my pairs kept out of the next release even if last week's flag said yes, so that my withdrawal takes effect now.

`tests/test_pair_release_eligibility.py` · Story

- **O1** At release, a pair is out when there is no yes in force now; nobody without a yes is even asked about their service.
- **O2** A pair is also out when the owner's service is ending, its Take is gone, its project is leaving, or its texts are no longer a pair; each is counted under its reason.
- **O3** A source that cannot be read stops the surface: nothing is written.

*Why:* PLF-1.1 "Dataset eligibility"; audit PLF-P5.

### As a speaker, I want my Take's transcript ready sooner after Stop, with the same words on the same clock, so that the Ideal Text and the feedback come sooner.

`tests/test_parallel_transcription.py` · Story

- **O1** With the setting on, a long Take is transcribed in parts at once, cut in its pauses, and its words and segments come back in order on the Take's own clock (N418).
- **O2** A part that fails gives the one whole-file call, as always; with the setting off, a Take is always the one call.

*Why:* Founder, the Claude Code chat, 3 November 2026: "build it behind a setting" (N418).

### As a speaker, I want the histories of my paragraphs (every old version) to load fast and exactly as the single-paragraph view shows them, so that my history is complete and the app stays quick.

`tests/test_part_histories_batch.py` · Story

- **O1** Each paragraph's history in the batch is exactly the single view's, and what the paragraphs share is read once, not once per paragraph.
- **O2** If a batched read fails, that paragraph falls back to its own read and its history is still complete.

*Why:* F5, founder 2026-10-08; Never-rule 5 (every old version stays).

### As a speaker, I want never to be charged while the Terms I agreed to say the service takes no payment, so that what I agreed to matches what happens. (Payments.)

`tests/test_payment_copy_contract.py` · Story

- **O1** The check fails when a Stripe key is set and the Terms in force claim that no payment is taken.
- **O2** Every Terms copy we ship describes what is paid for (the paid plans; from Terms 3.7 the packages, N44) and makes no such claim, so a Stripe key may be set (the copy decision came first).
- **O3** This process's own config does not contradict the Terms in force.

*Why:* 2026-09-17: STRIPE_SECRET_KEY was live in production while the Terms said "there is no paid plan and we do not take payment". LIMITS The check lives in this test file only; production has no boot or readiness check for it yet. O3 passes trivially wherever the key is absent (CI); it bites on a box that holds the key.

### As a person in WillpowerLab, I want a profile as on WhatsApp, my name, who I am, my About and my photo, that the people I chat with see, with the photos and files we shared (N366).

`tests/test_people_profiles.py` · Story

- **O1** My profile starts with my account's name; I change my name, who I am and my About, and the person I chat with sees them, my new name as the chat's title too.
- **O2** An empty name or anything over its limit is refused, and nothing is kept.
- **O3** My photo is kept and shown to me and to the person I chat with; anyone not in a chat with me gets 404; I can take it away.
- **O4** Their profile lists the photos and files shared in our chat; mine lists what I sent in any chat.
- **O5** As a user with no ring at all, I have my profile (N456; was ring 6 only).

`tests/test_person_asks.py` · Story

- **O1** Within 30 days of a "no" in the chat, asking again is refused (TOO_SOON); after 30 days it is allowed.
- **O2** A consent no screen can answer (share, email) is never asked (INVALID_CONSENT).

### As the founder, I want retired constructs, demographic inputs and direct provider calls kept out of the code, and exercise routes behind the purpose gate, so that the Phase-1 legal boundary holds.

- **O2** Universal voice confidence has no demographic input.
- **O3** The protected user-data modules import no provider client (a lint: it reads source text).

### As a speaker who asks for deletion, I want the copies our providers hold deleted, or a lawful retention recorded, so that erasure completes and never claims more than it did. (Deletion.)

`tests/test_phase1_deletion_completion.py` · Story

- **O1** A provider copy is reported deleted only when the delete is verified; nothing remains.
- **O2** A contractual retention is recorded with its rule; without a rule it is refused.
- **O3** Anything unverified fails closed: wrong provider, unknown mode, an object ref outside the contract, an unverified or crashing delete.
- **O4** The OpenAI adapter calls a file deleted only when the provider then answers "not found" for it.

*Why:* Phase-1 deletion completion (0312). The freeze's acceptance of every target the orchestrator builds is proven on the database in tests/test_account_deletion_starts_postgres.py and tests/test_phase1_deletion_completion_postgres.py.

### As a speaker whose only recording is a practice attempt, I want my deletion request to actually erase it, so that "delete my account" deletes everything, and nobody else can fold my recording into their erasure. (Deletion.)

`tests/test_phase1_deletion_completion_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The freeze accepts my practice object (the request moves on, the target is pending), and marking it purged stamps deleted_at once.
- **O2** Someone else's request cannot freeze my object.
- **O3** Every target kind the orchestrator sends (FREEZE_TARGET_KINDS) is one the real freeze accepts; any other kind is filed unknown.
- **O4** An older Take linked to my account only by my user id takes my owner principal (0485), so my erasure no longer stops on it; asking twice changes nothing.
- **O5** A Take that names another owner principal stays unresolved, and my erasure still stops for review on it.

*Why:* B-4 (audit 2026-09-22, 0354): the freeze and the mark raised SOURCE_INVALID for processing_practice_objects, so such a request sat at 'requested' forever with nothing erased. O4, O5: founder 2026-10-20 (0485), three test accounts' older Takes stopped every erasure with LEGACY_TAKE_OWNER_PRINCIPAL_UNRESOLVED. These cases EXECUTE the real functions on a disposable database (rehearsal tier, released lane).

`tests/test_phase1_gate_identity.py` · Story

- **O1** A bearer user passes the enforced gate as themselves (the gate resolves that user's principal, and authorizes core service for it).
- **O2** A fresh guest is refused, mints a guest token, and then passes as that new principal.

*Why:* 2026-09-21 401 storm: the before_request gate ran before the view's auth decorator and saw every caller as anonymous (#422/#425/#427). The REAL app runs through routing; only token verification and the ownership/authority reads are stubbed.

### As a speaker, I want the app to open fast without the consent gate re-asking the database on every read, while a refusal or any consent change takes effect at once, so that speed never weakens consent.

`tests/test_phase1_gate_memory.py` · Story

- **O2** A refusal is never remembered: the next read is asked and refused.
- **O3** A write always asks.
- **O4** A consent write forgets every remembered yes: yes, withdraw, and the next read is asked again and refused.

*Why:* S1, 2026-10-08: about three queries ran before every Ideal Text read.
