### As a speaker, I want my transcript cut into pieces that never cross a slide, never end mid-sentence and keep exact audio times, so that each paragraph and moment plays the right words. (Transcript: the pieces are what the Ideal Text and every moment are built from.)

`tests/test_pieces_canonical.py` · Story

- **O1** A piece never crosses a slide: a revisited slide is a new run, every piece's words and audio lie inside one visit, ordinals run in time order.
- **O2** Piece boundaries land on sentence ends, and sentence ends survive ghost words in the punctuation alignment.
- **O3** What is persisted reads back unchanged (text and audio offsets).

*Why:* Founder 2026-07-14: the <=200-char piece IS the moment (F1-CORE).

### As a speaker, I want slide-fit scores, overall scores and ranks never to appear in my readout, while my coach can see them, so that I see words, not numbers (Never rule 1).

`tests/test_pieces_slide_stickiness.py` · Story

- **O1** The speaker readout JSON contains none of slide_stickiness, overall_score or rank.
- **O2** The coach packet carries all three.
- **O3** With the LLM down, a take still persists every piece, each with a lexical (degraded) slide-fit score, so the coach still gets one.

*Why:* AC-9 fence (founder fix-pack BE-5, 2026-07-16).

### As a speaker, I want my Take to open as soon as it is ready, and any skipped step to be named, so that I am never stuck and problems are visible. (Record -> process -> Ideal Text.)

`tests/test_pipeline_degradation.py` · Story

- **O1** The job flips the session to 'ready' before the job itself finishes.
- **O2** A healthy job keeps its result shape (no 'degraded' key); a job whose run fell back carries the stage names, never messages.
- **O3** The Ideal Text assembler saves the text even when a trailing stage fails, and names that stage; a healthy assembly names nothing.
- **O4** When the whole assembly fails it returns False, saves nothing, and says so by name.

*Why:* Audit Q-C1: best-effort stages were swallowed into log lines. The analysis worker's own stages are pinned in tests/test_analysis_worker.py.

### As a speaker, I want the app to turn my voice into a plan, so that each Take works on the one thing that will help most, in words, never a score.

`tests/test_plan.py` · Story

- **O1** The plan is three levels of four Takes, twelve in all, with no practice days (N564 item 2).
- **O2** The bar shows the level's name and its four marks, the done ones filled, never more than four and never a points total, a score or a percentage; after four Takes the next level starts, after the third level the set is done (N564 items 3 and 5).
- **O3** The level comes from what was measured today, strongest first: a key line said faster than the rest gives "Let your key line land"; with nothing measured it is "Make it yours", never a guess.
- **O4** The key line is always the first follow-up, and never more than three; a fact already said is not asked again, a named worry still is.
- **O5** The AI's question is kept only in its signed template's shape; anything else falls back to the plain template, and a slot the AI left unfilled ("{who they will speak to}") never reaches the speaker: not in the question, not in the facts, not on the plan card.
- **O6** The dates read as the chat writes them ("14 November", "Sat 14 Nov", "Tuesday 27 October, 10:00").
- **O7** A Take reaches its level when the check that picked it no longer fires; the next level is the next thing heard, never one the plan already had, else "Make it yours".

*Why:* The plan, built one to one with the prototype (founder 2026-10-26, N203, N204); a level moving on, N134 items 3 and 4, N209; the training as levels of four Takes, never points, N564; the rules are frontend docs/flows/the-plan.md §1.

### As a coach, I want a speaker's plan to reach my queue when it needs me, so that I pick their level blind, or record my video for a level that hasn't moved, and the speaker's plan follows my answer.

`tests/test_plan_coach.py` · Story

- **O1** A plan reaches the queue on its speaker's card as a new plan, the speaker's ask, or a level three Takes without moving, the ask first; a speaker with no Take in the queue gets a card of their own; a coach who can't hear the speaker's language never sees it.
- **O2** The pick is blind: the plan opens on the voice message, the key line and the five levels, never the plan's level; What happened comes with the pick: the coach's level, the machine's, the deadline, and stress and ready only when the speaker gave them.
- **O3** The coach's answer takes the plan out of every coach's queue until the speaker asks again or the level is stuck again; "Change my answer" picks again; "Audio unclear" takes it out of this coach's queue only.
- **O4** A stuck level opens on its name and the Take the coach's video goes on; once shared, the plan leaves the queue.
- **O5** Only the voice message and the key line play to a coach.

*Why:* The coach's side of the plan, built as the founder's prototype draws it (N213); the rules are frontend docs/flows/the-plan.md §1 C5, G10 and H1, and docs/flows/coach-panel.md.

### As a speaker on the plan, I want Will to ask for my yes before the plan's personal questions, and to delete what I gave if I turn it off, so that I share how I feel only when I choose to.

`tests/test_plan_consent.py` · Story

- **O1** With the switch off, the plan asks as it did: no ask, nothing written.
- **O2** With it on and no answer yet, Will asks once, after the speaker's first voice message, and waits; nothing else is taken meanwhile. "Yes, ask me" is kept (the sentence's hash, the policy version) and the plan goes on: the worry question, the two ratings.
- **O3** "Not now" is kept; the worry is never asked or kept, the ratings are not asked (then or at any later moment), the plan still reaches the plan card (its calls hidden, N315) and its end without "How did it go?" or the summary call.
- **O4** The card's Turn off deletes the personal answers: the answers and their recordings, their bubbles in the Lounge, the plan's worry, Will's memory of them (his note is cleared), the feelings before a Take; then it is kept. A step that fails leaves the yes on and says so, never "deleted".
- **O5** A plan made before the switch is asked at its next personal moment: the last ratings, once a set of three levels is done, wait for the yes; "Not now" skips them. A plan no longer ends on a date (N564 item 5), so no "How did it go?" comes the day after one.
- **O6** When the answer cannot be read, nothing personal is asked; a yes that cannot be kept is not a yes: Will asks again before the next personal question.
- **O7** "How do you feel?" before a Take is kept for a speaker without a plan, and on a plan only after its own yes; the plan tells the app which. With the switch off, as before for everyone.

*Why:* The plan's own yes, drawn and approved by the founder 2026-10-27 (N211; Privacy 3.9 section 4e, to be published); the rules are frontend docs/flows/the-plan.md.

### As a new speaker on the plan's ring, I want Will to ask me a few questions in the Lounge before my first Take and give me a plan, so that my practice starts from my event and my own voice.

`tests/test_plan_flow.py` · Story

- **O1** Someone without the plan sees nothing of it: no plan, nothing written.
- **O2** A new speaker with no Take is asked, once: "Hi Artur! A few quick questions before your first practice." and the voice message's two questions; nobody is asked "When is your deadline?" (N564 item 1).
- **O3** "No date yet", on a plan still at that step, gives no date and no two months (N564 items 1 and 7).
- **O4** Each voice answer is transcribed under the caller's authorization, kept with its recording, kept for Will, and shown as the speaker's voice bubble; when the AI fails Will says the plain words, and the key line is always the first follow-up, right after Will's reply ("Let me draft your plan. A few more questions first." is gone, N319 item 4).
- **O5** The two ratings are kept and never shown back as a number: each tap is the speaker's turn, "Answered" with a lock (N339); then, with the calls hidden (no ring row ``plan_calls``, N315), straight to "You're all set. Here's your plan." and the card, with no call asked.
- **O15** With the calls on, they are asked as before: the kickoff call, then the summary call; skipping both still gives "You're all set. Here's your plan." and the card. A plan left at a call when they are hidden moves on to the plan when the Lounge opens, without a summary ask.
- **O6** There are no practice days (N564): after a Take nothing like "That's your 15 minutes for today." comes; the goodbye is "Good job. See you next time."; under it the plan's bar, once per Take: the level's name and its four marks, never "{n} of {total} practices done."; the first open on a later day without a Take writes "Hi Artur, good to see you back!" with the bar, and today's one thing, once.
- **O7** A new project's first Take, on a plan with a talk's date, asks "Is this for {date}?"; Yes keeps the plan, No starts a new plan's questions (no deadline asked), the old plan kept in the history.
- **O8** Only a talk has a date: the card shows it, changing it says nothing, and there are no practice days to change (N564 item 6).
- **O9** Every Take fills one of the level's four marks; after four, the next thing heard, never one the set had; three on a level none reached are noted for the coach's queue, once; a coach's level runs its four Takes too. Nothing about it is said in the chat; the bar after the fourth Take's walk shows that level full.
- **O18** The twelfth Take ends the set of three levels: its last ratings, the summary session while the calls are on, and a new set of three on what the Takes show; the plan never ends on a date (N564 item 5).
- **O10** A Take's walk reads the level the plan had when the Take was recorded, so it reads the same on every opening; off a plan, or on "Make it yours", the walk is as today.
- **O11** While a speaker is onboarded (the questions; a new speaker's plan until the first Take), the principles show nothing and Will's chat does not answer as them; after the first Take they are back; a plan waiting for the next Take holds nothing back (N243).
- **O12** A voice answer with no speech in it, or no words, is not kept and not sent on: Will says "Sorry, I didn't quite catch that. Could you say it again?" and the same question stays (N262).
- **O13** An answer that puts Will right is not an answer to his question: Will agrees, says back what he now has, and asks the same question again; the correction takes the old answer's place and the old one stays in the history (N262).
- **O14** A new speaker can record before answering: "Start your first recording" is offered while the questions are asked; after that Take's walk Will asks again where he was; the plan, once made, counts that Take as its first practice (N262).
- **O16** With the first Take on (always with no ``first_take`` ring row, N457 item 3; a row can hold it back, N401), a new speaker is not asked in the chat: Will says his four-line welcome (N492), and the plan hands the app the five slides (N462). After that Take's walk Will reads it slide by slide: each answer is kept as the plan answer it replaces (the voice message on slide 2, the audience, the key line on slide 5) and all five in Will's notes; the plan's facts and its topic come from what was said; a day said gives the plan and its card at once, that Take its first practice; no day said asks no deadline (N459 item 1) and gives no date (N564), and the plan and its card above the goodbye. Kept at the end of the Take's processing, the voice message and the key line keep their own voice, and the coach's blind pick plays them (N213); the walk's end then keeps nothing twice.
- **O17** On a plan already running (made from Takes or "Keep practising"), "Two quick taps before your plan." after its first Take ends on "You're all set. Here's your plan." and the card; the plan is not begun again (N538). A new project's first Take on such a plan asks "Is this for {date}?" (H2); after Yes, the ratings it came in place of, then the plan (N538).

*Why:* The plan, built one to one with the prototype (founder 2026-10-26, N203, N204); a level moving on, N134 items 3 and 4, N209; the principles waiting for the onboarding, N243; record first, Will asking again and a correction taken, N262; the calls hidden, not removed, N315; the plan simplified, N319; the goodbye's day, N347; three levels of four Takes, never points, no deadline but a talk's, N564; the rules are frontend docs/flows/the-plan.md §1.

### As a speaker on iPhone or Safari, I want my recordings to play back, and the playback copy deleted with the recording, so that I can hear myself and deletion leaves nothing. (Record -> next Take: hearing the Take; and deletion.)

`tests/test_playback_copy.py` · Story

- **O1** A stored short recording gets its Safari-safe copy (.playback.m4a, audio/mp4) beside it at upload; the original is untouched.
- **O2** The player is handed the original until the copy exists, and the copy from then on (an old recording's copy is made on first play).
- **O3** Deleting a recording deletes its copy FIRST; if the copy cannot be deleted, the original is not touched (fail closed).
- **O4** A video recording or a non-recording file has no copy and keeps its original.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-10: playback silent on the iPhone and on Safari. ffmpeg is stood in for (an audio file becomes b"m4a", a video None): this file pins our wiring, not the encoder.

### As a speaker who asks for deletion, I want every practice recording I make to be registered for deletion, and refused saves to leave nothing behind, so that deleting my data really removes my voice. (Deletion.)

`tests/test_practice_audio_objects.py` · Story

- **O1** A saved attempt and its recording's registration land together (the registration names the exact bytes and the owner); the upload stays.
- **O2** If either half is refused, neither stands: the attempt is rolled back and the uploaded recording is deleted, verified by its bytes.
- **O3** With no owner principal the save is refused before anything is written, and the upload is deleted; the take's missing owner falls back to the project's.
- **O4** The purge turns the registration into a storage target it executes (r2_object at the same bucket, key and hash).

*Why:* 2026-09-16: practice audio was uploaded with nothing recording that it existed, so a purge removed the rows and left the voice in the bucket.

### As a speaker, I want each practice try judged by the machine as Good job or try again with a word of encouragement, up to three tries, without numbers, so that practice moves me forward.

`tests/test_practice_check.py` · Story

- **O1** decide returns praise, again or moved_on with a line key; a "not yet" names the cue that went the wrong way; a rewrite is judged on its voice alone; a voice only a little surer is not yet a Good job, one surer by the lock's margin is (N84).
- **O2** The loop takes up to three tries, a later praise moves the landing try, a fourth try is refused (409).
- **O3** The check route answers the speaker with the outcome and the line key only: no lane, cue value or measured index (Never-1: no scores, numbers or verdicts shown to speakers).
- **O4** The machine's decision is never written as the speaker's own answer (L3).

*Why:* Founder lock 2026-10-06 (the Feedback walk; decisions log N52.3).

### As a speaker who accepted the policy in force, I want my exercise and the cues that route it, whatever older recording the Take is read through, so that the walk offers me what my Data & consent page says is on (WIL-90; founder 2026-10-20).

`tests/test_practice_gates_read_the_speaker.py` · Story

- **O1** The Manager's exercise offer asks about me as my own page reads me (`take_speaker_principal`), never about one recording's acquirer.
- **O2** The verbal-cue gate asks the same question of the same person.

*Why:* #1206 moved the coach's gate; the Manager's offer and the cues still read the recording's acquirer, so an older or claimed Take got no exercise while the page showed practice on.

### As a speaker under a policy where practice is part of the service, I want practice on for me whatever I ticked before, and my objection to blind checks recorded once, so that the service matches the policy I accepted.

`tests/test_practice_is_part_of_the_service_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Under a policy that makes practice required, practice (and personalised practice) reads as on even after I had switched it off; under the optional policy nothing changes.
- **O2** My objection to blind checks is recorded once, however often it is sent, and applies only to me.

*Why:* Founder N55, N66.2 (migration 0454).

### As a speaker, I want the practice attempt I chose (and any I saved) kept, and the rest removed 30 days after the practice closed, so that my storage matches the promise. (Deletion.)

`tests/test_practice_retention.py` · Story

- **O1** Thirty days after a practice closed, every attempt it no longer promises to keep is deleted: the recording first, then its row.
- **O2** Never taken: the attempt they chose, an attempt they saved, an attempt the Voice Album admitted, anything in a practice still open.
- **O3** If the Album cannot be read, nothing in that practice is deleted.
- **O4** A recording that cannot be deleted keeps its row, so the next sweep finds it again.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-16, option A ("keep only the attempt they chose; delete the rest after 30 days"). Nothing deleted by age before this.

### As a speaker, I want my Confident Voice practice recording sent to the transcription provider only under a permit, so that nothing I say leaves the app without a record.

`tests/test_practice_transcription.py` · Story

- **O1** A permit for this Take (recording_id None: a practice try is new audio) is taken BEFORE the provider is called, and the call is logged started then completed (Never-3: every provider call goes through the processing-authorization path).
- **O2** On a provider failure the call is logged started then failed and the error propagates.
- **O3** Each word's recognition ``confidence`` reaches the result unchanged (the practice comparison reads it).
- **O4** With authorization not enforced it is the same code path: the provider is called once and the result is the same, with no permit.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-16: the practice call moved behind the authorization boundary before the Phase-2 gate could be lifted.

### As the founder, I want the clean-up of practice made without the consent tick to erase only the list I approved, so that no one who did tick loses practice.

`tests/test_practice_without_the_tick.py` · Story

- **O1** The preview lists only unticked people who have practice, with a hash; ticked and no_receipt people are only counted, never listed.
- **O2** Execute with the approved hash erases exactly that list.
- **O3** A list that changed since approval (hash mismatch) erases nobody.
- **O4** An unknown category or an unreadable list stops everything.

*Why:* Founder decision 1 (2026-09-25): previewed, approved clean-up only.

### As the founder, I want the database to say correctly who never ticked the "Personalised practice" box, so that erasing practice made without the tick touches only those people (consent).

`tests/test_practice_without_the_tick_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Accepted without the tick and never turned it on -> `unticked`, the only group the erasure script touches.
- **O2** Accepted WITH the tick -> `ticked`, even after turning it off later: a yes once given protects that person's practice from this erasure.
- **O3** Accepted without the tick, then turned it on -> `ticked`.
- **O4** Never accepted anything -> `no_receipt`, reported only.
- **O5** Browser roles cannot call the function.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-25, decision 1 (0363): the tick was recorded and read by nothing, so practice was made for people who left it empty. Runs list_practice_without_the_tick_v1 on the released rehearsal lane, against the optional-consent suite's policy (one optional purpose: the tick).

### As a speaker, I want praise for good delivery or structure to show even when the star lane is off, so that my Yes answers come with a card naming what I did well.

`tests/test_praise_detectors_own_switch.py` · Story

- **O1** With PRAISE_DETECTORS_ENABLED on (star lane off), detector praise is stamped, competes in the praise lane, and is served with its cue keys and no number (Never-1: no scores shown to speakers).
- **O2** With it off, the detector praise is excluded and the lane falls back to the Manager's tentative praise.
- **O3** The worker's praise-only pass writes only praise rows, never an issue.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-06, QA3 A: praise detectors get their own switch.

### As a speaker, I want deleting a presentation or one Take to remove exactly those Takes, and nobody else to be able to delete them, so that deleted work is gone and nothing else is (deletion).

`tests/test_presentation_delete.py` · Story

- **O1** Deleting a presentation deletes every Take of that deck, and no Take of another deck or of a deckless talk.
- **O2** Deleting Take n deletes the n-th oldest Take of that deck, only it.
- **O3** Someone else asking for the same presentation or Take gets 404 and nothing is deleted.
- **O4** A Take the database refuses to delete (its lineage still points at it) answers 409 and the route does not report success.

*Why:* FE PR #161 (hard delete, founder call). The database delete itself (v2_delete_sessions) is stubbed here: these cases prove which Takes the routes ask it to delete, for whom, not the SQL.

### As a speaker uploading a deck, I want a clear "too big" or "wrong type" answer before anything is stored, so that I know to export a lighter PDF.

`tests/test_presentation_extract.py` · Story

- **O1** A file that is not a PDF is refused as an unsupported type.
- **O2** An empty PDF is refused as invalid input.
- **O3** A PDF over the size cap is refused as too large, with the limit in MB as a number and named in the message, so the screen can say it.

### As a speaker, I want one currency for my practice and my coach's time, so that any package can pay for a call and a bigger one simply lasts longer. (Critical path: payments.)

`tests/test_pricing_v4.py` · Story

- **O1** While pricing v4 is off, nothing changes: the shop, the prices and the unit are today's.
- **O2** With it on, tokens read in hundreds of today's units: Starter $39 for 3,900, Plus $99 for 11,000, Pro $149 for 18,000; a short Take reads 10, chat 1, the free grant 120, the coach's minute 200.
- **O3** A package bought with it on is charged and granted at the new price, and Stripe's page names it as the cards do ("Plus · 11,000 tokens").
- **O4** A Take that reaches the coach costs nothing, with v4 on or off: the coach answers every Take and no price list carries a review.
- **O5** A call can be booked with tokens at its own price, a token a cent; with too few tokens, or with v4 off, nothing is booked and the slot is free.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-26, N198 (the prototype's version 3, "accepted, start the build"); N187 for the coach's rate; N205 for the review (items 3 and 4: "Yes, for the first one, yes").

### As a speaker, I want to be told when I said a passage better in an earlier take, and to accept or keep it, so that my Ideal Text keeps my best wording.

`tests/test_prior_take_changes.py` · Story

- **O1** Fragments align by text, not position; an offer is made only where the old fragment clearly beats the new one, anchored on the NEW words and proposing the OLD ones; the new take holding its ground is silence.
- **O2** A fragment the speaker already decided is never offered again.
- **O3** The offer carries no score or internal vocabulary (Never-1).
- **O4** Accept writes an approved bake row; keep writes a dismissal with no replacement (Never-5: the text changes only on the speaker's yes).

*Why:* Founder decision 2026-07-20 #4 (cross-take discernment).

### As a developer, I want a processing refusal raised inside a processing stage to reach callers as itself, so that the upload route answers with the refusal's own code, not a generic 500.

`tests/test_processing_authorization_error.py` · Story

- **O1** A refusal raised inside a processing stage comes out as the same refusal, with its code intact.

*Why:* The refusal was a frozen object; the stage wrapper turned it into an unrelated error, and the upload route answered a generic 500.

### As a speaker, I want my recording processed even if a worker dies or a job is delivered twice, so that a Take never sits on "processing" forever.

`tests/test_processing_jobs.py` · Story

- **O1** A job that already finished is not run again when delivered twice; a job from before the last fix still reaches processing and finishes.
- **O2** A failed run is released and re-queued at once (not marked failed), and the log line names the refusal code and its real cause.
- **O3** An Ideal Text that cannot be confirmed fails for good without re-queueing, and the Take is marked so.
- **O4** Sending the same recording twice reuses the job already running, and a new job carries the current take contract.
- **O5** An abandoned job that used up its tries is marked failed, and so is its Take; a manual retry re-queues the same job with its audio kept.

*Why:* Never-rule 3: a delayed re-queue waited 6 min 53 s in production.

### As a speaker waiting for my take, I want the progress bar to move on real work and the messages to talk about my take, never a score, so that the wait feels alive and honest.

`tests/test_processing_progress.py` · Story

- **O1** Each stage the run's ledger starts moves the bar once, in run order, to a strictly higher point between the job's first (5) and last (90) coarse emits; a finished stage does not move it again.
- **O2** A stage with no position (post-serve, unknown, misspelt) reports nothing: the bar is never guessed.
- **O3** What the speaker reads while waiting is about their take and names no score, rank, percent or number (Never-1).

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-19: "it feels like it's stale"; the bar sat still from 15 to 55 through the longest part of the run.

### As the founder, I want a product purpose to run only when its registry row says it is operational, so that consent decisions, not code switches, open features (consent, on the path that gates a feature).

`tests/test_processing_purposes.py` · Story

- **O1** A phase-1, operational, authorising row runs the handler.
- **O2** A missing row, a phase-2 row and `operational = false` each answer 410 PURPOSE_NOT_OPERATIONAL, and the handler never runs.
- **O3** A registry read that fails (the database raises) also answers 410; it does not become a 500 and does not open the door.

*Why:* routes/phase2_guard.py used to answer 410 unconditionally while claiming to read the registry (0335 made the answer yes).

### As a speaker deleting my account or a project, I want what the Take showed me, my answers and every Paragraph version deleted while job evidence is kept, so that my erasure finishes and keeps only what the law needs (deletion).

`tests/test_product_records_purge_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Without the v1.4 rules the erasure stops for review on these records and deletes nothing (fail closed, as before v1.4).
- **O2** With the rules, seeded by scripts/phase1_retention_rules_v1_4.sql as the founder runs it, the account erasure reaches done: those records are deleted under product-records-v1, the job and its events are kept under job-evidence-v1, and a stranger's records are untouched.
- **O3** A project erasure deletes that project's records and keeps the other project's.
- **O4** Outside a running purge, the append-only trigger (0424) still refuses a direct delete, even with the rules active.

*Why:* N14.3: these four tables stopped every erasure; founder 2026-10-05, decisions log N48.4 Q15 A (retention schedule v1.4). Runs the Python production runs (DataPurgeOrchestrator.run, ProjectPurgeOrchestrator.run) through the SQL client of tests/test_account_deletion_starts_postgres.py, as service_role, on the released rehearsal lane. Object storage is faked (tests/test_take_purge_postgres.py). The lane never applied 0293, so the two Take tables, the three immutable triggers and production's service_role grants are laid down here as those files write them. The lane's ACCOUNT graph lists no job, so job events are proved through the PROJECT graph (0380). The job plumbing (0425: outbox, carry-over, orphan metadata) is not covered here.

### As a speaker, I want to archive a project so it leaves my list and can be restored, so that my list stays tidy without losing anything.

`tests/test_project_archive.py` · Story

- **O1** Archive stamps archived_at only on the owner's own, not-erased project.
- **O2** Restore clears archived_at on that same project.
- **O3** A project that is not the owner's (or is erased) is a 404.
- **O4** The list hides an archived project; Data & consent (include_archived) still lists it, marked archived.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-26, decisions log N14.

### As a speaker, I want Delete on a project to be a request I can cancel within 7 days, with no new Takes added meanwhile, so that I can change my mind and nothing new lands in a project about to go.

`tests/test_project_deletion.py` · Story

- **O1** Asking to delete my project creates a pending request; someone else's project answers "not found".
- **O2** A project waiting to be deleted refuses a new Take, but if the lock cannot be read, recording still goes ahead.
- **O3** Cancelling after the operator confirmed is refused.
- **O4** The operator's confirm returns the id of the erasure it starts.
- **O5** What the speaker is shown about the request never names the owner.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-25, decisions log N8.

### As a speaker, I want my project deletion request handled safely in the database, so that only I can ask, a double tap is one request, and cancelling leaves a record (deletion).

`tests/test_project_deletion_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The owner's request is pending and due in 7 days.
- **O2** Someone else's request for my project is refused (PROJECT_NOT_FOUND).
- **O3** A double tap (same key) returns the same request; the same key can never name a different project.
- **O4** At most one open request per project: a second key returns the open one.
- **O5** Cancelling works while pending and keeps the row as `cancelled`; a confirmed request cannot be cancelled.
- **O6** Browser roles can neither call the functions nor read the table.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-25, decisions log N8 (0364): Delete is a request an operator confirms within 7 days. Runs request_/cancel_project_deletion_v1 on the released rehearsal lane.

### As a guest speaker, I want only my own guest token to open my projects, and none once the account is claimed, so that nobody else can open my work.

`tests/test_project_ownership.py` · Story

- **O1** The token issued to a guest opens that guest's own unclaimed principal.
- **O2** The same token is refused once the principal is claimed, and against another guest's secret.

### As a speaker, I want deleting one project to erase its words and audio and leave my other project untouched, so that I can remove one talk without losing everything.

`tests/test_project_purge_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The confirmed project's erasure finishes, and my delete request is marked done.
- **O2** That project's words are gone and its audio deleted; its project and Take rows stay only as empty receipts.
- **O3** My other project keeps its name, its Take and its words, and my account and consent record stay.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-26: "make the delete work" (N8, N9, N12).

### As a speaker deleting one project, I want only that project's rows deleted, and any account table the project purge cannot place to stop it for review, so that a project deletion never silently keeps a project's rows and never wipes my account (deletion).

`tests/test_project_purge_scope.py` · Story

- **O1** Every name the project scope places (re-pointed, account-level, wiped with its parent, held) is a real account-keyed registry entry, placed exactly once, and every re-pointed locator is one the project graph fills.
- **O2** A re-pointed table counts only this project's rows; an account table nobody placed, where the account has rows, stops the purge for review.
- **O4** A speaker with lesson bookings, goal history, a coach's card about their goal, Will's private note, feedback answers and memory events can delete a project without it stopping for review: the project's Takes' answers and events go with it, the account's rows stay, and the account purge still deletes every one of them.
- **O3** The project orchestrator refuses an account request and the account orchestrator refuses a project request; each request is routed to its own orchestrator.

*Why:* P1-B, decisions log N8/N12: the project graph lists no principal or user, so an unplaced account-keyed table would reach no row at all. O4: found 2026-10-20, the WIL-74 tables (speaker_memory and its events, feedback checks, goal history, the coach's goal card) were never placed, so a project purge for any speaker with a note stopped for review with PROJECT_SCOPE_UNRESOLVED. Python orchestrator only, over the in-memory `PurgeDb` of tests/test_purge_user_locators.py; the real project purge runs on the released lane in tests/test_project_purge_postgres.py. Whether each re-pointed column exists in the schema is not checked here: that purge would fail on a missing column there.

### As a speaker recording my next Take, I want the recording screen set up from my project: the words from my latest spoken Take and the project's current slides, so that I never re-enter my setup. (Critical path: next Take.)

`tests/test_project_setup.py` · Story

- **O1** The slides and presentation come from the latest spoken Take that carried a deck, with the Take they were first used on; the words (topic, audience) from the latest spoken Take; a read-aloud Take never counts.
- **O2** The setup is only the setup fields: no score, count, Take number or session id reaches the speaker (Never-1), and the setup itself is there.
- **O3** Someone else's project answers 404 and shows nothing of it.

*Why:* #1144 (founder 2026-10-16), then N257 (2026-10-29): the deck is the project's current one, updated inside the project.

### As a speaker, I want my Take found only when the Take, the project and the owner all match, so that no one can read my Take by guessing ids.

`tests/test_project_tenant_isolation.py` · Story

- **O1** Looking up my Take with the right project but someone else as owner returns nothing, because all three ids are checked together.

### Every LLM prompt in services/prompts/ is hashed into prompts.lock.json, and the code matches the lockfile.

`tests/test_prompt_registry.py` · Guard

- A prompt (or its builder logic) edited without regenerating the lockfile; the failure names the prompt ids that were added, removed or changed.

*Why:* If removed: A prompt can change without review and the speaker's feedback shifts silently. (The behavioural half, golden evals against the changed prompts, runs in the prompt-evals CI job.)

### As the founder, I want every measurement stamped with a hash of the detector that made it, and the rating write never broken by the stamp, so that I can tell if fillers or other definitions changed under my data.

`tests/test_provenance.py` · Story

- **O1** The hash ignores reordering and moves when a word is added.
- **O2** The live filler lexicon hashes to a seed a migration records.
- **O3** Unknown (no recorded hash, unknown detector) is SUSPECT; an unreadable detector_version table stamps nothing (UNSTAMPED); a match stamps ok.
- **O4** A missing label_revision table never fails a rating write.

*Why:* "If fillers are corrupted all my data goes nuts" (founder, 2026-08-10).

### As a speaker, I want a brief database hiccup not to fail my Take, so that processing continues instead of sitting at 59%.

`tests/test_provider_permit_dropped_connection.py` · Story

- **O1** A dropped connection or a timeout is sent again with the same key until it answers.
- **O2** A real denial is final and is not sent again.
- **O3** A database that never answers gives "unavailable" (503) after a bounded number of retries, not "denied".
- **O4** The log line for a refusal names the check that failed.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-15: a Take sat at 59% for seven minutes.

### As a speaker whose Take is retried later, I want an expired permit replaced, so that a retry the next morning still processes my Take.

`tests/test_provider_permit_reissue.py` · Story

- **O1** An expired permit handed back for a repeated request is issued again under a new key derived from the old one, and the original record is kept.

*Why:* Founder's Take of 2026-10-02 failed every retry on a 15-hour-old permit.

### As a visitor, I want to read the Terms and Privacy Policy without signing in, and my sign-up to record the Terms version I was shown, so that I see what I agree to and the record says what I agreed to (consent).

`tests/test_published_policy_text.py` · Story

- **O1** Anyone, with no owner resolved, gets the exact stored text of the one policy in force, cacheable for five minutes.
- **O2** A policy not yet activated, already retired, or unreadable is never served: 404 PROCESSING_POLICY_INACTIVE.
- **O3** A sign-up records the Terms version of the policy in force, not the old constant; a sign-up without the tick creates no account.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-25, decisions 2 and 3: /privacy could not load for a visitor and /terms showed the retired v1.2 text.

### As a speaker deleting my account, I want my "skip" answers emptied but kept as a receipt, so that my erasure finishes instead of stalling (deletion).

`tests/test_purge_owner_skip_rows.py` · Story

- **O1** An account erasure finds my skip rows by my user id (not my principal) and files them as a tombstone under the deletion-evidence rule; someone else's skips and coaches' rows are not counted.
- **O2** A coach's own rows in the same table still stop that coach's account erasure for review.

The wipe itself is proved on the real schema by tests/test_a_skip_keeps_an_empty_receipt_postgres.py.

### As a speaker deleting one project, I want only account-wide deletions to pause my services, so that my other projects keep working.

`tests/test_purge_scope_postgres.py` · Story

- **O2** All nineteen functions are installed and each carries the narrowed predicate exactly once, and none keeps the old account-wide one.

### As a speaker deleting my account, I want my older Takes, saved before a Take carried its owner, erased with the rest, so that "delete my account" does not stop on them (deletion).

`tests/test_purge_settles_legacy_take_owners.py` · Story

- **O1** Before an erasure freezes, my older Takes take my account's owner (0485), and the frozen inventory holds no unresolved legacy Take.
- **O2** When they cannot be settled, the erasure still stops for review on them, exactly as before (fail-closed).

### As a speaker deleting my account, I want rows saved under my user id (coach readings, listener answers, votes, my chats, my website chats) deleted too, so that nothing outlives my account (deletion).

`tests/test_purge_user_locators.py` · Story

- **O1** An account erasure whose principal id is not its user id deletes exactly my rows in each user-keyed table and leaves everyone else's.
- **O2** A project erasure leaves those rows: they belong to the account.
- **O3** A `principal` locator addresses only a column that holds a principal, so the next table keyed by a user id cannot be filed under it again.

This file covers the PYTHON orchestrator only, through its public steps (build_inventory, freeze_inventory, resolve_targets). `PurgeDb` below is an in-memory stand-in for the tables and the purge RPCs, NOT the SQL: the freeze and resolve functions are proved on the released lane by tests/test_purge_scope_postgres.py and tests/test_project_purge_postgres.py. tests/test_purge_owner_skip_rows.py, tests/test_purge_v1_4_rules.py and tests/test_project_purge_scope.py import `PurgeDb` from here.

### As the founder, I want the v1.4 retention rules to change nothing until I run the signed script, and then product records to be deleted and job evidence kept, so that nothing changes before I sign (deletion).

`tests/test_purge_v1_4_rules.py` · Story

- **O1** Every ruled dependency names one of the two signed rules with that rule's category; before the rule only the job row was ever anything but external_review; the signed schedule names every one of them.
- **O2** Inert until signed: with no rule, a rule of the wrong category or a withdrawn rule, a product record stops the erasure for review and the job row is a plain delete, exactly as before v1.4, in the account scope; in the project scope the record stops the purge as unplaced.
- **O3** With the rules active, a product record is deleted under its rule (only my rows; in a project purge only that project's) and the job is kept under the job-evidence rule.
- **O4** A rule withdrawn after the freeze deletes nothing.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-05, decisions log N48.4 Q15 A. Python orchestrator only, over the in-memory `PurgeDb` of tests/test_purge_user_locators.py. The real tables, scripts/ phase1_retention_rules_v1_4.sql and migration 0424's trigger are proved on the released lane by tests/test_product_records_purge_postgres.py.

### As a person using the WillpowerLab iPhone app, I want a notification when my coach's feedback is in or someone in my chats writes, so that I come back at the right moment (N385, for N365 item 4).

`tests/test_push_devices.py` · Story

- **O2** Without Apple's push key on the server, nothing is sent.
- **O3** An app run from Xcode is reached on Apple's sandbox, which is then remembered; the push carries the title, the line and where to open.
- **O4** An address Apple no longer knows is forgotten.

### As a host, I want one recording to answer everyone who asked me the same thing, so that I answer a question once and everyone who asks it, now or later, gets my answer in their own chat with me (N584).

`tests/test_question_bundles.py` · Story

- **O1** Questions that ask the same thing gather in one card in Will's chat (N602): "2 people asked", the question large, "Ariel and Ola", "See who asked" and "Record one answer"; "See who asked" opens "Who asked", each person's own words, name and where they asked ("in your chat", "on your website"), then "Split them up"; a different question starts its own card.
- **O2** My one recording goes to everyone in the bundle, each privately in their own chat with me, under their own question quoted; nobody gets it twice.
- **O3** Someone who asks the same thing later joins the bundle and waits for me: my assistant sends nothing by itself yet (N606). The card is back with "Record one answer", and my next recording reaches only them.
- **O4** Without the chat builder's ring nothing is bundled and the queue is 404.
- **O5** My own voice replies in my chats are never bundled as questions.
- **O6** My recorded answer reaches each asker's iPhone like any answer.
- **O7** "Record one answer" asks my recording consent again: withdrawn, nothing is sent.
- **O8** When an asker's account is erased, their words leave the bundle: it reads as the next question, and nothing of theirs is kept.

*Why:* "how do we bundle the people who have similar questions ... so that I can answer it to that whole group with my one recording?" (founder, 9 November 2026, N584).

`tests/test_reacceptance_signal_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Someone holding a receipt for an older policy only is flagged reacceptance_required, with the version they last agreed to.
- **O2** A first-timer (no receipt at all) is not flagged and has no version: the two people are now told apart.

### As a speaker who recorded Takes, I want my account deletion to finish and leave only an empty receipt, so that every word and recording is gone.

- **O1** Erasing an account whose Take has its permanent record still finishes, with nothing left for manual review.

### As a speaker, I want my Ideal Text ready quickly after I answer feedback, and my answer never blocked by that refresh, so that the next open is fast and answering always works.

- **O1** However many answers land in one window, the document is rebaked once; an answer after the window closes asks for the next rebake.
- **O2** Never-3: a broken queue never fails the answer - it is still saved.

### As a speaker, I want only my spoken takes to count as takes, and a new spoken take never folded into an earlier re-read, so that my take numbers and Ideal Text stay right.

`tests/test_recording_lane_guards.py` · Story

- **O1** A "read" upload without its spoken take (no paired_session_id), or an ideal-text re-read (no paired_snippet_id, lane retired), gets 422 and nothing is stored.
- **O2** Every accepted upload gets a fresh Take id; a session id the browser sends (a spent one) is never reused for the new recording.

### As a speaker, I want my chosen helper words to stay on their slide after a later Take, and out-of-date ones refused, so that my helper words stay until I pick new ones.

`tests/test_recording_roots.py` · Story

- **O1** Helper words whose Paragraph was changed or belongs elsewhere are refused rather than shown in the wrong place.
- **O2** The helper-words route returns the helper words currently chosen.
- **O3** Helper words picked in an earlier Take still appear, on the slide's Paragraph now.

`tests/test_remaining_route_contracts.py` · Story

- **O2** A transcript edit lands on the right snippet or chunk; bad edits are refused.

### As a speaker who repeats a phrase by accident, I want a clearer version offered in my own words, so that I see the sentence said once without anything added.

`tests/test_repeated_phrase_feedback.py` · Story

- **O1** Words said twice in a row, or a sentence that stops and starts over, are offered back said once, using only my words.
- **O2** Emphasis ("Thank you, thank you.") and a sentence repeated on purpose are left alone.
- **O3** The repeat fix wins the rewrite lane over the weaker fallback.
- **O4** A weak opener ("So", "Well,") goes with the repeat (N124).

### As a coach, I want a mid-take re-read to show as part of its take, not a separate item, so that I review one take as one piece of work.

`tests/test_reread_fold.py` · Story

- **O1** The coach queue row marks the parent take (has_reread), is pseudonymized, and carries no user_id and no intake context.
- **O2** A read never competes as a take: only spoken takes count.
- **O3** A read enters the review flow but never sends its own coach email; a spoken take does.

`tests/test_research_quorum.py` · Story

- Only blind answers in the ledger's lanes count; a self-report, a legacy "peer" lane, a row with no lane and an answer given after seeing the clip's other side don't. Two "Not sure" answers are two labels, but no agreement and no kappa pair.

*Why:* LEARNING-AND-QUORUM §3 B3: the figures counted the "peer" lane and rows with no lane, and treated Not sure as a definite answer.

### As the founder, I want old data cleaned on schedule only when I turn the live key on, in a safe order, so that nothing is deleted early or out of order.

`tests/test_retention_cleaner.py` · Story

- **O1** A dry run only counts what is due and deletes nothing.
- **O2** A live run is refused while the live key is off.
- **O3** A live run deletes in order: old guest accounts, then the measurements, then the recording, then its event, then old logs, then old financial rows.
- **O4** If the code's list of tables disagrees with the database, nothing is deleted; the cleaner only touches tables reviewed for deletion.

### As the founder, I want the clean-up on the real schema to delete exactly what is due and nothing kept for evidence, so that I can trust the first live run.

`tests/test_retention_cleaner_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The report, a dry run and the founder's script count the same lines, exactly the rows due in the world built here; a dry run deletes nothing. An account under erasure is left to that erasure.
- **O2** A live request with RETENTION_CLEANER_LIVE off is refused and deletes nothing.
- **O3** A live run deletes exactly the due rows and objects (rule 1 guests via the account purge, rule 2 recordings with their voice measurements, rule 3 logs but never dev_bugs, rule 4 financial records) and no evidence row.
- **O4** A second run finds nothing left to do.
- **O5** A recording whose object will not go keeps no deletion event, but its measurements still go.
- **O6** Without the purge kill switch no guest is erased (rules 2-4 still run); a guest whose erasure stopped for review is never re-run.

*Why:* Migrations 0423/0426, services/retention_cleaner.py; founder 2026-10-05, decisions log N48.4 Q16 A and N50. Every case runs in ONE rolled-back transaction on the released lane. The cleaner's PostgREST calls become SQL as service_role (the client from tests/test_account_deletion_starts_postgres.py). The world is set in 2001 (AS_OF), so nothing other suites write is ever due here. Stand-ins: object storage is faked; every retention rule the purge can ask for is seeded active; tables this lane lacks are built from their own migrations; 0426 is re-applied inside the case; Supabase's default service_role grants are given on the other tables only.

### As a speaker, I want a rewrite I answered "Keep my words" to stay away until I change that Paragraph, so that I am not asked the same thing again.

`tests/test_rewrite_declines_q3.py` · Story

- **O1** While the Paragraph's words are unchanged, the declined rewrite is excluded as declined_by_owner and not selected.
- **O2** Once the Paragraph's words change, the same rewrite is eligible again.
- **O3** Only declines from an earlier Take, recorded before this Take began, count; a candidate already in this Take's frozen selection is never excluded.
- **O4** An unreadable decline record applies nothing (the rewrite is offered). (Never-5 neighbour: the speaker's own words decide; nothing is invented.)

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-05, N48.2 Q3 A (PARTS 12.3, the Intent Ledger).

### As a speaker, I want a Clearer version offered only when my words need help, and whenever they do in the ways people really speak, so that I see a better way to say it and never a rewrite of words that were fine.

`tests/test_rewrite_gate.py` · Story

- **O1** Good words never get a rewrite; fillers, hedges, broken sentences, repeats and off-slide words count as needing help.
- **O2** A rewrite opens the moment only if it sits in the moment's clip, is backed by evidence, really fixes the words, uses only my words and is no harder to say; otherwise practice opens on my voice.
- **O3** Mending a broken or repeated sentence counts as a real fix.
- **O4** Words that undermine me (an apology or an ask for permission, the idea played down, self-doubt) need help even once; the same sentence said without them, nothing added, is a real fix that opens the moment, and a playing-down phrase that would need rewriting makes no rule rewrite (N85).
- **O5** A restart in one sentence keeps its last attempt; a sentence said again on purpose ("I will say it again. ...") is never touched.
- **O6** An approval tag at a sentence's end (", okay?", ", right?", ", you know?", ", yeah?") goes, and the sentence ends on a full stop.
- **O7** A playing-down "just" goes; "just now", "just in time", "just before" and "just as" stay.
- **O8** A weak opener ("So,", "And so,", "So yeah,", "Well,") goes with any other rewrite of its sentence; on its own it needs 3 or more per 100 words; "so that" and "so far" stay.
- **O9** On the founder's Take 4, the sentences a coach would reword get a Clearer version, each only taking words out and each passing the gate.
- **O10** Hesitations the Ideal Text already removed never make the words need help, and a rewrite is a real fix only against the words on screen.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-18: "words first, then voice"; 2026-10-23 (N124, "Let's do 1-4"; N121, "There won't be any fillers cause they are already corrected").

### As the founder, I want features switched on per person by ring in the database, with a one-way kill and change history that cannot be edited, so that rollouts are safe and auditable.

`tests/test_rings_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A person at or above the feature's ring has it; below does not.
- **O2** An ordinary kill turns it off and the unkill turns it back on.
- **O3** The Python preview (services/rings.rule_reaches) agrees with the database on ring, attribute rule, default ring and kill.
- **O4** A killed one-way feature refuses the unkill.
- **O5** The change tables refuse UPDATE and DELETE.
- **O6** The service key can read every ring table but write none.

*Why:* 0394 (a_feature_reaches_a_person_by_ring.sql), R-1. Runs on the released lane; every write is rolled back.

### As the founder, I want the feature-ring preview to follow the same rule as the database, a broken read to close the door, and a one-way kill to stay killed, so that what I see in the People tab is what people get and no ring ever stands in for consent.

`tests/test_rings_rule.py` · Story

- **O1** Each clause decides: not killed, ring >= the feature's ring, every attribute in its allowed list; a person with no row has the default ring and no attributes.
- **O2** A feature with a consent purpose is on only while that consent is current; the highest ring does not replace it.
- **O3** The gate's read says yes when the database says yes, and a failed read or an invalid principal answers no.
- **O4** Killing a one-way row is felt at once by the confidence writer (no wait for its cache), and the database's refusal of the unkill reaches the panel as 409.

*Why:* Rings 0394. The database rule itself is proved on the released lane (tests/test_rings_postgres.py); this is the Python side.

### As an operator, I want the deploy-time row-security check to report exposures without ever crashing a boot, and to say SKIPPED when it could not run, so that the site stays up and nobody reads a false "OK".

`tests/test_rls_guard.py` · Story

- **O1** Findings exit 0 without --strict (the boot path never passes it).
- **O2** An unreachable database says skipped, never "OK", and exits 2.
- **O3** Extension-owned routines and tables are excluded from both queries (reads the SQL text: CI has no Postgres).
- **O4** The by-design allowlist is exactly the pinned set (it may only shrink); anything not on it is still a finding.

*Why:* scripts/rls_guard.py runs in bin/railway-web.sh; docs/RLS-AUDIT.md.

### As the founder, I want one page that shows where each planned piece of work stands, read from GitHub, so that I see progress without asking anyone or ticking anything by hand.

`tests/test_roadmap_overview.py` · Story

- **O1** An item carried by an open PR reads "in review", by a draft PR "draft", and "done" only when every PR that finishes it is merged.
- **O2** A merged part alone never reads "done"; a branch with no PR reads "in progress"; nothing found reads "not started".
- **O3** A Linear id in the title of a PR opened since the plan's date ties that PR to its item; an older PR with the same id does not.
- **O4** When GitHub cannot be read, every item reads "unknown" and none reads "done".

### As a speaker, I want a phrase I said confidently to become my helper words and lock its Paragraph in one step, and be removable, so that my helper words follow my choices.

`tests/test_rooting_phrase_qualification_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A phrase I said with a confident "yes" qualifies to become helper words directly.
- **O2** Choosing it locks the Paragraph and sets the helper words in one step; doing it twice changes nothing more, and the Ideal Text is not rebuilt.
- **O3** Removing the helper words clears them and keeps the Paragraph locked.

*Why:* Never-rule 5: helper words stay until the speaker picks new ones.

### As a speaker, I want each rooting-phrase qualification state to give me clear next steps and never a score, so that I am never shown a number.

`tests/test_rooting_phrase_qualification_v1.py` · Story

- **O1** Each ready/pending state maps to its own named actions and coverage.
- **O2** Every blocked, stale or invalidated state offers no action and reads "pending" - never a dead end that exposes why the machine refused.
- **O3** Never-1: no state's controls carry a score or a number.

*Why:* Paired-review blindness (Never-2) lives in SQL behind a disabled exposure flag; a grep of the migration could not prove it, so it is not claimed here.

### As the founder, I want no model to be swapped in through a config row without the promotion flag, so that my speakers' Ideal Text is never written by an unapproved model.

`tests/test_runtime_config_keys_are_enumerated.py` · Story

- **O1** The set of runtime_config keys that can name a model is closed: every surface's key and every model key named in services/, routes/ or scripts/ is enumerated, and an unlisted key is refused, never read.
- **O2** Reading an enumerated key is gated: a row is served only for a surface the founder named; otherwise the caller's default, also for the chat and copilot keys (R-13).

*Why:* R-13 / LEGACY-1 (Job 1 review 2026-09-22): runtime_config is service-role writable with no RLS.

### As the founder, I want the database itself to refuse an unapproved model setting, so that even someone with the service key cannot change the model behind a speaker's Ideal Text.

`tests/test_runtime_config_model_guard_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Writing any of the gated model settings straight into the database is refused with "model promotion not permitted".

*Why:* Audit 2026-09-22 (LEGACY-1, R-13): one INSERT could change the Ideal Text model within a minute.

### As a salesperson, I want a lost deal I note down to point me at the step that stalled and one change to it, never at me, so that the next call goes better and the note does not feel like a verdict.

`tests/test_sales_framing.py` · Story

- **O1** A note about a sale that stalled gets "Which step did it stall at?" once, under its "Noted.", with the sale's four steps to tap (N241 P2).
- **O2** A tap on a step gets that step as my bubble and one change to that step from Will's guide; even when the model cannot answer, the guide's own change for that step; a second tap changes nothing.
- **O3** A sales note gets the step question, never also the worry answer; a note that is not a sale goes on to the worry answer.
- **O4** Without the ring row, or for a crisis note, nothing is asked.

*Why:* Linear WIL-114; Will's guide signed 2026-10-29 (N258).

### As a speaker, I want "Say It Stronger" cards compared only with my own past and never with numbers, and delivered even when the job ends, so that I get words I can act on, not scores.

`tests/test_say_it_stronger.py` · Story

- **O1** The job can wait for the cards, so they land before the worker exits.
- **O2** Never-1: a card's coaching copy with a digit ("3 times better") or an internal term is dropped; the speaker's rewrites and plain-word copy are kept.
- **O3** The model is told how this moment compares with the speaker's OWN average, in words only - never the raw measurement.

- **O2** After a broken connection, the scheduler reconnects on the next check.

*Why:* 2026-10-15: no delayed job moved for 6 min 53 s after a deploy.

- **O2** Another account presenting that token is still refused.

### As a speaker using slides, I want my words matched to the right slide even when the recorder starts late, so that each slide's paragraph of my Ideal Text has the words I said while it was on screen. (Critical path: record -> process -> Ideal Text.)

`tests/test_slide_clock_offset.py` · Story

- **O1** A measured offset moves every slide tap onto the audio clock by exactly that amount (never before 0), so the first words after a tap land on the new slide instead of the old one.
- **O2** No offset, or one that is unusable (zero, not a number, absurd), leaves the timeline exactly as recorded.
- **O3** The intake refuses an out-of-range offset loudly (seconds sent as ms is the classic bug) and keeps a sane one as sent.

### As a speaker, I want my transcript split into one paragraph per slide at the moment I changed slide, so that my Ideal Text follows my deck. (Critical path: record -> process -> Ideal Text.)

`tests/test_slide_word_split.py` · Story

- **O2** Every slide of the deck gets its paragraph, even one nothing was said on.

- **O4** A run-on sentence gets a full stop at a real pause, so a piece can end there, and not one word is changed.
- **O5** The boundary measurement finds the pause a speaker left at a tap, and reports a tap they talked straight through.

`tests/test_snippet_transcription_request.py` · Story

- **O1** The file name is fixed up to its lowercase extension and its type sent; no language is forced without a hint.
- **O2** The returned text is trimmed, malformed word times are dropped, the duration is in ms, and the clip's seconds are recorded as usage.

- **C2** the chat no longer handing the note to Will

- **C4** a note carrying a number reaching Will, or the founder unable to correct it

### As a coach, I want my rating stored exactly as I gave it, blind by default, so that the training data holds only real, independent human judgements.

- **O2** A wrong-typed value (a truthy string, a bool) is refused, never coerced.
- **O3** A state with no written definition cannot be rated.
- **O4** The lane comes from the rating act's provenance: an unverified coach act is bootstrap, never panel.
- **O5** Aggregation leaves out bootstrap rows, a self-report in any lane and an answer given after seeing the other side, and counts "audio unclear" apart.
- **O6** A five-rater panel at 60% outweighs a unanimous pair.

`tests/test_stories_csv.py` · Guard

- **O2** In the lens I see the notes it came from with their days; Accept or my own words keep it ("Noted. Added to your strengths."); Not now keeps nothing and it is not suggested again (N2).
- **O3** A later note that shows a kept strength gets "That's your 💪 … again." once, and the note is kept under it; the Life Panel lists every note, newest first; only I archive one, and it stays (N1, N3, N5).
- **O4** The first open of the day before a talk (the plan's deadline or a date a note named) brings back one kept strength with its note, once; no date, nothing (N4).
- **O5** Without the ring row, or for a crisis note, nothing is read or shown.
- **O6** The Life Panel's menu has Strengths right after Principles, only with the ring row (N1).

### As a speaker, I want my Accept/Keep taps on a suggestion in the Ideal Text to be saved, so that my choice sticks and I am never told "Couldn't save that just now".

`tests/test_suggestion_feedback.py` · Story

- **O2** A guest without a signed Guest ID (a bare session UUID) is refused 404 and nothing is recorded.

### As the founder, I want Will to show me one pattern from my own # notes of the last seven days, on Sunday evening when there is one and whenever I ask in my own words, and to walk me through it in a lens, so that I name the bug myself, see the principle I had, write my own reflection and keep the principle it teaches only if I say so.

`tests/test_sunday_note.py` · Story

- **O1** On Sunday from 6 pm in my time zone, once, the case comes by itself only when my notes show a pattern, and not again within seven days; with nothing to find, nothing is sent.
- **O2** Will reads only my # notes of the last seven days (the talk goal and my principles as context), and the case is one sentence, no numbers.
- **O3** Asked in my own words, "We spotted a pattern!" and the case come with Not now | Investigate; with nothing to find, one bubble says so, and a # note or anyone else is left alone.
- **O4** The lens asks, in order, which bug (four, or my own new one), which of the two likely principles I had (or None yet), my reflection (or Skip), then Will's new principle; only Accept or my edited wording adds it to my Life Panel; every step is a row of the one thread, a repeat tap writes nothing, and the lens reopens where it stands.
- **O5** Not now under the case lands as my bubble, then "Noted.".
- **O6** Will reads my last case in the chat.
- **O7** A week whose notes show sales stalling asks "Which step was it?" with the sale's four steps (or my own) in place of the bugs, only where the sales framing is on; without it, the bugs as before (N241 P3).

*Why:* Will learning plan, step 2: the Sunday note as a lens (locked 2026-10-26, N158, N170; rules in the frontend's principles.md §1 L).

### As a speaker who sent a Take to my coach and to others, I want their answers gathered under my Take, so that I see my coach's answer and the best of the others in one place, and only I see the gathering (N587 items 3 to 5, N588 item 4).

`tests/test_take_answers.py` · Story

- **O1** Under my Take, one bubble shows my coach's answer and the best other answer under each heading, with the names of everyone who answered; all of them open under "On your speaking" and "On the matter", each with the way into that person's chat.
- **O2** Someone who has not answered is not listed; a Take I never sent is 404, and so is anyone else's Take.
- **O3** A question I recorded and sent to more than one person (N596) gathers its answers the same way.

*Why:* "if you send them the request and they all answered, you should be able to receive all of them" (founder, 9 November 2026, N587).

### As a speaker, I never want to see my pitch, pace or pauses as numbers, so that feedback stays in words (Never rule 1).

`tests/test_take_comparison.py` · Story

- **O1** The retired take comparison (GET /v2/explore/arc/<id>/take-comparison, founder 2026-10-19) answers 410 GONE, reads nothing, sends no measurement number, and logs retired_route_called so any caller left shows up.

*Why:* It sent raw Hz, wpm and pause milliseconds to the speaker, and no frontend called it. services/take_comparison.py has no caller left; deleting it is a follow-up.

### As a speaker, I want the walk to know who my talk is for and what I want them to do, from what I already told Will or my plan, so that the moment it praises and the one it fixes are the ones my talk needs, and nothing changes when it knows nothing.

`tests/test_take_context.py` · Story

- **O1** What I told Will (the talk's setup) comes first, then my plan's answers, then a guess read from my Take; a guess never replaces something I said. The key line I said to Will counts on the talk's later Takes too (N294 item 2).
- **O2** A sentence that carries my key line or my call to action counts in full when I said it, at half weight when it was only a guess, and not at all when nothing is known.
- **O3** The walk picks that sentence as my strongest when it is close to the top, and its fix lands there first; with no context the pick is as before.
