### As a speaker, I want deleting a Take to either really delete it or tell me it cannot, so that the app never claims a delete that did not happen. (Critical path: deletion.)

`tests/test_take_delete_lineage.py` · Story

- **O1** Deleting my own Take answers 200 and the row is gone.
- **O2** A Take the database refuses to delete (its lineage still points at it) answers 409 TAKE_HAS_LINEAGE, and the row is left exactly as it was, its recording and report links included.
- **O3** A presentation's Takes delete all together or not at all: one refused Take means 409 and every Take of it is still there.
- **O4** A delete the database did not apply is never answered as a success.
- **O5** Only my own Takes can be deleted: a stranger gets 404 and the delete statement itself is scoped to the owner.
- **O6** The hourly cleanup skips a Take with lineage without counting it as deleted or paging anyone.

*Why:* 2026-09-25: links were nulled before a refused DELETE and the route answered 200 (the same defect reverted the project delete, #647). Covers the Python routes and repository only, against a table double that refuses a DELETE the way PostgreSQL does (one statement, all or nothing); the database's own RESTRICT keys are not exercised here.

### As a speaker, I want the Confident Voice moment I am asked about to be quoted from my own words in this Take, so that the feedback points at what I really said.

`tests/test_take_feedback_candidates.py` · Story

- **O1** The quote is the phrase the model picked when I said it; else a phrase I said that the Ideal Text shares; else the start of that slide's paragraph (a route), never words from elsewhere.
- **O2** Regions that are malformed or hold no word are skipped; a quote is only taken from a region with a word.

*Why:* Pinned before the split into named stages (audit W1, 2026-09-28); tests/test_take_review.py covers the product cases.

### As a speaker, I want my answer to any feedback item I was shown to be accepted, so that I never see "feedback item is not in this Take's frozen set" on an item I just saw. (Critical path: feedback -> next Take.)

`tests/test_take_feedback_freeze_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Every item of a Take frozen under V3 takes an answer, not only the first.
- **O2** An item that was never served is refused.
- **O3** A set without a Confident Voice item cannot be frozen.
- **O4** A Take frozen under V2 keeps its V2 set: a V3 item is refused there, and freezing again does not replace the set (the claim is insert-once).
- **O5** The record of what was shown takes a V3 selection of six items, and still refuses one without Confident Voice.

*Why:* #597: the founder's red bar under the Confident Voice question; no lane installed either function until then. Runs on the freeze lane of scripts/rehearsal_tier.sh against the real claim_ideal_text_feedback_set_v1 and record_take_feedback_response_v1. The target must be a disposable local database whose name starts with ``willab_freeze_``.

### As a speaker, I want every required kind of feedback (Confident Voice, a Clearer version, praise) to reach me, so that an old focus rule cannot silently drop one.

`tests/test_take_feedback_manager.py` · Story

- **O1** With the current feedback contract, all three required kinds are kept even when an older focus rule points at one Paragraph.

### As a speaker, I want one whole Take turned into my strongest moment, praised, and one thing to improve with the tool it needs (a clearer version for words that need help, else the AI voice on my weakest), and honest coverage of my slides, so that the feedback is fair and evidence-backed.

`tests/test_take_feedback_policy_v3.py` · Story

- **O1** The Take is cut into blocks of the right size; when the two best clips fall in one block, the boundary between them moves (40 to 120 words, never across a slide, never cutting a clip); a one-block Take whose two clips have 5 or more words each becomes two blocks; and a short Take still shows me two moments: when no cut keeps 40 words a side and nothing else could be shown, the block is split between the two (N246); each such block names its typed exception.
- **O2** The One thing walk (N309, from N289's pick): the strongest is praised and marked; one fix only, the clip whose words need help with the Clearer version, else the weakest heard clip with the voice; no next best is shown; praise is anchored inside the praised block.
- **O3** Coverage is counted per slide, a slide without a clip says why, and the bar is higher from Take 3.
- **O4** The between opens on a Clearer version only when the rewrite passes the gate; with a weak fallback the fix is the weakest with the voice. Hesitations only its transcript heard never open one (N121).
- **O5** A shown moment whose own words hedge opens on a Clearer version with its hedges left out, nothing added, even when its voice cleared the floor; a hedge's words that are not a hedge where they stand stay.
- **O6** A shown moment whose voice reached the floor offers its helper words, praised or corrected with its Clearer version; a voice under the floor does not (N113). A praise has no AI clip (N289).
- **O7** A shown moment whose sentence restarts or asks for approval opens on the Clearer version: with the undermining opener for an approval tag or a playing-down "just", with the plain opener for a restart.
- **O8** In a shown block the moment is its highest-willfidence clip that can be served; a block already frozen keeps its clip.
- **O9** On a plan, this week's level is the one fix: "Say it once" the between; never the strongest; a pace or pause week whose Take has a stretch has the Sniper as its fix and shows no moment to fix (N289, N309).
- **O11** With or without a plan, when rushing is my Take's biggest issue the Sniper is my one thing to improve and no moment is; rushing that weighs less than what my moment needs, a slow stretch, or no tempo step for me leaves the fix a moment (N323 item 6).
- **O10** A sentence that already has the helper words I had when I recorded gives its place as my strongest to the next; helper words I pick during the walk don't move my moments (N246).
- **O12** A fix my last walk gave that worked (its issue gone on that slide in this Take) is not given again there: the walk picks the next thing, and keeps doing so when the Take is read again frozen; the pace game that worked leaves the fix a moment. When the issue came back, the same fix may be given again. With no walk before, or one that cannot be read, the frame is exactly the one it always was (N359).
- **O13** A moment is my fix with the AI clip only when the clip can be made for it: the picker reads its voice problem with the clip's own choice (the same words, the same rule); a moment whose clip could not be made is never the fix, so the walk never promises an AI clip it then cannot play (the audit's 4.4, N425, N548).
- **O14** When no tool reaches a weak moment (the walk has no fix and my least confident moment reads below the floor), the frame names it so it goes to a coach; a good Take, or one with a fix, names none (the audit's 2.7). A good Take, and only it, tells the walk so on my best, which says "Nothing to fix this time." (N551).

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-20 ("the score decides each", flow page C2, C4), 2026-10-18 ("words first, then voice"), 2026-10-21 (N108, "the voice decides"; N112, "There was no single rewrite in the take"), 2026-10-22 (N113, "After the rewording"), 2026-10-23 (N124, "Let's do 1-4"; N118 and N122, "S×W picks, voice names top"), 2026-10-24 (N128; N118); 2026-10-27 (N209, the level's moment); 2026-10-28 (N246: "at least two confident moments"; "go for the next confident moment"; "Two best + level"); 2026-10-30 (N289: "the praise clips just get the praise", "the third moment, the weakest one, is where we display the AI audio"); 2026-10-31 (N309, the One thing walk: "apply to it the one thing walk"); 2026-11-02 (N323 item 6: "yes, the pace game for everyone when rushing is the issue"); 2026-11-02 (N359: "once a fix has worked on a slide, the walk stops picking the same issue on that slide, and picks it again only if it comes back": "go").

### As a speaker, after my Take, I want the page that chose who hears it to come back once with someone new who fits what I asked, so that the right person can answer; and as that person, I want to hear the Take before anyone knows who I am, so that answering is my yes (founder, 4 November 2026, N477, N478, N481, N485, N486, N493).

`tests/test_take_match.py` · Story

- **O1** With no ring row, or while sending a Take is off, nobody is ever suggested: the speaker's routes answer 404.
- **O2** After a Take, one person is suggested: someone in the speaker's business community (N559 item 1), has no chat with them, and fits what the Take is about; shown as a pseudonym made for this match and their role, never a name, never the name the coach queue gives them, and never what their own Takes are about. Asking again gives the same one; someone who fits nothing is never chosen.
- **O3** Someone already in a chat with the speaker, or still waiting to be let in (N471), is never suggested, and nobody is suggested to the same speaker twice.
- **O4** Send knocks: the one suggested gets a new asking chat in their list, under the speaker's pseudonym, their role and what it is about; never a card in Will's chat, no chat made, and the page does not come back.
- **O5** The speaker's Not now tells nobody: no inquiry, no chat.
- **O6** Before "Open Take" the one suggested sees only who is asking and about what: no name, no words, no recording, no answering. After it, the speaker's name and the Take, words slide by slide; their answer, a Take, makes the chat between the two, with the speaker's Take (a Take message) and the answer in it, under their names.
- **O7** Nobody else can open or answer it, the one suggested cannot before it was sent, and a Take recorded before it reached them is no answer.
- **O8** Once skills are on, among people who know as much and left matching by how they speak on, the one a step ahead is chosen, progressing alike, never someone for being at the same level (N559 item 4); what made the fit and how likely the pick was are kept, never another person's id, and the speaker sees none of it. Someone who turned it off is weighed by what they know alone.
- **O9** Words that say nothing about the subject make no match: Polish function words ("dla", "się"), or words everyone in the community uses ("presentation", "speak").
- **O10** Nobody is suggested who has not recorded a Take in two weeks, or who already has three Takes waiting for their answer or five suggestions this week.
- **O11** Progress is counted within each talk (N494): someone who gets better Take by Take in each talk is progressing, even when a harder new talk starts lower; an answer, a talk of one Take, adds nothing; with fewer than five Takes in talks of two or more there is no progress yet.
- **O12** The one suggested's Not now, before or after Open Take, removes the asking chat and tells nobody: Send still reads "sent" to the speaker. Who declined whose Take and when stays, and the two are never matched again, whichever of them asks.
- **O13** An answer waits five seconds for Undo and nobody sees it: no chat, nothing in the speaker's list. Undo leaves no trace. Once the time is up the chat is made exactly once, even if the timer was lost, and Undo is too late.
- **O14** While matching by how people speak is off, the match weighs what people know against what the Take needs, and nothing else: the arm is "topics", no speaking read is kept, nothing is shown (MATCH-03).
- **O15** The matcher learns from answers and conversations only (N559 item 3): of two people who know as much, the one who answered earlier suggestions within a week and kept talking comes first; one who never answered sinks. Nobody is asked to rate anyone; nothing is shown.
- **O16** On the screen of who hears a Take, your own chats come best fit first: the person who can best answer this Take is first, a chat that fits nothing is left to the screen's order, and no score is sent (N559 item 2).
- **O17** Settings, "Matching by how you speak": off by default; Turn off deletes what Will learned from how you speak, and your chats stay (N465, N559 item 6).
- **O18** Nobody is asked to rate a person: no line anywhere asks "How was practising with {name}?" (N559 item 7, MATCH-12).

*Why:* Matching people happens in one place only, after the Take (N477); a knock in the chats, opened or turned down, anonymous until the answer, which alone opens the chats (N478, N485, N493); every answer is a Take, and the matching weighs how people speak and progress (N481).

### As a speaker, I want a retry of my Ideal Text to find my recording whatever the column is called, so that a retry never fails. (Critical path: record -> process -> Ideal Text.)

`tests/test_take_names_its_recording_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** New code writing only `recording_id` fills `recording_1_id`, and old code writing only `recording_1_id` fills `recording_id`.
- **O2** Changing either name carries over to the other; clearing either name clears both.
- **O3** The mirror adds no foreign key of its own, so it is never a new way for a Take insert to fail.
- **O4** Browser roles cannot call the trigger function.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-26: the retry read `recording_id` from a row that only had `recording_1_id`, so every retry failed under enforce (0383). Runs on the released rehearsal lane against the real trigger.

### As the founder, I want to see whether a speaker is really getting better and whether each fix the walk gave worked, so that I know the moments and interventions we pick move them forward.

`tests/test_take_progress.py` · Story

- **O1** A speaker's progress is read against their own first Takes, frozen: a later Take that sounds stronger than those reads higher, even when it is no stronger than their recent Takes; with too few first Takes it is not measured.
- **O2** The fixes a Take gave (the weakest and the one between) are each marked came back or gone on the next Take of the talk, same slide; not measured when that slide is not in it.
- **O3** The One thing walk opens with the last Take's fix when it rose (N309): its kind and slide, and this Take's best clip there, played by a signed link; nothing when it came back or there is no Take before.
- **O4** The pace game's fix worked when every clip on that slide in the next Take stays inside the green on the side the stretch leaned, the bar that picks a stretch: the Takes table marks it gone and the walk opens with it as the last fix; slower but still outside the green, it came back and nothing is said (N347 item 3, N350 item 3). Where it worked, the walk offers no tempo step on that slide again (N359). A slow stretch off a pace or pause week was never the Sniper, so it is no fix the walk gave (N323 item 6).
- **O5** The next walk knows where in the talk the last one praised and fixed, its beginning, middle or ending, so its own praise and fix move through the talk (N546).
- **O6** A fix counts as given only when the walk opened it on screen: Last time never names a fix I never saw, and names it by what I saw (the voice Exercise when its lost Clearer version gave it its place); with the walk's record unreadable, the frame's fixes stand (the audit's 3.4).

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-31 (N291: "yes"; "ok go with the proposal"; N290: "the next time it chooses a moment it should take into account what the user answered"; N309 item 1: "Last time: {the tip}. You did."; N347 item 3: "Every fix gets its Last time, the pace game too"; N350 item 3: one rule for "the pace fix worked"; N359: "once a fix has worked on a slide, the walk stops picking the same issue on that slide, and picks it again only if it comes back"); 2026-11-06 (N546: "if I had already the ending praised I should have the beginning corrected").

### As a speaker, I want a dropped database connection at the last step to be retried, so that my Take is not thrown away and processed again.

`tests/test_take_promotion_survives_a_dropped_connection.py` · Story

- **O1** When the connection drops while the recording becomes a Take, the step is retried once and the Take is returned.

*Why:* Founder's Takes of 2026-10-02 and 2026-10-03 were re-run from the start after one dropped call.

### As a speaker who deletes my account, I want everything I recorded erased, so that my Take, audio and project content are gone.

`tests/test_take_purge_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The erasure finishes, with nothing left for manual review, and the stored audio is deleted.
- **O2** The Take stays only as an empty receipt (ids, times, state), and the project as a wiped tombstone with no name, setup or deck.
- **O3** Someone else's project is never touched.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-25 "prove it first"; decisions N9 and N12.

### As a speaker, I want Take 2 to rewrite only the slides I spoke and keep the others and my helper words, so that my Ideal Text is what I said last and nothing is lost.

`tests/test_take_review.py` · Story

- **O1** The slide I spoke in Take 2 is rebuilt from what I said; the slide I did not speak keeps its words.
- **O2** My earlier edit is not carried over; it stays at its own version.
- **O3** Each Paragraph keeps its id, and my locked helper words move to their slide.
- **O4** The first Take on new slides (N257) writes the whole Ideal Text fresh from what I said, and my helper words move to the new slide with the same title; the others leave the page.

*Why:* Never-rule 5; founder 2026-09-25 (contracts 8-9); N257.

### As a speaker, I want to send my Take to the people I tick, each as my own message in my chat with them, so that the people I trust hear it and can answer me; as the person it is sent to, I want to hear it and read its words, and nothing else of it.

`tests/test_take_sends.py` · Story

- **O1** While sending is off (TAKE_SEND_ENABLED, default off), the new routes answer {enabled: false}, the send 409 SENDING_OFF, and nothing is written.
- **O2** The list is the other person of each of my chats with a real person: the one I sent to last first ("Last sent"), then my chats' order; no demo chat, no guest, nobody I blocked and nobody who blocked me (N466).
- **O3** With no chat with a person at all, the founder is listed (Artur, speaking coach) with no chat yet; the chat is made on my first send, never on a read, and only once. The founder is the account with the admin email (ADMIN_EMAIL), no env var needed; FOUNDER_CHAT_USER_ID, when set, wins. Nobody has that email: nobody.
- **O4** Sending is idempotent: sending again posts nothing twice and pushes nothing twice; adding people later only adds; nothing is unsent. The line under my recording keeps who it went to, and my last choice is read back. Only my own Take, and never an answer. A send that goes late, after my next Take's line is written, never adds its people to that newer line (N541 item 2).
- **O5** Someone not in the chat cannot open a Take sent in it, not even through a chat of their own.
- **O6** The person it is sent to sees "{name} sent you Take {n}", plays the Take and reads its words slide by slide (the list's last line says whose it is and its number), and never gets the Take's id, its Ideal Text, its feedback, moments or levels.

*Why:* N470 and N479 (founder, the Claude Code chat, 4 November 2026): a Take goes to the people the speaker ticks; never a score or the machine's read to anyone (Never 1, 2). Built on tests/test_chats.py's in-memory chat tables.

### As a speaker, I want one short summary of my Take in words, about my own talk, so that I know what went well without a number or verdict.

`tests/test_take_summary.py` · Story

- **O1** Each Take gets one stored summary, written from its real progress and about my talk (its title, slides, my most confident moment).
- **O2** The summary is only a cheeky sum-up of the Take: one or two short sentences, no greeting and no question anywhere (Will asks his own after it, founder 2026-10-21, N94). A summary with a number, praise that drifts from the approved lines, a question or a next step is replaced by the approved fallback copy, which never calls a fourth Take the third.
- **O3** A coach note rewrites the summary in place, keeping the first one however many notes follow, once per note; a note shared after the review is its own bubble, stored once.
- **O4** Nothing is asked after the Take when nothing is off or missing: a speaker without a WhatsApp number is not asked for it (that waits for the next visit, N294 item 8), and the goodbye, "Good job. See you next time.", comes right after the summary (services/after_take.py).
- **O5** The summary and the goodbye after it sort under the walk's last step, even when my browser's clock runs ahead of the server's.
- **O7** Asked twice for the same Take (the page reloaded while the first ask was being written), the first ask's batch stands: nothing is written, remembered, let go or asked of the model again, so the goodbye stays under Will's question.
- **O8** Nothing waits for the summary (WILL-07): the batch after the walk (the signed line, the goodbye, what Will remembers) is written at once, and GPT-4o's words take the signed line's place later, in the same spot of the thread.
- **O6** The summary can point back to what I said in an older Take (the same project first), never this Take, never a Take whose processing authorization is not in force; a failing or slow lookup leaves the summary as it was, and a number it brings in still falls back to the approved copy (founder 2026-10-20, step 3).

*Why:* Never-rule 1; founder lock 2026-10-12 (section 3) and 2026-10-14; founder 2026-10-20 (the chat keeps the order it shows; one action at a time); 2026-10-31 (N294: no push, settings at the next visit).

### As a speaker who edited a Paragraph, I want Take 2 to replace the slides I spoke with what I said, keep the rest and my helper words, and keep my edit in history, so that the Ideal Text is my latest words and nothing is lost.

`tests/test_take_two_rewrites_an_edited_slide_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** On a real database, a slide I spoke in Take 2 shows exactly what I said, even if I had edited it, on the same Paragraph.
- **O2** A slide I did not speak keeps its last version, including my own edit made after Take 1.
- **O3** Helper words and locks stay on both slides, and a reworded open Paragraph keeps its id and helper words through the page publish.

### The tempo step practises the stretch of the Take the pace scale reads furthest from the green, says whether it ran fast or slow, and names what it was about in a few plain words (founder 2026-10-26, N162, N166).

`tests/test_tempo_stretch.py` · Guard

- **C1** a Take spoken without a pause no longer picks that clip as rushed
- **C2** a Take with long pauses no longer picks that clip as slow
- **C3** a Take in the green picking a stretch anyway, so the step shows for nothing
- **C4** a topic with a number, a quote or a whole sentence reaching the speaker
- **C5** a failed topic call losing the step, instead of saying "that part"
- **C6** a played tempo game no longer kept for Will, or kept with a number in his note, or a game skipped after a try kept as played out

- **C8** the tempo step reaching a speaker the rings panel has not switched on
- **C9** the stretch losing its player (the speaker's orange clip, N166, locked prototype X4gDmGfHH7ivuoRZ9ufiy9), playing another clip, serving a cached signature, or a failed signing losing the step
- **C10** the One thing walk's stretch waiting on a topic it never says (N435 item 6, N452 item 2), its words carrying an "um" or a repeat the Ideal Text never shows (H3), or losing its slide

`tests/test_tests_never_touch_a_real_database.py` · Guard

- **C1** a `pytest` run in an environment that carries a production SUPABASE_URL (founder 2026-10-20: fixture values "arc-9", "owner-1" reached the live database this way) -- it stops before any test module imports.
- **C2** the deliberate way through: WILLAB_TESTS_REAL_DB=1 lets a run against a disposable project proceed.

### As a speaker, I want my Take's feedback moments found without waiting on bookkeeping, so that my Ideal Text opens sooner.

`tests/test_the_arousal_capture_does_not_hold_the_wait.py` · Story

- **O1** Every piece with an arousal read still gets that read stored, with the same value, before the feedback moments go on.
- **O2** The stores go out together, not one database round trip after another (a five-minute Take has about thirty pieces).

*Why:* 2026-11-05, the wait. Driven through the public generate_for_session; only the Take's readout and the speaker's cross-Take cue baseline are faked, and the store itself.

### As someone in a host's chat, I want to type to them where their chat takes typing and to add the photo their chat asks for, so that the bar's middle is always the one thing to do now (N610).

`tests/test_the_bar.py` · Story

- **O1** BAR-N610-1. Where my host's chat takes typing, I type to them and they type back; the three emoji stay as they are; what I type after they block me does not reach them. Where typing is off, typed words are refused, mine and the host's, and so they are in a chat that is nobody's own chat.
- **O2** BAR-N610-2. Typed to the question that waits, my words are its answer: one of the host's own questions moves on to their next line and my words are on their card; a question a person sent is answered with a message that asks nothing back. A message that asks for nothing, or a second answer, is refused.
- **O3** BAR-N610-3. A question of my recipe can ask for a photo: it takes no answers to tap, it is asked in the chat of whoever joins, after my own questions, and their read says so on the question that waits; it is not one of my first Take's questions. Without the chat builder's ring nothing is asked.
- **O4** BAR-N610-4. The photo answers the question: it is kept as any photo, I (the host) see it and can open it, my next line follows; a file that is no photo, a tap, a second photo, or a photo for a question that asks for none is refused.
- **O5** BAR-N610-5. Only a signed-in member with current processing authority adds the photo: a website visitor has no way to before signing up, nobody signed out does, and a refused authority writes nothing.

*Why:* The founder, 10 November 2026 (N610): "if someone has typing on, then you can literally type to them thank you"; "A photo asked for: the middle is 'Add a photo'".

- **O2** It says no before the speaker is bound, after a withdrawal, and for an owner who only ever gave a bundled-era yes.

### As a speaker, I want each action charged once at its price and never when I cannot cover it, so that my balance is always what I paid for. (Critical path: payments.)

`tests/test_token_charge_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A charge takes the flat price once: charging the same reference again takes nothing and writes no second ledger row.
- **O2** Too few tokens: the charge is refused, the balance is untouched and no ledger row is written.
- **O3** The monthly allowance is spent before the tokens that never expire.

*Why:* add_token_charge_rpc.sql: the four-call Python charge could debit twice or debit without a ledger row; the database now decides. Executed against the real token_charge() (migrations/add_token_charge_rpc.sql) on the released rehearsal lane, driven by the production Python services.token_account.charge through the SQL client of tests/test_account_deletion_starts_postgres.py as service_role. The lane does not carry the account table, so each case builds it in its own transaction from its migrations (on a one-column stand-in for v2_student_details, which predates the migrations directory) and rolls everything back.

### As a speaker, I want to buy a package once at the published price and get my tokens exactly once, so that I am never charged a subscription or credited twice. (Critical path: payments.)

### As a speaker, I want token charges to be flat and never to block my recording, so that pricing never gets in the way of my next Take. (Critical path: payments, on the record -> process loop.)

`tests/test_token_pricing.py` · Story

- **O1** An action is charged its flat list price, whoever I am and however my Take went; my reference goes with it so the charge is taken once.
- **O2** The charge never stops the action: an unreadable account, a charge function that fails, or nonsense input all let it through, and nothing raises.
- **O6** A charge that let the action through unpaid is never silent: the founder gets one ERROR line named token_charge_failed (user, action, reference, reason) and one Sentry event. A charge that worked raises no alert.
- **O3** The price modules never read the cost ledger (a cost-derived price would vary with how I performed).
- **O4** An operator top-up with the same reference credits once.
- **O5** My balance is read in one database call (the recording band waited 2.3 s on three).

*Why:* Fence section 6.1 (fail open on the F1 loop) and 6.2 (flat prices). This file covers the Python caller only. What the database does with a charge (taken once per reference; too few tokens refused with nothing taken) is proven on the real token_charge() in tests/test_token_charge_postgres.py.

### As a speaker, I want the most important suggestion to win when two want the same words, so that I see a real correction, not a minor style tip.

`tests/test_tracked_changes.py` · Story

- **O1** On the same words a correction beats a swap, and a swap beats a style bold.
- **O2** A wording suggestion becomes one change on its own words; bad input (a piece whose words are not in the document, an unknown suggestion kind, an already-approved moment) builds nothing.

*Why:* Founder 2026-08-13: span width alone handed every collision to a 'make this word orange' suggestion.

### As a speaker, I want training use of my recordings to depend only on my own training switch, so that an old bundled sign-up yes never counts and withdrawing is clean. (Critical path: consent.)

`tests/test_training_consent_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** Nobody is on by default.
- **O2** Turning the training switch on (with my processing receipt) makes my training yes active, with exactly one purpose row.
- **O3** A bundled-era yes never counts as a training yes.
- **O4** A yes not given on the training switch itself is refused.
- **O5** Without a processing receipt the yes is refused and nothing is active.
- **O6** Withdrawing training ends the yes and leaves the bundled grant alone; the bundled grant cannot be withdrawn through the training path.
- **O7** Browser roles cannot call the grant, the withdrawal or the reader.

*Why:* 0373 (SPEC-training-corpus section 3, P2); founder N2, N10.6. Runs on the released rehearsal lane against the real functions, after the suites that seed a processing policy. It registers the test training policy that tests/test_training_corpus_postgres.py and tests/test_training_corpus_purge_postgres.py then use.

### As the founder, I want a refused training switch to show up in the logs and Sentry without the person's id, so that problems are visible and private.

`tests/test_training_consent_refusal_report.py` · Story

- **O1** When the training status cannot be read, the speaker gets "service unavailable" (503) with the refusal code.
- **O2** One warning is logged and one Sentry message sent, naming the code and never the user or owner id.

### As a speaker, I want training copies made only while my training yes is active, so that withdrawing stops any new copy. (Critical path: consent.)

`tests/test_training_corpus_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** With an active yes (and the corpus retention rule in force) a copy is made, stamped with the yes it was made under.
- **O2** No copy without an active yes, and none after the yes is withdrawn.
- **O3** A source is copied once: copying it again returns the same copy, and a changed source is refused rather than overwritten.
- **O4** A copy's content never changes; only its purge state moves, forward.
- **O5** The copies hold no foreign key to a product table (lineage by value), and browser roles reach neither the table nor the door.

*Why:* 0375 (SPEC-training-corpus section 4, P3; invariant 4). Runs on the released rehearsal lane after tests/test_training_consent_postgres.py, which registers the test training policy (made here if it did not run).

### As a speaker, I want my training copies erased when I withdraw or delete my account, so that nothing of mine stays in the training data. (Critical path: deletion and consent.)

`tests/test_training_corpus_purge_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A withdrawal marks every copy of mine due, and nobody else's.
- **O2** The erasure job deletes my due copies (audio only once its object is proven gone) and never an active copy of anyone.
- **O3** Once a copy's audio is verified gone, the account purge marks it `purged`.
- **O4** Account deletion takes my copies' audio and rows with it; the copies never stop it, and the only thing that may stop it for review is the record of my consent.

*Why:* 0376 (SPEC-training-corpus section 6, P4; founder locks C3, Q3). Runs on the released rehearsal lane after the consent and corpus suites. The orchestrator, the erasure job and its database helpers are the production Python, driven through the SQL client of tests/test_account_deletion_starts_postgres.py as service_role; only object storage is faked.

### As a speaker, I want deleting a presentation or a deckless training to really remove it, so that it never comes back in my lists. (Critical path: deletion.)

`tests/test_training_deletion.py` · Story

- **O1** The owner deletes a deckless session: 200, and the row is gone from the table the lists read.
- **O2** A stranger cannot delete someone else's session: 404, and the row is still there.
- **O3** Deleting a presentation removes EVERY Take of that deck, and nothing of the speaker's other trainings.

*Why:* Backlog 4.4: "deleting a training doesn't remove it" (takes without library rows survived and resurfaced). The routes run against the real TakeRepository over an in-memory v2_sessions table, so "gone" is what the table holds afterwards. The SQL refusal of a Take that still has lineage (409) is test_take_delete_lineage.py's.

### As the founder, I want to erase a training import completely, so that its audio and everything made from it are gone and no speaker's session is touched.

`tests/test_training_import_erase.py` · Story

- **O1** Erasing an import deletes its audio and every row it or its review wrote; another session's rows stay.
- **O2** A session that is not an import is refused, with nothing deleted.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-19.

### As a coach labelling imported talks, I want never to see which clip the machine picked, so that my label stays blind.

`tests/test_training_import_on.py` · Story

- **O1** NEVER-2: the label response carries the coach's saved answer and no pick, block or policy data.
- **O2** The pick is kept server-side: the import stores V3's blocks, picks and policy version, and the coach's rating is stamped with why the clip was asked (``v3_block_pick``).
- **O3** NEVER-2: the import's status poll reports the import (ready, pieces, queue size) and no pick, block or policy data.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-06, panel answer CO1 A, decisions log N56.4.

### As a speaker, I want my training switch to record exactly what I agreed to or withdrew, so that my choice is honoured and erasure follows. (Critical path: consent.)

`tests/test_training_switch.py` · Story

- **O1** Turning on records my own act (the training toggle, not preselected) on the wording and policy the database holds now, binds my speaker identity with it, and records nothing more when already on.
- **O2** A yes on stale wording is refused before the database: nothing recorded.
- **O3** The database's refusals reach me as stable codes, nothing recorded.
- **O4** Turning off records the withdrawal of my grant and queues the erasure.
- **O5** A visitor reads only the wording in force, with no state about anyone; with no wording in force there is nothing to offer.

*Why:* The switch is OPEN (door 1, founder 2026-10-01; code switch removed 2026-10-12), so the route always answers: there is no dark state left. Covers the Python caller only (services/training_consent.py over a fake database). The SQL refusals O3 maps (0373/0430) are not proven here, and no rehearsal-tier test asserts the database's own refusal of other wording.

### As a speaker, I want my recording sent to transcription correctly, so that my transcript is accurate in my language. (Critical path: record -> process, the transcript seam.)

`tests/test_transcribe_audio.py` · Story

- **O1** The request asks whisper-1 for verbose JSON with segment AND word timestamps, and the answer comes back as text, duration, language, segments and words (absolute seconds; a malformed word dropped).
- **O2** Where word timestamps are refused, a segments-only call still returns the transcript.
- **O3** Non-English audio drops the English prompt and keeps only my words.
- **O4** A provider failure is wrapped and reported, never returned as text.
- **O5** What the transcriber wrote that nobody said (a stretch it calls silence, a video's "Thanks for watching") is not in my transcript, its segments or its words; my own "Thank you." stays (N276).

*Why:* 2026-07-29: an English prompt on Polish audio gave an empty transcript. A fake client records the request; no network, no prompt text asserted verbatim (the prompt registry owns it).

### As a speaker, I want my transcript built into one paragraph per slide, so that my Ideal Text starts from what I said. (Critical path: process -> Ideal Text; Never-5: only my latest Take.)

`tests/test_transcript_document_build.py` · Story

- **O1** The document is built from my LATEST spoken Take only; an earlier Take or a read-aloud never supplies its words.
- **O2** One paragraph per slide, in spoken order, and the coach's slide fix moves a piece to the slide the coach chose.
- **O3** A document over the length cap is cut, keeping only whole pieces whose offsets still point at their own words.
- **O4** No words, no document.

### As a speaker, I want my recordings served only through short-lived signed links, so that a leaked link does not expose my voice forever.

`tests/test_user_content_not_public.py` · Story

- **O1** Each of the four stored snippet row shapes resolves to a signed link against its own bucket, never a URL under a public base; stored public URLs are re-signed on read.
- **O2** When R2 cannot sign, a stored key falls through to a Supabase signed link, never a bare key or a public URL.

*Why:* DPIA RISK-11 (2026-09-17): every read path handed out permanent public bucket URLs. The four row shapes do not share a code path.

### As a speaker, I want my suggestion answers and Confident Voice practice (start, attempt, complete) to work and refuse bad input, so that practice never loses my recording.

`tests/test_user_sessions_route_contracts.py` · Story

- **O1** A suggestion answer is saved (200 {"saved": true}); a bad target is 400 and someone else's Take is 404 with nothing written.
- **O2** Starting practice on a moment creates it for me (201); an existing one is resumed (200) without a second row, on the other sentence I asked for while it has no try; a bad or premature answer is refused (400, 409).
- **O3** An attempt is stored and recorded (201) with no automatic outcome; a closed practice is 409 and a passage mismatch is 422 with nothing stored.
- **O4** Keeping an attempt completes the practice and dismissing closes it (200); keeping an attempt that is not the strongest is 400 with nothing kept.
- **O5** The list of my Takes the app reads on every open names each Take's topic and state, and a Take's deck (its slide text and timeline) never comes with it.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-12: route logic moved into services; these hold the routes' status and refusal contracts across that move. Decorators are peeled with inspect.unwrap.

### As a speaker, I want my trainings list to show which talks have an Ideal Text ready, so that I know where I can record next.

`tests/test_user_trainings.py` · Story

- **O1** GET /v2/user/trainings groups Takes by arc, one card per talk, Takes in take order.
- **O2** A talk is ideal_ready once the coach has corrected every slide; a talk with a slide still uncorrected is not.
- **O3** A coach's training import is never in the coach's own project list (it has no project to archive, so it blocked "One project at a time").
- **O4** Each Take carries the upload key it was sent with, so a Take kept on the phone knows whether it arrived (N556 item 3).

*Why:* Founder 2026-07-13 (arc-grouped trainings). The save-at-publish half (POST /v2/internal/publish-session-results) is retired and answers 410 (routes/v2/canonical_publish.py), so it has no test here.

### As a speaker, I want my answer to Confident Voice feedback recorded as a valid decision on real production-shaped data, so that the feedback loop works outside tests.

`tests/test_v3_end_to_end_production_shape.py` · Story

- **O1** On production-shaped data (a transcript and a DIFFERENT, shorter Ideal Text, snapshot-bound), the chain runs end to end and serves a Confident Voice row that quotes the Ideal Text and is backed by the spoken words.
- **O2** The speaker's answer to that row becomes a canonical decision carrying the row's own candidate, membership and exposure ids.
- **O3** A candidate-set write that returns nothing usable (or another set's id) is logged, not silent; the feedback is still served, but without lineage, so no answer can attach to it.

### As a speaker not on any allowlist, I want V3 feedback to reach me, so that every speaker gets the same feedback. (Critical path: feedback.)

`tests/test_v3_reaches_every_speaker_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** The V3 source gate lets in a speaker who is on no allowlist.
- **O2** The founder can switch the allowlist back on without a deploy, and then the same speaker is refused.
- **O3** A speaker with an open account-wide deletion is refused (privacy).

*Why:* Migration 0415 (migrations/v3_reaches_every_speaker.sql; founder 2026-10-03, N29 answer 1, "Every speaker"). Runs on the rehearsal tier (lane "confident-moment released"). Every write happens inside a transaction that is rolled back.

### As a coach, I want each pick sheet answered with one of its own clips, and the machine's picks behind a door only the server opens, so that my blind answers are clean.

`tests/test_v4_asks_coaches_two_blind_questions_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** An answer among the sheet's clips is kept; an answer outside them is refused by the table itself.
- **O2** NEVER-2: a coach's own login (anon, authenticated) cannot read the sheets, where the machine's picks are stored; only service_role can read and write them.

*Why:* 0453, V4 B1.8/B1.9, build plan D-ML-13 (BLIND COACH).

### As a coach, I want the pick and "surer" sheets to show me only clips and words, so that I answer without the machine's guess.

`tests/test_v4_coach_sheets.py` · Story

- **O1** The pick sheet shows only the clip, a letter, the words and the audio timing, never the machine's ids, sureness or choice.
- **O2** The "surer" sheet shows only the two wordings, never the slice, level, bar or the quality being varied.
- **O3** A Take with two feedback versions still fills the sheets, each moment asked once, and a moment the database refuses is skipped, not an error.

`tests/test_v4_pick_logging.py` · Story

### As a speaker, I want the silent spoken-word cue log never to reach me or a dataset, so that untested detectors do not affect my feedback.

`tests/test_verbal_cue_shadow_postgres.py` · Story

- **O1** A known cue's verdict is logged once per (clip, cue, version); a retry writes nothing new.
- **O2** A logged verdict can never be changed, and no verdict can be marked as serving a user.
- **O3** A cue the library does not measure is never logged.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-28, D2 + D3 (migration 0386, the shadow stage).

### As a speaker, I want feedback on later Takes to point at the right words and never show internal numbers, so that a slide I did not speak cannot confuse it.

`tests/test_verbal_lanes_take_document_n48_1.py` · Story

- **O1** A verbal row on words of this Take is mapped into the Take's own transcript (its served span shifted past the unspoken Slide).
- **O2** A verbal row on a Slide this Take did not speak, or with no served text to prove it, is excluded as ``document_span_unmapped``.
- **O3** NEVER-1: the rows that leave the server carry the feedback's words and no rank, evidence, score, version or other number (AC-9).
- **O4** A served clearer version names the moment it corrects (its block), so the walk draws it on that moment and not as a moment of its own (founder 2026-10-20: "the rewrite doesn't appear at all"), even when it was cut from the whole text under the Take's first clip: it names the clip its words were said in (founder 2026-11-06, N536: the walk showed "the closest one" in its place).
- **O5** When the walk's Clearer version is lost on the way all the same, the voice fix kept behind it is walked in its place, with its AI clip; with none, no moment is the fix (founder 2026-11-06, N547).

*Why:* N48.1 (Wave 1, E1), founder-approved 2026-10-05: from Take 2 on the served Ideal Text and the Take's transcript are two documents.

### As a speaker, I want a moment in my Voice Album only when the machine, I and a coach all agree, and shown without scores, so that the album is trustworthy and number-free.

`tests/test_voice_album.py` · Story

- **O1** A moment enters only with all three yeses (machine reads it confident, the speaker said yes, a coach said yes); missing any one keeps it out.
- **O2** A refresh run again adds nothing: the moment is in the album once.
- **O3** NEVER-1: the album read shows the moment's words and place, and no confidence, tags, metrics or score.

### As a speaker, I want a moment added to my Voice Album only when all three yeses align and removed only on a coach's explicit No, so that my album is never changed by accident.

`tests/test_voice_album_clip_reconcile.py` · Story

- **O1** My yes, the machine's confident read and a professional coach Yes on a Take of this project add the clip, on its Take and slide.
- **O2** A coach's explicit No removes the clip (no publish gate since the arc-level delivery was retired).
- **O3** Any other read writes nothing: a missing yes, a weak or unreadable machine read, a Take of another project, a database failure.

*Why:* L3 / contract 65: incomplete reads must never withdraw a saved moment.

`tests/test_voice_album_history.py` · Story

- **O1** The history tells, in order, my answer, my coach's agreement (as "Your coach"), the exercise with my attempts, and my note.
- **O2** A lane with no record stays quiet: it is simply absent, never a placeholder or a guess.
- **O3** NEVER-1: no lane carries a score, ratio, confidence value or classifier output, and the machine's read is never an event.
- **O4** NEVER-2: the story behind any moment outside the Album never tells the coach lane, so its silence cannot reveal a coach's verdict.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-18 ("the whole history so I can see from where it came from") and 2026-09-25 ("not any moments"); AC-9.

### As a speaker, I want a "practise new" queue of my starred clips I have not answered, shown without scores, so that I practise the right moments.

`tests/test_voice_album_practice_queue.py` · Story

- **O1** Every starred clip I have not answered is in the queue, in Take and Slide order, with its playback; a clip without a star is not.
- **O2** A clip I answered leaves the queue, whatever the answer and wherever I gave it (in a Take review or in this queue).
- **O3** NEVER-1: the queue carries no score and no machine read.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-18, the Album's "Practice new"; AC-9.

### As the founder, I want each moment's confidence read to stay on the server, stay neutral when a clip is middling and stay silent when it cannot read, so that the moments we praise are right.

`tests/test_voice_confidence.py` · Story

- **O1** Every measurable piece is stamped with the current read: a score in [-1, 1], an internal band and the version.
- **O2** A middling clip reads exactly 0.0 (neutral), never forced to a pole.
- **O3** No baseline, or too few measurable cues, stamps nothing: silence, never an invented 0.0.
- **O4** NEVER-1: the stamped read never reaches what a speaker is served (here the "practise new" queue built from the same pieces).

### As the founder, I want to say or type a note into a private notebook in the Lounge, so that Will learns from what I think between Takes and nobody else reads it.

`tests/test_voice_notebook.py` · Story

- **O1** Someone without the notebook types "#..." and the chat goes on as before, and the app is told the notebook is off (no mic).
- **O2** The first note asks the signed question; Yes keeps the waiting note and answers "Noted."; Not now keeps nothing.
- **O3** A voice note is transcribed under the caller's authorization, the hesitations taken out and every other word kept, the recording kept, and the thread shows "# " + the words, then "Noted.".
- **O4** A note that sounds like a crisis is kept and answered at once with the chat's safety reply; before the question is answered it is never asked instead of that reply.
- **O5** Will reads the notes, only once the person said Yes.
- **O6** Every typed "#" is one note, as the small mic's: the Life Panel's old tags are notes too, and "#note" is read as "#" (N253).
- **O7** While a note is said, its words so far come back, hesitations and all, and nothing is kept (N194); streamed, the key to stream is given only to someone who said Yes (N396).
- **O8** A note the app sends twice under one key (the app closed mid-send) is kept and answered once (N194).
- **O9** The person fixes a voice note's words: the note and its row in the thread change, Will does not answer again, and only their own voice note can be fixed (N194).
- **O10** Every fifth note that names someone gets the short line "🔒 Security disclaimer" under its "Noted."; the others, and notes with no name, get nothing more; nothing is changed or deleted (N411 item 3).
- **O11** Before any note, once three Takes have their summaries, opening the Lounge brings "We spotted a strength!", the strength in one sentence and the notebook's ask, once; after Not now, again only after three more Takes; after Yes, never; a sentence with a number, or none, writes nothing until the next Take (N411 items 1, 5).
- **O12** A Yes given on the dump screen writes "Yes" and "Noted." and asks nothing; a Yes in the chat still asks "What's on your mind today?" (N514).
- **O13** Opening the app asks the notebook's state (the dump screen opens on its answer, N522): the ring and the Yes are read side by side, and my time zone is written only when it changed, not on every open.

*Why:* Will learning plan, step 1 (founder 2026-10-20, "approved, start step 1"; words signed the same day).

### As a speaker who just said a better try of a sentence that doubted me, I want Will to show me my own words that prove otherwise, so that the better sentence feels true, and I want to be able to say "Not here".

`tests/test_walk_quote.py` · Story

- **O1** A sentence that doubted me gets one quote: the signed line and my own kept words, word for word with their date; the same sentence gets the same quote again, and any other sentence in that Take gets none (N268 items 1, 2, 4).
- **O2** When Will finds nothing that fits, or names something I don't keep, there is no quote: never invented.
- **O3** "Not here" keeps the notebook out of every later walk until the Life Panel's switch turns it back on (N268 item 5).
- **O4** Without the ring row or the notebook there is no quote and no switch; a Take that is not mine is refused (N268 item 3).

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-29, the Claude Code chat (N268).

### As a speaker, I want Will's words in my walk written for my Take, plain and kind, so that they talk about what I said and how it sounded, never about me being broken, and the walk never waits on them.

`tests/test_walk_voice.py` · Story

- **O1** Words that may reach me are plain: no number, no word that says I am broken or names a measurement, no coaching idiom, at most two sentences, 220 characters and one question, and a quote is only ever my own words; anything else is refused (N557, N565, Never 1).
- **O2** Will writes only for a speaker the writer is on for, on their own Take, at a step the walk has; otherwise the walk says its signed line.
- **O5** My private note is never repeated back to me: a draft that quotes it, or carries a run of its words without quote marks, is refused; my own spoken words may still be quoted (N565 item 2, SPEAKER-MEMORY.md).
- **O4** After a try, Will hears how it went (it landed, one try left, or the last) and is told what to offer for each; introducing the moment to try, what it will be walked with (a clearer way to say it, or the AI clip). A try the walk does not name gets no written reply.
- **O3** A draft that fails the check gets another try, told what was refused and why, up to three in all; then, or after a failed call, the walk says its signed line.

*Why:* Founder 2026-11-09 (N565: "make the answers smart. So not locked in, but smart... we can set the rules for those messages"; N557: "It assumes that you're broken and we need to fix you"; the founder's ratings: "to complex", "drawing in... this sounds wierd"). The style itself is graded by the golden set, tests/evals/golden/walk_voice.jsonl.

### As a speaker, I want Will's written walk message to be made only under my Take's processing authorization, so that no word of my Take reaches the AI provider without a permit we can record and delete.

`tests/test_walk_voice_authority.py` · Story

- **O1** When the authorization mode enforces, the GPT-4o call that writes Will's message runs inside my Take's protected provider scope, bound to my principal and my Take; with no principal for the Take, no call is made and the walk says its signed line.

### As a newcomer in Artur's chat, while my first Take is processed, I want Will to check what he heard and ask what I'd like to do here, so that my plan and my next step are mine before the walk ends.

`tests/test_way_in.py` · Story

- W1  Once Take 1 is sent: "While your feedback is being prepared, check what I heard.", then "What would you like to do here first?" with "Something else" first, "Learn from others", "Share what I know", "Prepare a talk", several then "Send" (FT-05, WID-04; N453, N573). W2  Once the Take's words are stored: "Here's how others will see you:" and the card from slide 1 (the role, About, topics as "Ask about {topic}", the name from sign-up); "That's right" keeps it, and only then may others see it; "Change it" brings "Tell me what to change." (FT-06; N453 item 2). W3  "Prepare a talk" alone asks "When is it?" with "No date yet", once; its date is the plan's; nobody is asked "When is your deadline?" (FT-10, PLAN-16; N564 item 1). W4  "Share what I know" brings the questions for people who join into the wait, then the agreement "Do you accept it?" with "I agree", and one "You're set." with "Copy my link ›" and "Your questions for people who join ›" (FT-11, FT-12, FT-13; N572). W5  Everyone gets the plan card and one "You're set."; nobody outside a team or sharing is asked the agreement (FT-12, FT-13). W6  After "You're set.": "Your feedback is almost ready. It will appear at the top of this chat." while it is prepared; "Your feedback is ready." with "Open your feedback ›" once it is; after the walk one bubble by the choice, the talk first, then sharing; "Record Take 2" stays faded until the walk is done (N592; WAYIN-N592-1..4). W7  "Learn from others" leads to "Next, ask someone who knows. Tap + to start." (FT-14, N592 item 2). W8  A speaking coach's link meets none of the choice's beats; the way in is in the speaking chat only (N572, N590; WAYIN-N590-1). W9  A first Take on which nothing was heard still gets its plan and "You're set." once its walk is done; no card is checked (N459 item 1).

*Why:* N453, N564, N572, N573, N590, N592; the rules are frontend docs/flows/the-plan.md §2.

### As a newcomer waiting for my first Take, I want Will's chat to keep answering while the app checks every few seconds what is due, so that the way in never stops with "too many requests" in the wait.

`tests/test_way_in_limit.py` · Story

- L1  The way in's poll (no answer, no audio) is not counted against the model's limit: more polls than that limit allows in a minute all pass, under the poll's own, higher limit. L2  An answer, which may call the model, is still counted against it.

*Why:* The app polls POST /v2/plan/way-in every 5 s while Take 1 waits (N572, N592); under the model's 400 per hour it got 429s after about half an hour (coordinator's end-to-end review, 9 Nov 2026).

### As a speaker, I want my recording made seekable without changing its timing, and kept as-is if that fails, so that playback works everywhere and my Take is never lost. (Critical path: record.)

`tests/test_webm_remux.py` · Story

- **O1** The remuxed file carries a seek index and exactly the same packets and timestamps as the recording (even one not starting at zero), so no stored offset moves.
- **O2** Any remux failure (ffmpeg error or timeout) keeps the original bytes and never raises, so the Take still goes on (Never-3: the loop is never broken).
- **O3** The bytes stored are the remuxed bytes, and the read-after-write checksum is taken on what is actually stored.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-08: Safari could not seek a clip inside its Take (MediaRecorder WebM has no Cues and no Duration).

### As a speaker, I want a Take stuck in processing to be recovered, so that I do not stare at "Working on your take" forever.

`tests/test_wedged_job_wall_clock.py` · Story

- **O1** A job running past its time limit, even with a fresh heartbeat, is flagged as stuck, released and re-queued once.

*Why:* Founder 2026-09-17: "It got stale and now it needs to function."

### As a speaker, I want to save my WhatsApp number and get one message when my coach first shares feedback, so that I know feedback arrived without spam.

`tests/test_whatsapp_number.py` · Story

- **O1** A valid number is saved in international form; a number without a country code is refused and nothing is written.
- **O2** I can read my number back and remove it.
- **O3** A coach's first share, told at once when there is no queue to wait on, sends one WhatsApp message (with the brand image when the template has one); editing a shared note or having WhatsApp switched off sends no WhatsApp. (Waiting for the coach to be done: tests/test_coach_feedback_notice.py.)
- **O4** When WhatsApp refuses a message, the log names its reason.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-12, "Add Phone Input for WhatsApp Notifications".

### As someone who gets our WhatsApp messages, I want to reply STOP and have them stop, and have any other reply reach a person, so that I am never messaged against my wish and an answer is never lost.

`tests/test_whatsapp_stop.py` · Story

- **O1** Meta's check when the webhook is set is answered only with the right verify token; with either key unset the door is shut (503).
- **O2** A delivery Meta did not sign changes nothing (401).
- **O3** STOP turns the messages off for the account at that number and the booking that carries it, and answers with the signed words; other accounts and other replies are left as they were.
- **O4** Once forwarding is on, any other reply reaches the founder by email with the number, the account's admin page and the reply's words, or what kind of message it was; while it is off nothing is emailed.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-28, N237; 2026-10-29, N265.

### Before Will answers in the chat, he looks up the speaker's own history (their Takes' transcripts, what their coach sent them, what they told him before) and reads the few pieces that best match the question as a private block (founder 2026-10-20, step 1).

`tests/test_will_looks_up_history.py` · Story

- **O1** only this speaker's rows can reach Will, never another speaker's, never Will's own replies, never an answer to "Did this help?"
- **O2** a coach's unsent word or draft never reaches Will; what they sent does
- **O3** the piece that matches the question best comes first
- **O4** a failing read gives no block and no error: Will answers as today
- **O5** a signed-in speaker's chat hands the block to Will; a visitor's does not
- **O6** a Take whose processing authorization is gone (withdrawn, expired) never reaches Will, nor what the coach sent on it; a failed check lets no Take through

*Why:* Will's answers should build on what this speaker already said and heard, without anything private to someone else, or to the coach, leaking in.

### As a speaker, after a Take I want Will to ask me a short question about the moment the walk just worked on, only when something is off or missing, that I answer with a tap or in my own words, so that the next feedback knows my talk without a form in front of my next Take.

`tests/test_will_question.py` · Story

- **O1** Will asks only when something is off or missing, the first that holds: a breather when the practice missed or the voice dipped two Takes in a row; "On purpose?" when the clearer line I practised last Take did not come back; who the talk is for; what I want them to do; from Take 3, with all three known, one deeper question. Nothing missing, no question; each of these once per talk, a breather never twice in a row.
- **O2** The tap answers come from what Will heard, the recording first: a Take that names its audience twice is checked ("Right?"); named once, those words come first, then what I said for an earlier talk, then the signed answers for my field, and no "Someone else": my own words go into "Something else" in the bar (N406 item 2); what I want them to do the same way. From the AI's read only words my Take says, word for word, short and without a number, are kept.
- **O3** A tap is kept for Will and fills the talk's setup, so the next Take carries who it is for; Will says it back, "{who}. I'll keep them in mind." (N294 item 5), any other answer gets "Got it, thanks 🙏"; then the message after the Take. "Someone else" under the check opens one follow-up, "Who is it for, then? 👀", with no tap answers, and the message waits; my typed words under it are the answer, or Skip. Skip lets the message go; a repeat tap writes nothing.
- **O4** Typed words under the question are the answer: what I want them to do lands as the talk's call to action, my own note on the talk kept. Distress is never taken as an answer: the chat's safety reply comes.
- **O5** After a Take that needs it, the summary's batch holds the message under Will's question, a bridge line between the summary and the question ("Ok, now let's dive into the content for a bit.", founder 2026-10-21, N94), and the summary hears the line I practised last Take when this Take said it.
- **O8** The question opens on my own words of the last moment the walk showed (N294 item 1): "When you said “…”, " where it reads on, else "We just worked on “…”. "; "On purpose?" quotes its own line and nothing more; with no moment the question is as signed.
- **O9** One at a time (N294 item 4): when both who the talk is for and what I want them to do are missing, what I want them to do follows my answer to who, from what Will heard first; a Skip ends them, and a "#" note under the question is no answer: the next question does not follow it.
- **O10** The key line is asked last, by voice (N294 item 2): once who the talk is for and what I want them to do are known and my key line is missing, Will asks the plan's signed question with who in it, opened on the moment, no tap answers; it follows my answer to what I want them to do, naming who I said; my answer is kept as the talk's key line (sure) where the plan and the picker read it, and for Will.
- **O12** Will says who it is for from my side (N406 items 1, 3 and 4): "my team" back as "Your team. I'll keep them in mind."; "Myself" as "I'll keep you in mind.", then what I want to do with no "them" and no taps, and the key line keeps "your audience". After a typed answer the goodbye waits under his next question until I answer it, even when the Lounge asks to let it go.
- **O11** An answer by voice answers Will's open question (N294 item 3, N319 item 1): the words heard are my voice bubble, Will thanks me, the chain goes on; nothing heard, or no question open, writes nothing.
- **O6** The founder's words are what Will asks from the next Take: a changed question and renamed answers (each answer keeps its follow-up by its place), a filled empty slot asked after the signed deeper questions; a number, a missing "{the board}" or a different number of answers is refused.
- **O7** Will says a question with a light touch only when it still means the signed one and keeps my own words in it once, one line, no number, still a question; otherwise he says the signed words. The tap answers never change.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-20 (N82): "ask me a lot of questions … a natural flow in the walk and in the lounge"; the bank signed the same day. 2026-10-31 (N294): "these questions, they should be related to the moment"; "they can be only one at a time. Only once you answer".

### As a speaker, I want the coach sheets about my Takes deleted when I turn training off, so that my withdrawal is real.

`tests/test_withdrawal_erases_sheets.py` · Story

- **O1** Turning training off deletes my Takes' rows from all three coach sheet tables, and only mine.
- **O2** If my Takes cannot be read, the switch still turns off, and the queued erasure job finishes the deletion later.

*Why:* Privacy 3.5 section 4a (3.5 pack, file 22 E4).

### As a speaker who withdraws, I want the next model trained without my data, so that my withdrawal reaches the model. (Critical path: consent withdrawn -> the thing stops.)

`tests/test_withdrawal_reaches_the_model.py` · Story

- **O1** A promotion reads the withdrawal live: a passing report promotes while everyone's yes stands, and the same report is refused as STALE the moment a speaker withdraws (no weekly sweep in between); nothing is served.
- **O2** A freshness that cannot be read refuses the promotion; nothing served.
- **O3** A candidate that reproduces a withdrawn speaker's text is failed for good and never promoted, whatever report is offered.
- **O4** The retraining is the NEXT run, built only from still-releasable pairs no run trained on: the withdrawn speaker's pairs stay out.

*Why:* Counsel 2026-10-01; audit DOOR-4-WITHDRAWN and DOOR-3-RETRAIN. Python seam (services/model_training.py, services/model_promotion.py) over the shared fake in tests/doors_fakes.py.

### As a speaker with a goal, I want my why, my vision and how I want to treat people kept in my own words, and my why waiting for me once each day, so that I remember what I am doing this for.

`tests/test_your_why.py` · Story

- **O1** After my first life goal, the next open of the Lounge asks once for my why with Not now | Start; a second Not now and it waits for me to ask ("#why" or my own words) (N255 Q3).
- **O2** Will drafts each line only from my answers and quotes them; a skipped question gets no line; all three skipped, nothing is drafted (Q2).
- **O3** Kept, the Lounge says "Noted. Your why is set." and each day, the first time it opens, my why waits in my words with "Where will it show today?", once that day (Q4).
- **O4** A changed line keeps the earlier words, dated; Will reads all three before it chats (Q5, Q6).
- **O5** Without the ring row, or while onboarded, nothing of it appears.

*Why:* Founder 2026-10-29 ("what about the reminder daily reminder about my goals? Like raison d'être"), N255; words signed N258.

## Part 2 · Evals: what the AI's words are graded on

Each set grades one AI surface: the production prompt and its checks run on every case,
the rules first (no digits, length, banned words), then a model grades the meaning.

### best_presentation

`tests/evals/golden/best_presentation.jsonl` · 2 cases

> SEED dataset (engineering, 2026-08-03) — L1 fence: verbatim-select + light polish.
> The founder's golden cases replace/extend these. Format: docs/prompt_evals.md

| Case | What it checks | Graded on |
|---|---|---|
| BP-SEED-01 | L1: lines stay mostly verbatim, no invented claims | Each slide's text is substantially the speaker's own spoken line with at most a few connective words changed; no new facts, numbers, or claims appear. |
| BP-SEED-02 | an already-clean line passes through unchanged | The returned text for the slide is the speaker's sentence essentially unchanged (identical or near-identical wording). |

### clearer_version

`tests/evals/golden/clearer_version.jsonl` · 2 cases

> SEED dataset (engineering, 2026-09-30) — proves the harness end-to-end for the coach's clearer-version draft.
> The founder's golden cases replace/extend these. Format: docs/prompt_evals.md

| Case | What it checks | Graded on |
|---|---|---|
| CLV-SEED-01 | hedges and restarts go, meaning and facts stay, nothing added | Returns only the rewritten passage: the new onboarding flow reduced support tickets by about a third, said plainly in the speaker's voice, with the hedges, fillers and restart removed and no fact, number or claim added. |
| CLV-SEED-02 | a clear passage stays nearly as it is; the rewrite does not pad | Returns only a passage of about the same length or shorter that keeps both facts (Tuesday ships, Monday noon cutoff, a week's wait) and adds no new sentence, preamble or commentary. |

### coach_comment_draft

`tests/evals/golden/coach_comment_draft.jsonl` · 2 cases

> SEED dataset (engineering, 2026-08-03) — feedback-generation surface.
> The founder's golden cases replace/extend these. Format: docs/prompt_evals.md

| Case | What it checks | Graded on |
|---|---|---|
| CCD-SEED-01 | plain-language note: no metric jargon, one strength one improvement | A warm note that references the speaker's goal, names one thing working and one concrete thing to improve, using plain delivery language (for these metrics: fast-ish pace, flat pitch, rushed pausing) with no technical terms or raw numbers about the voice. |
| CCD-SEED-02 | Polish transcript gets a Polish note | The entire note is written in Polish and follows the coach-note shape (encouragement first, one improvement). |

### emphasis_phrase

`tests/evals/golden/emphasis_phrase.jsonl` · 3 cases

> SEED dataset (engineering, 2026-08-15) — the ACCENT TARGET.
> Founder: "try not to style the whole paragraphs or chunks but key few
> words or a sentence". These cases pin the two ways that goes wrong: an
> invented phrase, and a pick so wide it accents everything. Format:
> docs/prompt_evals.md

| Case | What it checks | Graded on |
|---|---|---|
| EP-SEED-01 | picks a narrow verbatim phrase out of a three-sentence moment | A few words that carry the moment — the result or the turn, not the run-up, and never the whole passage. |
| EP-SEED-02 | verbatim in the SPEAKER's language — never translated to English | The accent target is copied from the Polish passage, character-for-character. |
| EP-SEED-03 | a flat, even passage: returning nothing is a correct answer | Either an empty pick (nothing stands out above the rest) or a short verbatim phrase — never a widened one to seem helpful. |

### exercise_script

`tests/evals/golden/exercise_script.jsonl` · 1 cases

> SEED dataset (engineering, 2026-09-30) — proves the harness end-to-end for the moment-based exercise draft.
> The founder's golden cases replace/extend these. Format: docs/prompt_evals.md

| Case | What it checks | Graded on |
|---|---|---|
| EXS-SEED-01 | a short second-person exercise for the spotted pattern on this exact passage, no scores | Under about 120 words, second person, names the restart pattern in plain words, gives one concrete thing to do on the next attempt of this passage (for example say the whole phrase once, pause, then continue) and makes no promise or judgement about the speaker. |

`tests/evals/golden/moment_suggestion.jsonl` · 3 cases

| Case | What it checks | Graded on |

| MS-SEED-02 | replace: same point, audience-appropriate, nothing added | The replacement restates the same admission in plainer, more composed words appropriate for customers — without adding any facts or claims the speaker did not make, and the why-line explains the swap in one sentence. |
| MS-N326-01 | replace with the verbal read's line (N326): the answer first, his words, the line never quoted | The replacement leads with the answer (a second shift makes ninety possible by March), mostly in the speaker's own words, adds no fact, name or number the speaker did not say, and does not quote the focus line. |

### will_question

`tests/evals/golden/will_question.jsonl` · 12 cases

> SEED dataset (engineering, 2026-10-20, N82) — Will's heard call: who the talk is for and what it asks, word for word or nothing.
> A strange read in production becomes a new row here. Format: docs/prompt_evals.md
> The light touch (founder 2026-10-20, N86): a row with a key and a question runs ``vary``; the output is {"question", "varied"}. A variation that fails read_varied falls back to the signed words, so "question" is always a question Will may ask.
> Will's lead-in (founder 2026-10-20, N89): a row with an ask and its signed words runs ``lead_in`` over the Take's facts; the output is {"message", "led"}. A lead-in that fails read_lead_in falls back to the ask as written, so "message" always still makes the ask.

| Case | What it checks | Graded on |
|---|---|---|
| WQ-SEED-01 | the audience said twice is read word for word | expect |
| WQ-SEED-02 | we and you are not an audience; nothing is guessed | expect |
| WQ-SEED-03 | an ask with a number is dropped, never rewritten | expect |
| WQ-SEED-04 | the ask is the speaker's own words | ask is a short phrase copied from the transcript asking to approve the pilot; audience is empty or a phrase the transcript says. |
| WQ-VARY-01 | the audience question, said with a light touch, still asks who the talk is for | a short friendly question asking who the speaker was talking to in their talk; the same meaning as 'Who were you talking to?', no advice, no new facts. |
| WQ-VARY-02 | the goal question keeps its two halves: do, or feel | asks what the speaker wants the audience to do or feel when the talk ends; the same meaning as 'When you finish, what do you want them to do? Or feel?' |
| WQ-VARY-03 | the speaker's own words stay in place, once | a check that the talk is for {heard}, asking the speaker to confirm; the placeholder {heard} appears exactly once. |
| WQ-VARY-04 | the practised line stays in place, once | says the line {words} practised last time did not come back in this Take and asks whether that was on purpose; {words} appears exactly once. |
| WQ-VARY-05 | the breather question still offers both: a breather, or something missing | acknowledges the speaker's effort and asks whether they need a breather or something feels missing; the same meaning as the signed question. |
| WQ-LEAD-01 | the home-screen ask, led in from the Take, still asks to add the app and keeps the facts' words | one or two warm sentences that connect to the speaker's talk or this Take without grading them, then asks whether to add the app to the home screen; no advice, no numbers |
| WQ-LEAD-02 | the WhatsApp ask keeps the number in it and invents nothing when the facts are thin | a friendly line that fits a first Take without claiming progress, then asks for their number for a WhatsApp when the coach replies |
| WQ-LEAD-03 | "Did this help?" led in as a reflective question stays a question about whether it helped | a short reflective lead-in about the practice, never a verdict on the speaker, then asks whether this helped |
